📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 结果 人乳头瘤病毒 16 E6 和 E7 癌蛋白降解 p53 和视网膜母细胞瘤蛋白,调节磷酸肌醇 3 激酶、蛋白激酶 B 和雷帕霉素信号传导的机制靶标,并导致基因组不稳定。 [10.1017/s0022215126106124]。
  • 吡唑啉弹 EN218 被鉴定为带有氯乙酰胺半胱氨酸反应弹头。进一步的评估导致了 (S)-WX48A 的开发,它与 E6 上的 C58 发生立体选择性反应,抑制 E6 介导的 p53 泛素化。 [10.1002/cbic.70590]。
  • 总共纳入了 25 项报告定性或混合方法的研究。综合结果从女性的角度产生了三个主题:女性对 HPV 自我采样的看法、女性对卫生专业人员 HPV 采样的经验以及女性对社区 HPV 采样的看法。女性对自我取样的接受程度很高,因为她们有对自己身体的控制感、手术过程的舒适感和节省时间的好处。
  • ≥20/10 HPF 时 TBA 增加与整个 队列研究 ( P = 0.002) 和 HPV 独立/p53 异常 vSCC ( P = 0.003) 较短的无复发生存期 (RFS) 相关。在调整肿瘤亚型、LVI、T 和 N 类别后,TBA 还与较短的疾病特异性生存期和总生存期相关(分别为 P = 0.048 和 P = 0.009)。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Results Human papillomavirus 16 E6 and E7 oncoproteins degrade p53 and retinoblastoma protein, dysregulate phosphoinositide 3 kinase, protein kinase B and mechanistic target of rapamycin signalling and drive genomic instability.
  • A pyrazoline hit, EN218, was identified with a chloroacetamide cysteine-reactive warhead.
  • A total of 25 studies reporting qualitative or mixed methods were included.
  • Increased TBA at ≥20/10 HPF was associated with shorter recurrence-free survival (RFS) in the entire cohort ( P =0.002) and HPV-independent/p53-abnormal vSCCs ( P =0.003). [10.1097/pas.0000000000002644].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: PLOS One  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-02  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pone.0361925  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 21, 11, e0361925

埃塞俄比亚主要利益相关者实施 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的推动因素和障碍:定性研究

Enablers and barriers to human papillomavirus vaccination implementation among key stakeholders in Ethiopia: A qualitative study
作者： Kibir Temesgen Assefa, Abebaw Gebeyehu Worku, Achenef Asmamaw Muche, Bisrat Misganaw Geremew, Mulat Adefris Woldetsadik, Kassahun Alemu, Kibir Temesgen
摘要中文翻译简介:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 是针对 宫颈癌 的有效且挽救生命的干预措施。然而,在包括埃塞俄比亚在内的资源匮乏地区,实现高、公平和可持续的吸收仍然是一项重大挑战。因此,本研究旨在探讨 HPV 疫苗接种 在埃塞俄比亚实施的推动因素和障碍。方法:在有目的地选择的代表不同社会生态背景的环境(阿法尔、阿姆哈拉、亚的斯亚贝巴和德雷达瓦)中采用解释性描述性定性设计。与青春期女孩(n = 14)、符合条件的青春期女孩的父母(n = 14)和社区代表(n = 14)进行了六次焦点小组讨论。此外,还与医疗保健提供者、扩大免疫计划 (EPI) 协调员以及来自政府和非政府组织的技术顾问进行了 19 次关键知情人访谈 (KII)。使用以实施研究综合框架 (CFIR 2.0) 为指导的组合演绎-归纳内容分析来分析数据。结果:多种相互作用的因素影响了 HPV 疫苗接种 在埃塞俄比亚的实施,这些因素被归类为社区、卫生系统和政策层面的推动因素和障碍。推动因素包括感知的 疫苗 属性和交付适应性、支持性政策和多部门战略伙伴关系、社会文化和基于信仰的驱动因素以及卫生系统准备情况和劳动力激励。然而,一些障碍限制了实施,包括对针对 9-14 岁女孩的怀疑和与同意相关的行政障碍、普遍存在的错误信息和认知阻力、卫生系统资源限制和协调差距、捐助者依赖风险和财务可持续性问题、以及结构性准入限制和公平差距。结论:虽然政策转变、外部战略伙伴关系和一线工作人员的奉献精神加速了 疫苗 的覆盖范围,但埃塞俄比亚 HPV 疫苗接种 计划的长期可持续性仍然受到多层次障碍的限制。要实现 2030 年全球 宫颈癌 消除目标,需要建立信任的风险沟通策略来反驳生育神话、简化同意程序、有针对性的国内资源调动以及针对服务不足的牧民和城市亚人群量身定制的具体实施模式。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨在埃塞俄比亚实施 HPV 疫苗接种 的推动因素和障碍,应对在资源匮乏环境中实现高、公平和可持续吸收的挑战。该研究通过关注不同背景下影响 HPV 疫苗接种 的社会生态因素来填补知识空白。
设计与对象
采用解释性描述性定性设计,涉及埃塞俄比亚四个地区的 6 次焦点小组讨论和 19 次与不同利益相关者(包括少女、家长、社区代表、医疗保健提供者和 EPI 协调员)的关键知情人访谈。
核心方法
通过焦点小组讨论和关键知情人访谈收集数据,并使用实施研究综合框架 (CFIR 2.0) 指导下的演绎-归纳相结合的内容分析进行分析。
主要结果
该研究确定了 HPV 疫苗接种 实施的多个推动因素和障碍,包括支持性政策、社区驱动因素和卫生系统准备情况,以及对同意、错误信息、资源限制和公平差距的担忧。
解释与创新
作者通过强调社区、卫生系统和政策层面因素的复杂相互作用来解释研究结果,强调需要制定量身定制的战略来解决这些障碍并确保长期可持续性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为制定有针对性的干预措施提供信息,通过解决社区观念、卫生系统限制和政策差距,提高 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率和公平性,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的定性性质和对自我报告数据的依赖限制了普遍性。样本量和环境的多样性提供了丰富的理解,但可能无法完全代表埃塞俄比亚的所有情况。

Enablers and barriers to human papillomavirus vaccination implementation among key stakeholders in Ethiopia: A qualitative study

Authors: Kibir Temesgen Assefa, Abebaw Gebeyehu Worku, Achenef Asmamaw Muche, Bisrat Misganaw Geremew, Mulat Adefris Woldetsadik, Kassahun Alemu, Kibir Temesgen
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is an effective and lifesaving intervention against cervical cancer. However, achieving high, equitable, and sustainable uptake remains a significant challenge in low-resource settings, including Ethiopia. Therefore, this study aimed to explore the enablers and barriers to HPV vaccination implementation in Ethiopia. METHODS: An interpretive descriptive qualitative design was employed in purposively selected settings representing diverse socio-ecological contexts (Afar, Amhara, Addis Ababa, and Dire Dawa). Six focus group discussions were conducted with adolescent girls (n = 14), parents of eligible adolescent girls (n = 14), and community representatives (n = 14). In addition, nineteen key informant interviews (KIIs) were conducted with healthcare providers, Expanded Programme on Immunization (EPI) coordinators, and technical advisors from governmental and non-governmental organizations. Data were analysed using a combined deductive-inductive content analysis guided by the Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research (CFIR 2.0). RESULTS: Multiple interacting factors influenced HPV vaccination implementation in Ethiopia, categorised as enablers and barriers across community, health system, and policy levels. Enablers included the Perceived Vaccine Attributes and Delivery Adaptability, Supportive Policy and Multisectoral Strategic Partnerships, Socio-cultural and Belief-based Drivers, and Health System Readiness and Workforce Motivation. However, several barriers limited implementation, including Suspicions About Targeting 9-14-year-old Girls and Consent-related Administrative Obstacles, Pervasive Misinformation and Cognitive Resistance, Health System Resource Constraints and Coordination Gaps, Donor Dependency Risks and Financial Sustainability Concerns, and Structural Access Constraints and Equity Gaps. CONCLUSIONS: While policy shifts, external strategic partnerships, and frontline worker dedication have accelerated vaccine reach, the long-term sustainability of Ethiopia's HPV vaccination program remains constrained by multi-level barriers. Progress toward the global 2030 cervical cancer elimination targets requires trust-building risk-communication strategies to counter fertility myths, simplified consent procedures, targeted domestic resource mobilization, and context-specific delivery models tailored to underserved pastoralist and urban sub-populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the enablers and barriers to HPV vaccination implementation in Ethiopia, addressing the challenge of achieving high, equitable, and sustainable uptake in low-resource settings. The research fills a knowledge gap by focusing on the socio-ecological factors influencing HPV vaccination in diverse contexts.
Design and population
An interpretive descriptive qualitative design was used, involving six focus group discussions and 19 key informant interviews with diverse stakeholders, including adolescent girls, parents, community representatives, healthcare providers, and EPI coordinators, across four regions of Ethiopia.
Core methods
Data were collected through focus group discussions and key informant interviews and analysed using a combined deductive-inductive content analysis guided by the Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research (CFIR 2.0).
Main results
The study identified multiple enablers and barriers to HPV vaccination implementation, including supportive policies, community drivers, and health system readiness, as well as concerns about consent, misinformation, resource constraints, and equity gaps.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings by highlighting the complex interplay of factors at community, health system, and policy levels, emphasizing the need for tailored strategies to address these barriers and ensure long-term sustainability.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted interventions to improve HPV vaccination coverage and equity, particularly in low-resource settings, by addressing community perceptions, health system constraints, and policy gaps.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's qualitative nature and reliance on self-reported data limit generalizability. The sample size and diversity of settings provide a rich understanding but may not fully represent all contexts in Ethiopia.

肿瘤出芽活动是 人乳头瘤病毒 独立 p53 异常外阴鳞状细胞癌复发的独立预后因素

Tumor Budding Activity is an Independent Prognostic Factor of Recurrence in Human Papillomavirus-Independent p53-Abnormal Squamous Cell Carcinoma of the Vulva
作者： Krisztina Lengyel, Thomas Denize, Kaitlyn James, Kyle M. Devins, Varvara Mazina, Lawrence Hsu Lin, Esther Oliva, Gulisa Turashvili
摘要中文翻译外阴鳞状细胞癌(vSCC)的预后由人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)和p53状态决定。由于肿瘤芽现象(TBA)和肿瘤-间质比(TSR)在其他癌种中具有预后意义,我们评估了它们在vSCCs中的重要性。收集了1990年至2022年诊断的肿瘤病例,并评估了临床病理数据、TBA和TSR。该研究纳入了172例肿瘤:其中73例(42.5%)与HPV相关,74例(43%)为HPV独立/p53异常,25例(14.5%)为HPV独立/p53野生型。患者的中位年龄为69.5岁(22至98岁)。大多数肿瘤分期为pT1b(132例,76.7%)。淋巴管血管浸润(LVI)在25例(14.5%)中存在,且在有淋巴结评估的病例中,淋巴结转移率为24.6%(33/134)。中位TBA分别为2(0-45)/1高倍镜视野(HPF)、4(0-198)/5 HPF和5(0-304)/10 HPF。中位TSR为60%(10%至90%)。70例(40.7%)患者出现复发(中位复发时间:14个月,2-141个月)。在全部肿瘤中,TBA≥20/10 HPF与复发无病生存期(RFS)缩短相关(P=0.002),在HPV独立/p53异常的vSCCs中也存在类似关联(P=0.003)。在调整了LVI以及pTNM分期中的T和N分类后,该关联在HPV独立/p53异常的vSCCs中依然存在(P=0.012),并在全部肿瘤中调整了肿瘤亚型和其他协变量后依然显著(P=0.01)。在调整了肿瘤亚型、LVI以及T和N分类后,TBA还与疾病特异性生存期(P=0.048)和总生存期(P=0.009)缩短相关。本研究提示,高TBA与vSCC的复发无病生存期缩短相关,尤其是在HPV独立/p53异常亚组中,同时在调整了肿瘤亚型、LVI、T和N分类后,也与疾病特异性生存期和总生存期缩短相关。这些发现需要进一步验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估外阴鳞状细胞癌 (vSCC) 中肿瘤出芽活动 (TBA) 和肿瘤间质比 (TSR) 的重要性,因为 vSCC 的结果是由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 和 p53 状态驱动的。
设计与对象
该研究纳入了 1990 年至 2022 年诊断的 172 个 vSCC 肿瘤,分为 HPV 相关亚组、HPV 独立/p53 异常亚组和 HPV 独立/p53 野生型亚组。队列研究 的患者中位年龄为 69.5 岁,大多数肿瘤分期为 pT1b。
核心方法
对已识别肿瘤的临床病理学数据、TBA 和 TSR 进行评估。根据 HPV 状态和 p53 状态对肿瘤进行分类,并计算中位 TBA 和 TSR。
主要结果
≥20/10 HPF 时 TBA 增加与整个 队列研究 ( P = 0.002) 和 HPV 独立/p53 异常 vSCC ( P = 0.003) 较短的无复发生存期 (RFS) 相关。在调整肿瘤亚型、LVI、T 和 N 类别后,TBA 还与较短的疾病特异性生存期和总生存期相关(分别为 P = 0.048 和 P = 0.009)。
解释与创新
该研究表明,高 TBA 与 vSCC 较短的无复发生存期相关,特别是在 HPV 独立/p53 异常亚组中,并且在调整肿瘤亚型、LVI、T 和 N 类别后,与较短的疾病特异性生存期和总生存期相关。这些发现值得进一步验证。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,TBA 可能是预测 vSCC 复发和生存的有用生物标志物,特别是在 HPV 独立/p53 异常亚组中,并且可以为未来的研究和临床实践提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和缺乏随机性而受到限制。样本量适中,研究结果需要在更大规模的前瞻性研究中进一步验证。

Tumor Budding Activity is an Independent Prognostic Factor of Recurrence in Human Papillomavirus-Independent p53-Abnormal Squamous Cell Carcinoma of the Vulva

Authors: Krisztina Lengyel, Thomas Denize, Kaitlyn James, Kyle M. Devins, Varvara Mazina, Lawrence Hsu Lin, Esther Oliva, Gulisa Turashvili
Original abstract (English)
Outcomes of vulvar squamous cell carcinoma (vSCC) are driven by human papillomavirus (HPV) and p53 status. As tumor budding activity (TBA) and tumor-stroma ratio (TSR) are prognostic in other carcinomas, we evaluated their significance in vSCCs. Tumors diagnosed from 1990 to 2022 were identified, and clinicopathologic data, TBA, and TSR were assessed. The cohort included 172 tumors: 73 (42.5%) HPV-associated, 74 (43%) HPV-independent/p53-abnormal, and 25 (14.5%) HPV-independent/p53-wild-type. Median patient age was 69.5 (22 to 98) years. Most tumors were staged as pT1b (132, 76.7%). Lymphovascular invasion (LVI) was present in 25 (14.5%), and lymph node metastases in 24.6% (33/134) of cases with nodal evaluation. The median TBA was 2 (0-45)/1 high-power field (HPF), 4 (0-198)/5 HPF, and 5 (0-304)/10 HPF. The median TSR was 60% (10% to 90%). Seventy (40.7%) patients experienced recurrences (median: 14 mo, 2-141). Increased TBA at ≥20/10 HPF was associated with shorter recurrence-free survival (RFS) in the entire cohort ( P =0.002) and HPV-independent/p53-abnormal vSCCs ( P =0.003). This association persisted in the HPV-independent/p53-abnormal vSCCs after adjusting for LVI, and T and N categories of pTNM stage ( P =0.012), and in the entire cohort after adjusting for tumor subtype and other covariates ( P =0.01). TBA was also associated with shorter disease-specific ( P =0.048) and overall ( P =0.009) survival after adjusting for tumor subtype, LVI, and T and N categories. Our study suggests that high TBA is associated with shorter recurrence-free survival in vSCC, particularly the HPV-independent/p53-abnormal subgroup, as well as with shorter disease-specific and overall survival after adjusting for tumor subtype, LVI, T and N categories. The findings warrant further validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the significance of tumor budding activity (TBA) and tumor-stroma ratio (TSR) in vulvar squamous cell carcinoma (vSCC), given that outcomes of vSCC are driven by human papillomavirus (HPV) and p53 status.
Design and population
The study included 172 vSCC tumors diagnosed from 1990 to 2022, categorized into HPV-associated, HPV-independent/p53-abnormal, and HPV-independent/p53-wild-type subgroups. The cohort had a median patient age of 69.5 years, with most tumors staged as pT1b.
Core methods
Clinicopathologic data, TBA, and TSR were assessed in the identified tumors. Tumors were categorized based on HPV status and p53 status, and the median TBA and TSR were calculated.
Main results
Increased TBA at ≥20/10 HPF was associated with shorter recurrence-free survival (RFS) in the entire cohort ( P =0.002) and HPV-independent/p53-abnormal vSCCs ( P =0.003). TBA was also associated with shorter disease-specific and overall survival after adjusting for tumor subtype, LVI, and T and N categories ( P =0.048 and P =0.009, respectively).
Interpretation and novelty
The study suggests that high TBA is associated with shorter recurrence-free survival in vSCC, particularly in the HPV-independent/p53-abnormal subgroup, and with shorter disease-specific and overall survival after adjusting for tumor subtype, LVI, T and N categories. These findings warrant further validation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that TBA could be a useful biomarker for predicting recurrence and survival in vSCC, particularly in the HPV-independent/p53-abnormal subgroup, and could inform future studies and clinical practice.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the lack of randomization. The sample size is moderate, and the findings need further validation in larger, prospective studies.

癌症政策实验室模型:加强乌干达循证 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 政策的卫生系统

The Cancer Policy Lab Model: Strengthening Health Systems for Evidence-Informed Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Policy in Uganda
作者： Annet Nakaganda, Gertrude Tumuhairwe, Aggrey Tumwiine, Pauline Nalweyiso, Bpharm, Alfred Jatho, Peninah Nattimba, Hillary Agnes Ataro
摘要中文翻译目的 建立和实施癌症政策实验室 (CPL) 模型,作为一个结构化平台,以加强国家卫生系统将全球证据转化为乌干达可操作的癌症控制政策和实践的能力。方法 乌干达 CPL(CPL-U)由 ANANDA 癌症研究中心(ACCR)召集,汇集了卫生系统不同层面的利益相关者,包括政策制定者、临床医生、研究人员、民间社会组织、患者代表和实施合作伙伴。该实验室采用了一种面向系统的方法,结合了证据合成、多部门对话和跨四个领域的结构化小组工作:政策和治理;融资和可持续性;公平和准入;以及实施和卫生系统。该实验室遵循四个步骤:(1) 定义关于 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 优先级的国家政策问题;(2) 综合全球和当地证据,包括 疫苗 有效性、成本效率和交付策略;(3) 促进结构化的多部门审议,以评估乌干达卫生系统内的可行性;(4) 共同提出政策建议并为决策者起草政策简报。结果 实验室提出了优化乌干达 HPV 疫苗接种 的可行建议,并制作了第一份关于 HPV 疫苗接种 国家传播和政策参与优先事项的 CPL 政策简报。建议包括维持单剂 HPV 疫苗接种 策略、优先考虑 9-14 岁的女孩,并在 20 岁以内追赶 疫苗接种,以及将定期政策审查制度化,以与新出现的证据保持一致。融资建议强调通过专门的初级卫生保健预算项目、增加政府共同融资以及利用国家成本效益分析来指导投资决策来加强国内资源调动。公平建议的重点是有针对性地惠及服务不足的人群,包括失学女童和弱势群体,同时加强沟通策略,以提高 疫苗 的接受度和需求。实施建议包括整合跨服务平台的 HPV 疫苗接种 交付、加强卫生工作者培训、改善供应链系统、加强多部门协调以及增强负责项目规模扩大的数据系统。结论 结构化、便利的多部门参与增强了建议的所有权、可行性和政策相关性。CPL 展示了一种实用且可扩展的模型,可将全球证据与当地政策和实施联系起来,同时加强卫生系统。该模型具有在不同环境下的癌症控制优先事项和其他卫生政策挑战中复制的巨大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在建立和实施癌症政策实验室(CPL)模型,作为一个结构化平台,以加强乌干达国家卫生系统将全球证据转化为可行的癌症控制政策和实践的能力。这一背景凸显了需要有证据支持的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 政策来解决 宫颈癌 和相关疾病。
设计与对象
该研究由 ANANDA 癌症研究中心 (ACCR) 召集的乌干达癌症政策实验室 (CPL-U) 参与,该实验室汇集了卫生系统不同层面的利益相关者,包括政策制定者、临床医生、研究人员、民间社会组织、患者代表和实施合作伙伴。该实验室专注于 HPV 疫苗接种 优先级及其在乌干达卫生系统内的实施。
核心方法
该实验室采用了一种面向系统的方法,结合了证据合成、多部门对话和跨四个领域的结构化小组工作:政策和治理;融资和可持续性;公平和准入;以及实施和卫生系统。它遵循四个步骤:确定有关 HPV 疫苗接种 优先事项的国家政策问题、综合全球和地方证据、促进结构化的多部门审议以及共同制定政策建议。
主要结果
该实验室提出了优化乌干达 HPV 疫苗接种 的可行建议,并制作了第一份关于 HPV 疫苗接种 优先顺序的 CPL 政策简报,以供国家传播和政策参与。融资建议强调通过专门的初级卫生保健预算项目、增加政府共同融资以及利用国家成本效益分析来指导投资决策来加强国内资源调动。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明了结构化、便利的多部门参与在增强建议的所有权、可行性和政策相关性方面的有效性。该模型的新颖之处在于,它在加强卫生系统的同时将全球证据与当地政策和实施联系起来。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为乌干达 HPV 疫苗接种 项目的 监测、融资和实施策略提供信息,特别是在优化 疫苗 优先顺序、改善公平性和可及性以及加强卫生系统以实现可持续政策采用方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖利益相关者的意见以及缺乏有关建议影响的定量数据而受到限制。由于分析的定性性质和缺乏直接的结果测量,证据的强度中等。

The Cancer Policy Lab Model: Strengthening Health Systems for Evidence-Informed Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Policy in Uganda

Authors: Annet Nakaganda, Gertrude Tumuhairwe, Aggrey Tumwiine, Pauline Nalweyiso, Bpharm, Alfred Jatho, Peninah Nattimba, Hillary Agnes Ataro
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE To establish and implement the Cancer Policy Lab (CPL) model as a structured platform to strengthen national health system capacity for translating global evidence into actionable cancer control policy and practice in Uganda. METHODS The CPL Uganda (CPL-U), convened by the ANANDA Centre for Cancer Research (ACCR), brought together stakeholders across different levels of the health system, including policymakers, clinicians, researchers, civil society organizations, patient representatives, and implementing partners. The Lab applied a systems-oriented approach combining evidence synthesis, multi-sector dialogue, and structured group work across four domains: policy and governance; financing and sustainability; equity and access; and implementation and health systems. The Lab followed a four-step process: (1) defining a national policy question on human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination prioritization; (2) synthesizing global and local evidence, including vaccine effectiveness, cost-efficiency, and delivery strategies; (3) facilitating structured multi-sector deliberations to assess feasibility within the Ugandan health system; and (4) co-producing policy recommendations and drafting a policy brief for decision-makers. RESULTS The Lab generated actionable recommendations to optimize HPV vaccination in Uganda and produced the first CPL policy brief on HPV vaccination prioritization for national dissemination and policy engagement. Recommendations included maintaining the single-dose HPV vaccination strategy, prioritizing girls aged 9-14 years with catch-up vaccination up to 20 years, and institutionalizing regular policy review to align with emerging evidence. Financing recommendations emphasized strengthening domestic resource mobilization through dedicated Primary Health Care budget lines, increased government co-financing, and use of national cost-benefit analysis to guide investment decisions. Equity recommendations focused on targeted outreach to underserved populations, including out-of-school girls and vulnerable groups, alongside strengthened communication strategies to improve vaccine acceptance and demand. Implementation recommendations included integrating HPV vaccination delivery across service platforms, strengthening health worker training, improving supply chain systems, reinforcing multi-sector coordination, and enhancing data systems for accountable programme scale-up. CONCLUSION Structured, facilitated multi-sector engagement enhanced ownership, feasibility, and policy relevance of recommendations. The CPL demonstrates a practical and scalable model for linking global evidence to local policy and implementation while strengthening health systems. The model has strong potential for replication across cancer control priorities and other health policy challenges in diverse settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to establish and implement the Cancer Policy Lab (CPL) model as a structured platform to strengthen national health system capacity for translating global evidence into actionable cancer control policy and practice in Uganda. The background highlights the need for evidence-informed human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination policy to address cervical cancer and related diseases.
Design and population
The study involved the Cancer Policy Lab Uganda (CPL-U), convened by the ANANDA Centre for Cancer Research (ACCR), which brought together stakeholders across different levels of the health system, including policymakers, clinicians, researchers, civil society organizations, patient representatives, and implementing partners. The Lab focused on HPV vaccination prioritization and its implementation within the Ugandan health system.
Core methods
The Lab applied a systems-oriented approach combining evidence synthesis, multi-sector dialogue, and structured group work across four domains: policy and governance; financing and sustainability; equity and access; and implementation and health systems. It followed a four-step process: defining a national policy question on HPV vaccination prioritization, synthesizing global and local evidence, facilitating structured multi-sector deliberations, and co-producing policy recommendations.
Main results
The Lab generated actionable recommendations to optimize HPV vaccination in Uganda and produced the first CPL policy brief on HPV vaccination prioritization for national dissemination and policy engagement. Financing recommendations emphasized strengthening domestic resource mobilization through dedicated Primary Health Care budget lines, increased government co-financing, and use of national cost-benefit analysis to guide investment decisions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of structured, facilitated multi-sector engagement in enhancing ownership, feasibility, and policy relevance of recommendations. The model is novel in its approach to linking global evidence to local policy and implementation while strengthening health systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, financing, and implementation strategies for HPV vaccination programs in Uganda, particularly in optimizing vaccine prioritization, improving equity and access, and strengthening health systems for sustainable policy adoption.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on stakeholder input and the absence of quantitative data on the impact of the recommendations. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the qualitative nature of the analysis and the lack of direct outcome measurements.

人工智能聊天机器人在 HPV 感染患者教育方面的准确性

Artificial Intelligence Chatbot Accuracy for Patient Education in HPV Infection
作者： Isabela H. Schettini, Li Yun Lim, Christopher D. Dwyer, Thomas L. Carroll
摘要中文翻译目标:聊天机器人是人工智能 (AI) 工具,使用大型语言模型来回答问题并模拟类人对话。患者通常使用聊天机器人进行有关医疗问题的自我教育。使用聊天机器人进行 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染和复发性呼吸道乳头状瘤病 (RRP) 患者自我教育搜索所获得的信息的准确性尚不清楚。这项工作旨在分析聊天机器人对有关喉部 HPV 感染和 RRP 的常见患者问题的回答。方法:作者提出了八个问题,以模拟现实世界中患者对三种不同聊天机器人模型(Claude、ChatGPT 和 Gemini)的询问。两名接受过专科培训的喉科医生使用 5 点李克特量表对每个模型的八个答案进行盲目评分,评估内容包括准确性、全面性以及与评分医生自己提供的答案的相似性。进行了描述性和统计分析。结果:Gemini 的总体平均分最高(4.7,SD 0.5),Claude 最低(4,SD 0.7)。双子座在每个评估方面的个人得分最高。在所有平台分析的方面之间,准确性得分最高(4.4,SD 0.6),但差异并不显着。单向方差分析测试显示三个平台之间存在总体显着差异(P < 0.0001)。使用事后 Tukey 测试进行平台之间的配对比较,只有当 Gemini 与 ChatGPT 和 Claude 单独比较时,结果才显着(P < 0.0001)。结论:双子座在答案准确性、全面性以及与医生评分者向患者提供的答案的相似性方面具有最高的总体和个人评分。与其他聊天机器人平台相比,它是唯一一个在回答有关喉部 HPV 感染/RRP 的常见患者问题方面表现出显着差异的聊天机器人平台。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估聊天机器人对有关喉部 HPV 感染和复发性呼吸道乳头状瘤病 (RRP) 的常见患者问题的反应准确性。使用聊天机器人进行 HPV 感染和 RRP 患者自我教育搜索所获得的信息的准确性尚不清楚。
设计与对象
这项工作使用三种不同的聊天机器人模型(Claude、ChatGPT 和 Gemini)分析了聊天机器人对有关喉部 HPV 感染和 RRP 的八个问题的回答。作者进行这项研究是为了模仿现实世界中患者的询问。
核心方法
作者提出了八个问题,以模拟现实世界中患者对三种不同聊天机器人模型(Claude、ChatGPT 和 Gemini)的询问。两名接受过专科培训的喉科医生使用 5 点李克特量表对每个模型的八个答案进行盲目评分,评估内容包括准确性、全面性以及与评分医生自己提供的答案的相似性。进行了描述性和统计分析。
主要结果
双子座的总体平均得分最高(4.7,SD 0.5),克劳德的得分最低(4,SD 0.7)。双子座在每个评估方面的个人得分最高。单向方差分析测试显示三个平台之间存在总体显着差异(P < 0.0001)。
解释与创新
双子座在答案准确性、全面性以及与医生评分者向患者提供的答案的相似性方面具有最高的总体和个人评分。与其他聊天机器人平台相比,它是唯一一个在回答有关喉部 HPV 感染/RRP 的常见患者问题方面表现出显着差异的聊天机器人平台。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为用于 HPV 感染和 RRP 患者教育的聊天机器人平台的开发和评估提供信息,强调选择准确和全面的人工智能工具进行医疗交流的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于一小部分八个问题和三个聊天机器人模型,这可能无法完全代表患者询问的范围或人工智能平台的多样性。由于研究的受控性质和缺乏更广泛的普遍性,证据强度中等。

Artificial Intelligence Chatbot Accuracy for Patient Education in HPV Infection

Authors: Isabela H. Schettini, Li Yun Lim, Christopher D. Dwyer, Thomas L. Carroll
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: Chatbots are artificial intelligence (AI) tools that use large language models to answer questions and simulate human-like conversations. Chatbots are commonly used by patients for self-education regarding medical issues. The accuracy of information obtained through patient self-education searches using Chatbots for Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection and recurrent respiratory papillomatosis (RRP) is unknown. This work is intended to analyze aspects of chatbot answers to common patient questions regarding laryngeal HPV infection and RRP. METHODS: Eight questions were asked by the authors to mimic real-world patient queries to three different Chatbot models (Claude, ChatGPT, and Gemini). Two fellowship-trained laryngologists blindly rated eight answers for each model using a 5-point Likert scale regarding accuracy, comprehensiveness, and similarity to a response the rating physician would themselves offer. Descriptive and statistical analyses were performed. RESULTS: Gemini had the highest overall mean score (4.7, SD 0.5), and Claude had the lowest (4, SD 0.7). Gemini had the highest individual scores for each aspect evaluated. Between the aspects analyzed across all platforms, accuracy was scored highest (4.4, SD 0.6), but the difference was not robust. The one-way ANOVA test showed an overall significant difference between the three platforms (P < 0.0001). A paired comparison between platforms was performed using a post hoc Tukey test, and the results were only significant when Gemini was compared individually with ChatGPT and Claude (P < 0.0001). CONCLUSION: Gemini had the highest overall and individual rated scores for answer accuracy, comprehensiveness, and similarity to what physician raters would offer their patients. It was the only chatbot platform that demonstrated a robust difference when compared to the others regarding answers to common patient questions on laryngeal HPV infection/RRP.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the accuracy of chatbot responses to common patient questions about laryngeal HPV infection and recurrent respiratory papillomatosis (RRP). The accuracy of information obtained through patient self-education searches using chatbots for HPV infection and RRP is unknown.
Design and population
This work analyzed chatbot answers to eight questions about laryngeal HPV infection and RRP using three different chatbot models (Claude, ChatGPT, and Gemini). The study was conducted by the authors to mimic real-world patient queries.
Core methods
Eight questions were asked by the authors to mimic real-world patient queries to three different Chatbot models (Claude, ChatGPT, and Gemini). Two fellowship-trained laryngologists blindly rated eight answers for each model using a 5-point Likert scale regarding accuracy, comprehensiveness, and similarity to a response the rating physician would themselves offer. Descriptive and statistical analyses were performed.
Main results
Gemini had the highest overall mean score (4.7, SD 0.5), and Claude had the lowest (4, SD 0.7). Gemini had the highest individual scores for each aspect evaluated. The one-way ANOVA test showed an overall significant difference between the three platforms (P < 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
Gemini had the highest overall and individual rated scores for answer accuracy, comprehensiveness, and similarity to what physician raters would offer their patients. It was the only chatbot platform that demonstrated a robust difference when compared to the others regarding answers to common patient questions on laryngeal HPV infection/RRP.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development and evaluation of chatbot platforms for patient education on HPV infection and RRP, highlighting the importance of selecting accurate and comprehensive AI tools for medical communication.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a small set of eight questions and three chatbot models, which may not fully represent the range of patient inquiries or the diversity of AI platforms. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the controlled nature of the study and the lack of broader generalizability.

在生产性 HPV16 感染期间,E8^E2 控制 E2 阳性复制中心的形成,该复制中心先于 E4 表达

E8^E2 controls the formation of E2-positive replication centers that precede E4 expression during productive HPV16 infection
作者： Franziska Kuehner, Fiona Hirn, Meret Beyer, Elke Straub, Tina Melanie Rehm, Janis Renner, Nora Roos, Thomas Iftner, Andreas Wieland, Frank Stubenrauch
摘要中文翻译未标记:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 将其复制周期与角质形成细胞分化结合起来。病毒基因组在未分化细胞中维持低拷贝数,并在分化的上基底细胞中的核复制灶内进行扩增。病毒蛋白E2和E8^E2分别充当病毒复制的激活剂和抑制剂。使用新型多克隆抗血清和可识别 E2 和 E8^E2 的单克隆抗体 22E2-B9,我们在含有附加型 HPV16 野生型 (wt) 或 E8^E2 突变体 基因组 的角质形成细胞中鉴定出不同的核 E2 灶。这些病灶与病毒 基因组 和细胞蛋白 RPA32、RAD51、γH2AX 和 BRD4 共定位,与病毒复制中心一致。细胞周期分析表明,E2 灶在 S 期形成并持续进入 G2 期。E8^E2 表达或抑制活性的丧失增加了复制灶的数量并诱导弥漫性核 E2 染色。只有一部分 E2 灶阳性细胞表达晚期病毒蛋白 E4,表明复制中心在强大的晚期基因表达之前出现。在器官型培养物中,在E2焦点阳性/E4阳性细胞之前,在基底上层中检测到E2焦点阳性/E4阴性细胞,而E2​​阴性/E4阳性细胞表明在生产性感染期间E2的动态调节。此外,许多wt细胞表现出类似于E8^E2突变细胞的E2焦点表型,表明E8^E2活性在角质形成细胞分化过程中下降。这些发现表明,E2阳性病毒复制中心受到E8^E2的严格调控,在生产性HPV16感染期间早期出现,并在病毒生命周期的生产阶段伴随着E2和E8^E2的动态调节。重要性:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 将其复制周期与角质形成细胞分化联系起来,但控制生产性病毒 基因组 扩增的机制仍不完全清楚。在这里,我们在 HPV16 感染的角质形成细胞中鉴定出不同的 E2 阳性核复制中心,并表明它们含有病毒 基因组 和参与 DNA 复制和修复的细胞因子。我们证明这些结构在 S 期形成,持续到 G2 期,并在晚期病毒 E4 蛋白的稳健表达之前出现,表明病毒 基因组 扩增在生产周期中比之前预想的更早开始。我们进一步表明,病毒阻遏蛋白 E8^E2 调节 E2 阳性复制中心的组织,并且 E8^E2 活性在角质形成细胞分化过程中似乎下降。总之,我们的研究结果揭示了高效 HPV16 感染期间 E2 和 E8^E2 的动态调节,并为病毒 基因组 扩增和晚期基因表达在上皮细胞分化过程中如何协调提供了新的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 将其复制周期与角质细胞分化结合起来,但控制生产性病毒 基因组 扩增的机制仍不完全清楚。
设计与对象
该研究使用新型多克隆抗血清和单克隆抗体 22E2-B9 研究了 HPV16 感染的角质形成细胞,以识别不同的核 E2 病灶。它还检查了器官型培养物,以观察生产周期中 E2 和 E4 表达的动态。
核心方法
研究人员使用新型多克隆抗血清和单克隆抗体22E2-B9来检测E2和E8^E2蛋白。他们分析了细胞周期阶段并观察了病毒 基因组 和细胞因子在 HPV16 感染的角质形成细胞中的定位。
主要结果
细胞周期分析表明,E2 灶在 S 期形成并持续进入 G2 期。E8^E2 表达或抑制活性的丧失增加了复制灶的数量并诱导弥漫性核 E2 染色。在器官型培养物中,在基底上层中先检测到 E2 灶阳性/E4 阴性细胞,然后再检测到 E2 灶阳性/E4 阳性细胞。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,E2 阳性病毒复制中心受到 E8^E2 的严格调控,在生产性 HPV16 感染期间早期出现,并在病毒生命周期的生产阶段伴随着 E2 和 E8^E2 的动态调节。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为病毒 基因组 扩增和晚期基因表达在上皮细胞分化过程中如何协调提供了新的见解,这可能为未来有关 HPV 复制及其对疾病进展的影响的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点关注 HPV16 感染的角质形成细胞和器官型培养物,可能无法完全代表体内条件。证据是中等的,因为它基于免疫荧光和细胞周期分析,没有直接的功能验证。

E8^E2 controls the formation of E2-positive replication centers that precede E4 expression during productive HPV16 infection

Authors: Franziska Kuehner, Fiona Hirn, Meret Beyer, Elke Straub, Tina Melanie Rehm, Janis Renner, Nora Roos, Thomas Iftner, Andreas Wieland, Frank Stubenrauch
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) couple their replication cycle to keratinocyte differentiation. Viral genomes are maintained at low copy numbers in undifferentiated cells and undergo amplification within nuclear replication foci in differentiated suprabasal cells. The viral proteins E2 and E8^E2 function as activators and repressors of viral replication, respectively. Using a novel polyclonal antiserum together with the monoclonal antibody 22E2-B9, which recognize both E2 and E8^E2, we identified distinct nuclear E2 foci in keratinocytes harboring episomal HPV16 wild-type (wt) or E8^E2 mutant genomes. These foci colocalized with viral genomes and the cellular proteins RPA32, RAD51, γH2AX, and BRD4, consistent with viral replication centers. Cell-cycle analysis showed that E2 foci formed during the S phase and persisted into the G2 phase. Loss of E8^E2 expression or repression activity increased the number of replication foci and induced diffuse nuclear E2 staining. Only a subset of E2 focus-positive cells expressed the late viral protein E4, indicating that replication centers arise before robust late gene expression. In organotypic cultures, E2 focus-positive/E4-negative cells were detected in suprabasal layers before E2 focus-positive/E4-positive cells, whereas E2-negative/E4-positive cells suggested dynamic regulation of E2 during productive infection. Moreover, many wt cells displayed E2 focus phenotypes resembling E8^E2 mutant cells, suggesting that E8^E2 activity declines during keratinocyte differentiation. These findings demonstrate that E2-positive viral replication centers are tightly regulated by E8^E2, emerge early during productive HPV16 infection, and are accompanied by dynamic modulation of E2 and E8^E2 during the productive phase of the viral life cycle. IMPORTANCE: Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) couple their replication cycle to keratinocyte differentiation, but the mechanisms controlling productive viral genome amplification remain incompletely understood. Here, we identify distinct E2-positive nuclear replication centers in HPV16-infected keratinocytes and show that they contain viral genomes and cellular factors involved in DNA replication and repair. We demonstrate that these structures form during the S phase, persist into the G2 phase, and arise before robust expression of the late viral E4 protein, indicating that viral genome amplification is initiated earlier in the productive cycle than previously appreciated. We further show that the viral repressor E8^E2 regulates the organization of E2-positive replication centers and that E8^E2 activity appears to decline during keratinocyte differentiation. Together, our findings reveal dynamic regulation of E2 and E8^E2 during productive HPV16 infection and provide new insights into how viral genome amplification and late gene expression are coordinated in differentiating epithelial cells.

Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) couple their replication cycle to keratinocyte differentiation, but the mechanisms controlling productive viral genome amplification remain incompletely understood.
Design and population
The study investigated HPV16-infected keratinocytes using a novel polyclonal antiserum and monoclonal antibody 22E2-B9 to identify distinct nuclear E2 foci. It also examined organotypic cultures to observe the dynamics of E2 and E4 expression during the productive cycle.
Core methods
The researchers used a novel polyclonal antiserum and monoclonal antibody 22E2-B9 to detect E2 and E8^E2 proteins. They analyzed cell-cycle phases and observed the localization of viral genomes and cellular factors in HPV16-infected keratinocytes.
Main results
Cell-cycle analysis showed that E2 foci formed during the S phase and persisted into the G2 phase. Loss of E8^E2 expression or repression activity increased the number of replication foci and induced diffuse nuclear E2 staining. In organotypic cultures, E2 focus-positive/E4-negative cells were detected in suprabasal layers before E2 focus-positive/E4-positive cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that E2-positive viral replication centers are tightly regulated by E8^E2, emerge early during productive HPV16 infection, and are accompanied by dynamic modulation of E2 and E8^E2 during the productive phase of the viral life cycle.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide new insights into how viral genome amplification and late gene expression are coordinated in differentiating epithelial cells, which may inform future studies on HPV replication and its implications for disease progression.
Limitations and evidence strength

靶向 人乳头瘤病毒 E6 癌蛋白的共价配体

Covalent Ligands that Target the Human Papillomavirus E6 Oncoprotein
作者： Charlotte M. Zammit, Daniel K. Nomura
摘要中文翻译高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 早期蛋白 6 (E6) 病毒癌蛋白是 HPV 介导的癌症的关键参与者,也是宫颈癌、肛门癌和大部分头颈癌的主要驱动因素。HPV E6 招募细胞泛素 E3 连接酶 E6 相关蛋白 (E6AP),也称为 UBE3A,而该 E6/E6AP 复合物反过来又招募肿瘤抑制因子 p53,导致泛素介导的 p53 降解。富含半胱氨酸的 E6 蛋白是通过共价半胱氨酸反应化学进行化学干预的有吸引力的靶标。虽然之前的研究已经发现了 E6 的肽共价抑制剂,但我们试图确定是否可以识别非肽功能结合剂。我们筛选了半胱氨酸反应性共价配体库,并鉴定了吡唑啉命中物EN218,其带有氯乙酰胺半胱氨酸反应性弹头。对吡唑啉类似物的评估导致了一种改进分子 (S)-WX48A 的开发,该分子与 E6 上的 C58 发生立体选择性反应,抑制 E6 介导的 p53 泛素化。虽然该分子仍处于早期阶段,需要进一步的药物化学优化以发挥靶向细胞活性,但我们的研究表明,E6 可以直接立体选择性地靶向共价非肽小分子,并为开发更多用于 HPV 治疗的药物样 E6 抑制剂奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索针对 HPV E6 癌蛋白的化学干预措施,该蛋白对于 HPV 介导的癌症(包括宫颈癌、肛门癌和头颈癌)至关重要。该研究试图解决对非肽功能结合剂作为肽抑制剂替代品的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及筛选半胱氨酸反应性共价配体库,以确定 HPV E6 癌蛋白的潜在抑制剂。重点是 E6 蛋白在招募 E6AP 和降解 p53 方面的作用,这在 HPV 相关癌症中至关重要。
核心方法
筛选半胱氨酸反应性共价配体库以鉴定功能性结合物。该研究评估了吡唑啉类似物,以开发一种改进的分子,该分子可与 E6 上的 C58 发生立体选择性反应,从而抑制 E6 介导的 p53 泛素化。
主要结果
吡唑啉弹 EN218 被鉴定为带有氯乙酰胺半胱氨酸反应弹头。进一步的评估导致了 (S)-WX48A 的开发,它与 E6 上的 C58 发生立体选择性反应,抑制 E6 介导的 p53 泛素化。
解释与创新
该研究表明,E6 可以直接立体选择性地靶向共价非肽小分子,为开发更多用于 HPV 治疗的药物样 E6 抑制剂提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发针对 HPV E6 的新治疗药物奠定了基础,这可能会改善 治疗 治疗 HPV 相关癌症(如宫颈癌、肛门癌和口咽癌)的效果。
局限与证据强度
该分子仍处于早期阶段,需要进一步的药物化学优化以发挥靶向细胞活性,这表明由于结果的初步性质,证据的强度适中。

Covalent Ligands that Target the Human Papillomavirus E6 Oncoprotein

Authors: Charlotte M. Zammit, Daniel K. Nomura
Original abstract (English)
The high‐risk human papillomavirus (HPV) early protein 6 (E6) viral oncoprotein is a key player in HPV‐mediated cancers and is the major driver of cervical, anal, and a substantial portion of head and neck cancers. HPV E6 recruits the cellular ubiquitin E3‐ligase E6‐associated protein (E6AP), also termed UBE3A, and this E6/E6AP complex in turn recruits the tumor suppressor p53, resulting in the ubiquitin‐mediated degradation of p53. The cysteine‐rich E6 protein presents an attractive target for chemical intervention through covalent, cysteine‐reactive chemistries. While previous studies have uncovered peptidic covalent inhibitors of E6, we sought to determine whether we could identify nonpeptidic functional binders. We screened a library of cysteine‐reactive covalent ligands and identified a pyrazoline hit, EN218, bearing a chloroacetamide cysteine‐reactive warhead. Evaluation of pyrazoline analogs led to the development of an improved molecule, ( S )‐WX48A, that stereoselectively reacted with C58 on E6 to inhibit E6‐mediated p53 ubiquitination. While this molecule is still in an early stage and requires further medicinal chemistry optimization to exert on‐target cellular activity, our study demonstrates that E6 can be directly and stereoselectively targeted with a covalent nonpeptidic small‐molecule and provides a foundation for developing more drug‐like E6 inhibitors for HPV therapy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore chemical interventions targeting the HPV E6 oncoprotein, which is central to HPV-mediated cancers, including cervical, anal, and head and neck cancers. The research sought to address the need for nonpeptidic functional binders as an alternative to peptidic inhibitors.
Design and population
The study involved screening a library of cysteine-reactive covalent ligands to identify potential inhibitors of the HPV E6 oncoprotein. The focus was on the E6 protein's role in recruiting E6AP and degrading p53, which is critical in HPV-associated cancers.
Core methods
A library of cysteine-reactive covalent ligands was screened to identify functional binders. The study evaluated pyrazoline analogs to develop an improved molecule that stereoselectively reacts with C58 on E6 to inhibit E6-mediated p53 ubiquitination.
Main results
A pyrazoline hit, EN218, was identified with a chloroacetamide cysteine-reactive warhead. Further evaluation led to the development of (S)-WX48A, which stereoselectively reacted with C58 on E6 to inhibit E6-mediated p53 ubiquitination.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that E6 can be directly and stereoselectively targeted with a covalent nonpeptidic small-molecule, offering a novel approach for developing more drug-like E6 inhibitors for HPV therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a foundation for the development of new therapeutic agents targeting HPV E6, which could lead to improved treatments for HPV-associated cancers such as cervical, anal, and oropharyngeal cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The molecule is still in an early stage and requires further medicinal chemistry optimization to exert on-target cellular activity, indicating that the evidence is moderate in strength due to the preliminary nature of the results.

骨髓间充质干细胞通过激活miR-129诱导HPV相关宫颈癌上皮间质转化的分子机制研究。

Study on the molecular mechanism of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells inducing epithelial-mesenchymal transition in HPV-associated cervical cancer through activation of miR-129.
作者： Nieying Ma, Kaijie Xu, Zhize Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 miR-129 在 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 中的作用,解决 miR-129 与肿瘤进展之间尚不清楚的关系。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 77 个 宫颈癌 和 30 个正常宫颈组织样本,其中 BMSC 与 SiHa 和 HeLa 细胞共培养。
核心方法
通过 qRT-PCR 测量 miR-129 表达,并分析与临床病理特征和 OS 的关联。将BMSCs与SiHa和HeLa细胞共培养,检测细胞增殖、侵袭以及E-cadherin、N-cadherin和MRP1的表达。

与正常对照相比,宫颈癌 组织中 miR-129 显着上调。miR-129 高表达与肿瘤大小、FIGO 分期和淋巴结转移相关,并且与较短的 OS 相关。BMSC 共培养上调 miR-129,促进增殖、集落形成和侵袭,而 miR-129 敲低则抑制这些表型。
解释与创新

miR-129 显示出作为 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 的诊断和预后生物标志物的潜力,并且 BMSCs/miR-129 轴可能有助于 宫颈癌 的进展。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到体外模型的使用和进一步体内验证的需要的限制。

Study on the molecular mechanism of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells inducing epithelial-mesenchymal transition in HPV-associated cervical cancer through activation of miR-129.

Authors: Nieying Ma, Kaijie Xu, Zhize Wang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: HPV-associated cervical cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related death in women worldwide. The role of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells (BMSCs) and miR-129 in tumor progression remains unclear. OBJECTIVES: To investigate miR-129 expression and its clinical significance in HPV-associated cervical cancer and to explore whether BMSCs regulate malignant phenotypes through miR-129. METHODS: miR-129 expression was measured by qRT-PCR in 77 cervical cancer and 30 normal cervical tissues. Associations with clinicopathological features and overall survival (OS) were analyzed. Diagnostic value was assessed by ROC curve. Rat BMSCs were co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells. Following miR-129 overexpression or knockdown, cell proliferation (CCK-8, colony formation), invasion (Transwell) and expression of E-cadherin, N-cadherin and MRP1 (Western blot) were examined. RESULTS: miR-129 expression was significantly higher in cervical cancer tissues than in normal controls (3.24±0.56 vs. High miR-129 expression correlated with tumor size ≥3 cm (P=0.009), FIGO stage III-IV (P=0.0034) and lymph node metastasis (P=0.038). High expression was associated with shorter OS (median: 28.4 vs. 46.2 months; HR=2.944, 95%CI:1.484-5.842, P=0.002). The AUC was 0.788 (95%CI:0.693-0.884), with a cut-off of 1.85 (sensitivity:76.9%, specificity:70.4%). BMSC co-culture upregulated miR-129 expression (SiHa: 3.52±0.42 vs. miR-129 overexpression promoted proliferation, colony formation and invasion, while knockdown suppressed these phenotypes. Overexpression of miR-129 reduced E-cadherin (0.42±0.05 vs. 1.00±0.08, P<0.01) and increased N-cadherin (2.85±0.22 vs. 1.00±0.10, P<0.01) and MRP1 (2.46±0.18 vs. Knockdown of miR-129 partially reversed BMSC-induced changes in these proteins. CONCLUSION: miR-129 is significantly upregulated in HPV-associated cervical cancer and shows potential as a diagnostic and prognostic biomarker. BMSCs upregulate miR-129 in cervical cancer cells and miR-129 modulates proliferation, invasion, EMT marker expression and MRP1 expression. These findings suggest that the BMSCs/miR-129 axis may contribute to cervical cancer progression, but further in-vivo and mechanistic studies are needed.

The study aimed to investigate the role of miR-129 in HPV-associated cervical cancer, addressing the unclear relationship between miR-129 and tumor progression.
Design and population
The study involved 77 cervical cancer and 30 normal cervical tissue samples, with BMSCs co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells.
Core methods
miR-129 expression was measured by qRT-PCR, and associations with clinicopathological features and OS were analyzed. BMSCs were co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells, and cell proliferation, invasion, and expression of E-cadherin, N-cadherin, and MRP1 were examined.
Main results
miR-129 was significantly upregulated in cervical cancer tissues compared to normal controls. High miR-129 expression correlated with tumor size, FIGO stage, and lymph node metastasis, and was associated with shorter OS. 人乳头瘤病毒 L1 衣壳蛋白的组装途径以静电相互作用编码。

The assembly pathway for human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein is encoded in electrostatic interactions.
作者： X Yuan, K N Nand, J C Patel, A Jin, C Bystroff
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 L1 蛋白自发折叠并组装成 60 nm 病毒样颗粒 (VLP),在高盐浓度下具有 T = 7d 二十面体对称性,但在低盐浓度下则不然。在组装过程中从低盐到高盐的快速增加会产生质量较差的 VLP,而在长达 48 小时内缓慢增加盐会产生高质量的 VLP。对这种行为的深入了解是通过基于 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 VLP 低温电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 结构的计算模型得出的。使用线性化泊松-玻尔兹曼方程计算静电势能垒。在单体最初快速寡聚成对称五聚体(壳粒)后,这些单元以能量顺序形成成对相互作用,最低势垒首先。但在高盐浓度下,无论是在模拟还是在实验中,能量势垒或多或少都是相等的,并且粒子形式不规则。相互作用界面由 突变 修改,以支持更小的 30 nm T = 1 二十面体对称性,而不支持 T = 7d 对称性。结果是更小的颗粒。通过仔细控制离子强度和/或通过设计突变界面残基,可以直接从未纯化的细菌裂解物中制备高质量、高免疫原性的VLP,而不需要使用拆卸/重新组装步骤。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 人乳头瘤病毒 L1 衣壳蛋白的组装途径以及盐浓度如何影响具有特定二十面体对称性的病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 的形成。
设计与对象
该研究使用基于 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 VLP 低温电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 结构的计算模型来研究组装过程,重点关注 L1 蛋白在不同盐浓度下的行为。
核心方法
研究人员使用线性化泊松-玻尔兹曼方程计算静电势能垒,并通过 突变 修改相互作用界面以支持特定的对称性。
主要结果
结果表明,盐浓度的快速增加会导致VLP质量较差,而在48小时内缓慢增加盐浓度会产生高质量的VLP。发现 突变 有利于较小的 30 nm T = 1 二十面体对称性,而不利于 T = 7d 对称性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,控制离子强度和突变界面残基可以直接从未纯化的细菌裂解物中形成高质量、免疫原性的 VLP,而不需要拆卸/重新组装步骤。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更有效和更具成本效益的方法来生产 HPV 疫苗 和使用 VLP 的诊断工具提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于计算模型和实验观察,在完全复制体内条件方面可能存在局限性,但证据得到模拟和实验数据的支持,使其具有中等的置信度。

The assembly pathway for human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein is encoded in electrostatic interactions.

Authors: X Yuan, K N Nand, J C Patel, A Jin, C Bystroff
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus L1 protein spontaneously folds and assembles into 60 nm virus-like particles (VLP), with T = 7d icosahedral symmetry at high salt concentrations but not in low salt. Rapid increase from low to high salt during the assembly process gives poor-quality VLPs, while slowly increasing salt, over as many as 48 h, gives rise to high-quality VLPs. Insight into this behavior is drawn from a computational model based on the cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) structure of the human papillomavirus Type 16 VLP. Electrostatic potential energy barriers were calculated using the linearized Poisson-Boltzmann equation. After initial, rapid oligomerization of monomers into symmetrical pentamers (capsomeres), these units form pairwise interactions in energetic order, lowest barrier first. But at high salt concentrations, the energetic barriers are more or less equal and irregular particles form, both in simulation and in experiments. The interaction interfaces were modified by mutation to favor the smaller, 30 nm T = 1 icosahedral symmetry and disfavor T = 7d symmetry. The results were smaller particles. By carefully controlling ionic strength, and/or by mutating interface residues by design, it is possible to make high quality, highly immunogenic VLPs directly from unpurified bacterial lysate, without requiring the use of a disassembly/reassembly step.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the assembly pathway of human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein and how salt concentration affects the formation of virus-like particles (VLPs) with specific icosahedral symmetries.
Design and population
The study used a computational model based on the cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) structure of the human papillomavirus Type 16 VLP to investigate the assembly process, focusing on the behavior of L1 protein under varying salt concentrations.
Core methods
The researchers calculated electrostatic potential energy barriers using the linearized Poisson-Boltzmann equation and modified interaction interfaces through mutation to favor specific symmetries.
Main results
The results showed that rapid increases in salt concentration led to poor-quality VLPs, while slow increases over 48 hours produced high-quality VLPs. Mutations were found to favor smaller, 30 nm T = 1 icosahedral symmetry and disfavor T = 7d symmetry.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that controlling ionic strength and mutating interface residues can lead to the formation of high-quality, immunogenic VLPs directly from unpurified bacterial lysate, without the need for disassembly/reassembly steps.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings could inform the development of more efficient and cost-effective methods for producing HPV vaccines and diagnostic tools using VLPs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on computational modeling and experimental observations, which may have limitations in fully replicating in vivo conditions, but the evidence is supported by both simulation and experimental data, giving it a moderate confidence level.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

使用 Swedescore 与传统阴道镜检查的系统方法检测 2 级或更严重宫颈上皮内瘤变的诊断准确性:一项多中心、非随机临床研究。

Diagnostic accuracy of a systematic approach using Swedescore versus conventional colposcopy for the detection of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse: A multicenter, non-randomized clinical study.
作者： Ane-Kersti Skaarup Knudsen, Line Winther Gustafson, Berit Bargum Booth, Tina Hovgaard Randrup, Ulrik Schiøler Kesmodel, Björn Strander, Ameli Tropé, Pekka Nieminen, Anne Hammer, SWEDESCORE Study Group
摘要中文翻译INTRODUCTION: 全球范围内,宫颈镜检查通常由未经认证的非专家人员进行,且不常规使用宫颈镜评分工具。为提高宫颈上皮内瘤变2级或以上(CIN2+)的检出率,已开发出一种系统性的宫颈镜评分工具Swedescore。我们旨在比较两种宫颈镜检查方法(系统性使用Swedescore与传统宫颈镜检查)在检测CIN2+方面的诊断准确性。MATERIAL AND METHODS: 本研究是一项多中心、非随机的临床研究,纳入了23-65岁因宫颈镜检查而被转诊且可见转化区(类型1和2)的女性。宫颈镜检查采用系统性评分工具Swedescore或传统方法进行。所有女性均采集了四份宫颈活检样本。宫颈镜检查人员从异常区域采集病变导向活检(目标活检),并根据需要从看似正常的区域采集非病变导向活检(随机活检)。金标准是基于所有四份活检样本确定的最严重宫颈上皮内瘤变2级或以上(CIN2+)。主要结局是CIN2+。我们计算了敏感度(SN)、特异度(SP)、阳性预测值(PPV)和阴性预测值(NPV)的95%置信区间,并比较了两种方法的结果。结果按年龄、体重指数(BMI)、高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)状态、筛查方法、索引细胞学检查、索引HPV类型以及宫颈镜检查人员进行分层。临床试验注册号 ClinicalTrials.gov NCT05870787. RESULTS: 共纳入929名女性(Swedescore组534名,传统方法组395名),Swedescore组在检测CIN2+的诊断准确性方面略高于传统方法组(SN: 84.7% vs. 77.8%,SP: 37.3% vs. 30.9%,PPV: 47.6% vs. 43.9%,NPV: 78.3% vs. 76.5%)。在≥50岁的女性中,Swedescore组的敏感度显著高于传统方法组(92.9% vs. 62.5%);在高危型HPV状态的女性中,Swedescore组的敏感度显著高于传统方法组(92.4% vs. 83.2%);当由实习医生进行宫颈镜检查时,Swedescore组的敏感度也显著高于传统方法组(91.8% vs. 79.5%)。无论HPV状态如何或筛查方法如何,Swedescore组的诊断准确性均略优于传统方法组,但差异无统计学意义。CONCLUSIONS: 我们的研究结果可能表明,在非专家环境下,使用Swedescore进行系统性宫颈镜检查与传统方法相比,与检测CIN2+的诊断准确性略有提高。这些结果应结合两种方法之间的基线不平衡进行解读。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在全球范围内,阴道镜检查通常由非专家进行,没有经过认证或没有常规使用阴道镜评分工具。系统性阴道镜评分工具 Swedescore 的开发旨在提高 2 级或更严重的宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN2+) 的检出率。
设计与对象
这项多中心、非随机临床研究纳入了转诊接受阴道镜检查并具有可见变性区(1 型和 2 型)的女性(23-65 岁)。使用系统评分工具 Swedescore 或传统方法进行阴道镜检查。
核心方法
我们计算了具有 95% 置信区间的敏感性 (SN)、特异性 (SP)、阳性预测值 (PPV) 和阴性预测值 (NPV),并比较了两种方法的结果。所有女性均采集了四份宫颈活检样本。阴道镜检查人员从异常区域收集病变定向活检(目标活检),并从看似正常的区域收集或不收集非病变定向活检(随机活检)。
主要结果
在纳入的 929 名女性中(Swedescore:534,传统:395),与传统方法相比,Swedescore 检测 CIN2+ 的诊断准确性略高(SN:84.7% vs. 77.8%,SP:37.3% vs. 30.9%,PPV:47.6% vs. 43.9%,NPV:78.3% vs.与传统方法相比,Swedescore 对 50 岁以上女性(92.9% vs. 62.5%)、具有高级细胞学检查的女性(92.4% vs. 83.2%)以及住院医师进行阴道镜检查(91.8% vs. 83.2%)的 CIN2+ 的敏感性显着更高。
解释与创新
无论 HPV 疫苗接种 状态和筛查方法如何,Swedescore 的表现均略优于传统方法,但差异不具有统计学意义。我们的研究结果可能表明,在非专家环境中,与传统方法相比,使用 Swedescore 进行系统阴道镜检查与检测 CIN2+ 的诊断准确性稍高相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于在非专家环境中使用系统阴道镜评分工具来改善 CIN2+ 的检测,并可能增强 宫颈癌 筛查计划。
局限与证据强度
应考虑两种方法之间的基线不平衡来解释这些发现。该研究是非随机的,诊断准确性的差异没有统计学意义。

Diagnostic accuracy of a systematic approach using Swedescore versus conventional colposcopy for the detection of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse: A multicenter, non-randomized clinical study.

Authors: Ane-Kersti Skaarup Knudsen, Line Winther Gustafson, Berit Bargum Booth, Tina Hovgaard Randrup, Ulrik Schiøler Kesmodel, Björn Strander, Ameli Tropé, Pekka Nieminen, Anne Hammer, SWEDESCORE Study Group
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Globally, colposcopy is often performed by non-experts with no certification or routine use of colposcopic scoring tools. A systematic colposcopic scoring tool, the Swedescore, has been developed to enhance the detection rate of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse (CIN2+). We aimed to compare the diagnostic accuracy of two colposcopy methods (systematic use of the Swedescore versus conventional colposcopy) for detecting CIN2+. MATERIAL AND METHODS: This multicenter, non-randomized clinical study included women (23-65 years) referred for colposcopy and with a visible transformation zone (type 1 and 2). Colposcopy was performed using the systematic scoring tool, Swedescore, or the conventional method. All women had four cervical biopsies collected. Colposcopists collected lesion-directed biopsies (target biopsies) from abnormal areas with or without non-lesion-directed biopsies (random biopsies) from areas that appeared normal. The gold standard was the most severe diagnosis (CIN2+) based on all four biopsies. The primary outcome was CIN2+. We calculated sensitivity (SN), specificity (SP), positive predictive value (PPV), and negative predictive value (NPV) with 95% confidence intervals and compared results between the two methods. Results were stratified by age, body mass index (BMI), human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination status, screening method, index cytology, index HPV type, and colposcopists. Clinical Trial Registration ClinicalTrials.gov NCT05870787. RESULTS: Among 929 women included (Swedescore: 534, conventional: 395), the diagnostic accuracy for the detection of CIN2+ was slightly higher with the Swedescore compared to the conventional method (SN: 84.7% vs. 77.8%, SP: 37.3% vs. 30.9%, PPV: 47.6% vs. 43.9%, NPV: 78.3% vs. Sensitivity for CIN2+ was significantly higher with the Swedescore compared to the conventional method in women ≥50 years (92.9% vs. 62.5%), those with high-grade cytology (92.4% vs. 83.2%), and when colposcopy was performed by residents (91.8% vs. Regardless of HPV vaccination status and screening method, the Swedescore performed slightly better than the conventional method, although the differences were not statistically significant. CONCLUSIONS: Our findings may suggest that, in a non-expert setting, a systematic colposcopic examination using the Swedescore was associated with a slightly higher diagnostic accuracy for detecting CIN2+ compared to the conventional method. These findings should be interpreted considering baseline imbalances between the two methods.

These findings may inform the use of systematic colposcopic scoring tools in non-expert settings to improve the detection of CIN2+ and potentially enhance cervical cancer screening programs.

小儿肛门生殖器疣:临床特征、传播 模式和 治疗 模式的多中心回顾性研究

Pediatric anogenital warts: A multicenter retrospective study of clinical features, modes of transmission, and treatment patterns
作者： Malorie Veyrieres, Alice Phan, Ruxanda Moschoi, Jasmine Flament, Eve Puzenat, Emmanuel Mahé, Valentine Heidelberger, Steff-Naud M, Simon Benkimoun, Marie Chinazzo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肛门生殖器疣是与 HPV 相关的良性病变,在儿童中并不常见,其 传播 模式存在争议,且有关其治疗的证据有限。
设计与对象
我们对法国 14 个医院皮肤科的 63 名被诊断患有肛门生殖器疣的儿童进行了一项多中心回顾性研究,以描述他们的临床特征、传播 模式和 治疗 模式的推测。
核心方法
作为二线 治疗,鬼臼毒素是最常用的处方疗法 (n = 6),其次是咪喹莫特 (n = 4) 和冷冻疗法 (n = 2)。
主要结果
Imiquimod was the most frequently prescribed first-line treatment (45/63, 71.4%), with a median treatment duration of 8 weeks; among 45 evaluable children, 22 (48.9%) achieved complete clearance within 8 weeks.
解释与创新
咪喹莫特的频繁超说明书使用强调了专门的儿科研究和基于证据的 治疗 建议的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
肛门生殖器疣主要影响年轻女孩(43 名女孩 [68.3%],诊断 的中位年龄:4.0 岁)和肛周区域;没有发现性虐待案件,而在近一半的可评估案件中,异种接种被认为是最有可能的 传播 模式。
局限与证据强度
总体而言,治疗 的耐受性良好,没有报告严重不良事件。

Pediatric anogenital warts: A multicenter retrospective study of clinical features, modes of transmission, and treatment patterns

Authors: Malorie Veyrieres, Alice Phan, Ruxanda Moschoi, Jasmine Flament, Eve Puzenat, Emmanuel Mahé, Valentine Heidelberger, Steff-Naud M, Simon Benkimoun, Marie Chinazzo
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Anogenital warts are benign HPV-related lesions that are uncommon in children, with controversial modes of transmission and limited evidence regarding their management. We conducted a multicenter retrospective study of 63 children diagnosed with anogenital warts across 14 French hospital dermatology departments to describe their clinical characteristics, presumed modes of transmission, and treatment patterns. Anogenital warts predominantly affected young girls (43 girls [68.3%], median age at diagnosis: 4.0 years) and the perianal area; no cases of sexual abuse were identified, while hetero-inoculation was considered the most likely mode of transmission in nearly half of evaluable cases. Imiquimod was the most frequently prescribed first-line treatment (45/63, 71.4%), with a median treatment duration of 8 weeks; among 45 evaluable children, 22 (48.9%) achieved complete clearance within 8 weeks. As second-line treatment, podophyllotoxin was the most frequently prescribed therapy (n = 6), followed by imiquimod (n = 4) and cryotherapy (n = 2). Overall, treatments were well tolerated, with no serious adverse events reported. Among the 45 children who received imiquimod with evaluable safety data, local adverse events occurred in 21 (46.7%). The frequent off-label use of imiquimod underscores the need for dedicated pediatric studies and evidence-based treatment recommendations.

Limitations and evidence strength
Overall, treatments were well tolerated, with no serious adverse events reported.

孟加拉国宫颈疾病女性的高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 优势和基因型多样性

High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Predominance and Genotypic Diversity among Women with Cervical Complaints in Bangladesh
作者： Md. Touki Tahamid Tusar, Rifat Ara, Anupam Das, Safayet Hossain Piyel, Md. Mahfuj Islam, Sarowar Haque, Sharmin Akter, Shaqib Adnan Rahman, Mohammad Saiful Islam Pathan, Shahana Pervin
摘要中文翻译背景 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是孟加拉国 宫颈癌 (CC) 的主要原因。本研究调查了患有宫颈疾病的孟加拉国女性中 HPV 的发生率、循环基因型及其系统发育关系。它还表征了来自孟加拉国的 HPV45 毒株的完整 基因组、谱系和 突变。方法 从孟加拉国一家三级医院采集了 102 名患有宫颈疾病的女性的宫颈拭子。使用基因特异性引物通过 PCR 扩增检测 HPV。在HPV阳性样本中,选择PCR条带强度较强的样本进行部分L1基因的Sanger测序。在 Illumina NovaSeq X Plus 平台上对代表性 HPV45 毒株进行完整的 基因组 测序。使用 MAFFT、IQ-TREE2 和 RAxML-NG 进行系统发育分析。使用生物信息学和结构建模工具进行突变、结构和蛋白质稳定性分析。结果 47.1% (48/102) 的参与者检测到 HPV DNA。基于PCR条带强度强,选择进行桑格测序的36个样本中,有21个样本成功测序。对测序菌株的基因分型显示,高危型HPV 基因型占主导地位(95.45%);HPV16(68.18%)和HPV18(13.63%)。系统发育分析证明了基因型特异性聚类以及与全球流行菌株的密切遗传相关性。HPV45 毒株 (HPV_MGH-TT01) 的完整基因组特征揭示了属于 A2 亚系的 7,849 bp 基因组,与全球参考毒株具有 98.0-99.9% 的核苷酸同一性。尽管结构分析预测只有较小的构象改变,但鉴定出多个氨基酸取代,特别是在 E2 蛋白内。结论 高危型HPV 基因型的优势和 HPV45 的基因组特征凸显了大规模 基因组监测 和扩大分子筛查对于孟加拉国预防 CC 的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV 是孟加拉国 宫颈癌 (CC) 的主要原因。本研究调查了患有宫颈疾病的孟加拉国女性中 HPV 的发生率、循环基因型及其系统发育关系。
设计与对象
从孟加拉国一家三级医院采集了 102 名患有宫颈疾病的女性的宫颈拭子。该研究重点关注 HPV 阳性样本,并对来自孟加拉国的 HPV45 毒株的完整 基因组 进行了表征。
核心方法
使用基因特异性引物通过 PCR 扩增检测 HPV。在HPV阳性样本中,选择PCR条带强度较强的样本进行部分L1基因的Sanger测序。在 Illumina NovaSeq X Plus 平台上对代表性 HPV45 毒株进行完整的 基因组 测序。
主要结果
47.1% (48/102) 的参与者检测到 HPV DNA。对测序菌株的基因分型显示,高危型HPV 基因型占主导地位(95.45%);HPV16(68.18%)和HPV18(13.63%)。
解释与创新
高危型HPV 基因型的优势和 HPV45 的基因组特征凸显了大规模 基因组监测 和扩大分子筛查对于孟加拉国预防 CC 的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,孟加拉国需要开展有针对性的 HPV 筛查和 监测 计划,以更好地了解和管理 HPV 相关的 监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于一家三级医院且样本量相对较小,这可能会影响普适性。由于研究的设计和范围,证据是中等的。

High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Predominance and Genotypic Diversity among Women with Cervical Complaints in Bangladesh

Authors: Md. Touki Tahamid Tusar, Rifat Ara, Anupam Das, Safayet Hossain Piyel, Md. Mahfuj Islam, Sarowar Haque, Sharmin Akter, Shaqib Adnan Rahman, Mohammad Saiful Islam Pathan, Shahana Pervin
Original abstract (English)
Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the main cause of cervical cancer (CC) in Bangladesh. This study investigated HPV occurrence, circulating genotypes, and their phylogenetic relationships among Bangladeshi women with cervical complaints. It also characterized the complete genome, lineage, and mutations of an HPV45 strain from Bangladesh. Methods Cervical swabs from 102 women with cervical complaints were obtained at a tertiary-care hospital in Bangladesh. HPV was detected by PCR amplification using gene-specific primers. Among the HPV-positive samples, those showing strong PCR band intensity were selected for Sanger sequencing of the partial L1 gene. Complete genome sequencing of a representative HPV45 strain was sequenced on an Illumina NovaSeq X Plus platform. Phylogenetic analyses were conducted using MAFFT, IQ-TREE2, and RAxML-NG. Mutational, structural, and protein stability analyses were performed using bioinformatic and structural modeling tools. Results HPV DNA was detected in 47.1% (48/102) of participants. Of the 36 samples selected for Sanger sequencing based on strong PCR band intensity, 21 were successfully sequenced. Genotyping of sequenced strains revealed a predominance of high-risk HPV genotypes (95.45%); HPV16 (68.18%) and HPV18 (13.63%). Phylogenetic analyses demonstrated genotype-specific clustering and close genetic relatedness to globally circulating strains. Complete genomic characterization of an HPV45 strain (HPV_MGH-TT01) revealed a 7,849-bp genome belonging to the A2 sublineage, with 98.0-99.9% nucleotide identity to global reference strains. Multiple amino acid substitutions were identified, particularly within the E2 protein, although structural analyses predicted only minor conformational alterations. Conclusions The predominance of high-risk HPV genotypes and the genomic characterization of HPV45 highlight the importance of large-scale genomic surveillance and expanded molecular screening for preventing CC in Bangladesh.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HPV is the main cause of cervical cancer (CC) in Bangladesh. This study investigated HPV occurrence, circulating genotypes, and their phylogenetic relationships among Bangladeshi women with cervical complaints.
Design and population
Cervical swabs from 102 women with cervical complaints were obtained at a tertiary-care hospital in Bangladesh. The study focused on HPV-positive samples and characterized the complete genome of an HPV45 strain from Bangladesh.
Core methods
HPV was detected by PCR amplification using gene-specific primers. Among the HPV-positive samples, those showing strong PCR band intensity were selected for Sanger sequencing of the partial L1 gene. Complete genome sequencing of a representative HPV45 strain was performed on an Illumina NovaSeq X Plus platform.
Main results
HPV DNA was detected in 47.1% (48/102) of participants. Genotyping of sequenced strains revealed a predominance of high-risk HPV genotypes (95.45%); HPV16 (68.18%) and HPV18 (13.63%).
Interpretation and novelty
The predominance of high-risk HPV genotypes and the genomic characterization of HPV45 highlight the importance of large-scale genomic surveillance and expanded molecular screening for preventing CC in Bangladesh.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted HPV screening and surveillance programs in Bangladesh to better understand and manage HPV-associated cervical cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single tertiary-care hospital and a relatively small sample size, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate due to the study's design and scope.

LY01620(一种针对 人乳头瘤病毒 16 相关癌症的治疗性 mRNA 疫苗)的非临床药理学和毒理学研究

Nonclinical pharmacology and toxicology studies of LY01620, a therapeutic mRNA vaccine against human papillomavirus 16-related cancers
作者： Guang-Ying Du, Nouredine Behloul, Chun-Jie Sha, Wen-Zhong Chen, Jing-Wei Tian
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染和相关癌症仍然是全球重大的公共卫生问题。在高危型HPV基因型中,HPV16表现出最高的流行病学患病率。病毒癌蛋白E6和E7是HPV16相关癌变的关键驱动因素,并已成为治疗HPV16相关宫颈上皮内瘤变和癌症的关键治疗靶点。HPV预防药疫苗已广泛应用于临床;然而,针对感染者的有效治疗干预措施仍然有限。因此,迫切需要开发治疗性HPV 疫苗。LY01620 是一种针对 HPV16 E6/E7 抗原的研究中 mRNA 治疗药物 疫苗。为支持LY01620的临床试验应用和开发,开展了全面的非临床研究,包括小鼠和大鼠的免疫原性评估、C3.43荷瘤小鼠的抗肿瘤活性和机制研究、大鼠单剂量组织分布研究、以及结合毒代动力学、局部刺激和免疫毒性评估的符合GLP标准的大鼠重复剂量毒性研究。LY01620在小鼠和大鼠中表现出很强的免疫原性,并通过诱导强Th1极化免疫应答、增加抗原特异性T细胞的频率、增强CD8+T细胞和成熟巨噬细胞向肿瘤的浸润而表现出显着的抗肿瘤活性。LY01620 mRNA 在腹股沟淋巴结和脾脏中表现出持续积累,可能促进持续的抗原呈递和延长免疫效应。在SD大鼠重复给药毒性研究中,LY01620表现出良好的安全性,最高剂量为300μg/只,未观察到不良反应。总之,临床前研究表明LY01620表现出良好的作用相关功效机制和良好的耐受性安全性,支持其进一步开发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种针对 HPV16 相关癌症的治疗性 mRNA 疫苗,以满足对 HPV16 感染和相关癌症进行有效干预的需求。
设计与对象
在小鼠和大鼠中进行了全面的非临床研究,以评估 LY01620(一种针对 HPV16 E6/E7 抗原的 mRNA 治疗剂 疫苗)的免疫原性、抗肿瘤活性和安全性。
核心方法
该研究涉及小鼠和大鼠的免疫原性评估、C3.43荷瘤小鼠的抗肿瘤活性和机制研究、大鼠单剂量组织分布研究以及符合GLP标准的大鼠重复剂量毒性研究。
主要结果
LY01620表现出很强的免疫原性和显着的抗肿瘤活性,在淋巴结和脾脏中具有持续的抗原积累,并且具有良好的安全性。
解释与创新
该研究结果支持进一步开发 LY01620 作为治疗 HPV16 相关癌症的 疫苗 药物,强调了其作用机制和安全性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究的结果可以为治疗性 HPV 疫苗 的开发提供信息,并有助于预防 HPV16 相关癌症。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用动物模型以及需要进一步的临床试验来确认LY01620在人体中的安全性和有效性。

Nonclinical pharmacology and toxicology studies of LY01620, a therapeutic mRNA vaccine against human papillomavirus 16-related cancers

Authors: Guang-Ying Du, Nouredine Behloul, Chun-Jie Sha, Wen-Zhong Chen, Jing-Wei Tian
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and related cancers remain significant public health concerns worldwide. Among high-risk HPV genotypes, HPV16 demonstrates the highest epidemiological prevalence. The viral oncoproteins E6 and E7 are crucial drivers of HPV16-associated carcinogenesis and have become key therapeutic targets for treating HPV16-related cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and cancers. HPV prophylactic vaccines have been widely implemented in clinical practice; however, effective therapeutic interventions for infected individuals remain limited. Therefore, the development of therapeutic HPV vaccines is urgently needed. LY01620 is an investigational mRNA therapeutic vaccine targeting HPV16 E6/E7 antigens. To support LY01620’s clinical trial application and development, comprehensive nonclinical studies were conducted, including immunogenicity evaluations in mice and rats, anti-tumor activity and mechanistic studies in C3.43 tumor-bearing mice, single-dose tissue distribution study in rats, and a GLP-compliant repeated-dose toxicity study in rats combined with toxicokinetics, local irritation, and immunotoxicity assessments. LY01620 demonstrated strong immunogenicity in mice and rats and exhibited significant anti-tumor activity through inducing a strong Th1-polarized immune response, increasing frequency of antigen-specific T cells, and enhancing infiltration of CD8 + T cells and mature macrophages into tumors. LY01620 mRNA exhibited sustained accumulation in inguinal lymph nodes and spleens, potentially facilitating persistent antigen presentation and prolonged immunological effects. In the repeated-dose toxicity study in SD rats, LY01620 exhibited a favorable safety profile, with the no observed adverse effect level was the highest dose of 300 μg/animal. In conclusion, preclinical studies have demonstrated that LY01620 exhibited favorable mechanism of action-related efficacy and a well-tolerated safety profile, supporting its further development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a therapeutic mRNA vaccine against HPV16-related cancers, addressing the need for effective interventions for HPV16 infection and related cancers.
Design and population
Comprehensive nonclinical studies were conducted in mice and rats to evaluate the immunogenicity, anti-tumor activity, and safety of LY01620, an mRNA therapeutic vaccine targeting HPV16 E6/E7 antigens.
Core methods
The study involved immunogenicity evaluations in mice and rats, anti-tumor activity and mechanistic studies in C3.43 tumor-bearing mice, a single-dose tissue distribution study in rats, and a GLP-compliant repeated-dose toxicity study in rats.
Main results
LY01620 demonstrated strong immunogenicity and significant anti-tumor activity, with sustained antigen accumulation in lymph nodes and spleens, and a favorable safety profile.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings support the further development of LY01620 as a therapeutic vaccine for HPV16-related cancers, highlighting its mechanism of action and safety.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study’s results could inform the development of therapeutic HPV vaccines and contribute to the prevention and treatment of HPV16-related cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s limitations include the use of animal models and the need for further clinical trials to confirm the safety and efficacy of LY01620 in humans.

人乳头瘤病毒 阴道和既往下生殖道肿瘤之间的基因型分布和一致性:基于组织的研究

Human papillomavirus genotype distribution and concordance between vaginal and prior lower genital tract neoplasia: a tissue-based study
作者： Borek Sehnal, Monika Rysava, Jana Drozenova, Petr Waldauf, Nikola Janovska, Radoslav Matej, Anna Babkova, Karolina Robova, Marketa Kalinova, Lukas Rob
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 HPV 基因型的分布及其在阴道上皮内瘤变 (VaIN2+) 和既往 HPV 相关下生殖道瘤变之间的一致性,因为该主题的数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
一项回顾性单中心 队列研究 研究的对象是经组织学证实且 HPV 相关 VaIN2+ 且曾接受过下生殖道肿瘤治疗的女性。
核心方法
使用多重实时 PCR 检测对福尔马林固定、石蜡包埋的组织进行 HPV 基因分型。比较了既往患有和未患有 HPV 相关下生殖道肿瘤的女性之间的 HPV 基因型分布,并评估了配对病变之间的基因型一致性。
主要结果
Of 72 eligible women, 39 (54.2%) had previous lower genital tract neoplasia. The vaginal lesion was genotyped in 56 women (30 with, 26 without previous neoplasia); both lesions were evaluable and HPV-positive in 28. HPV16 predominated, detected in 51.8% of the lesions with a valid result. HPV16 and/or HPV18 positivity was more frequent in women with previous neoplasia (66.7% vs. 38.5%; p = 0.059), as was VaIN3+ (66.7% vs. 42.3%; p = 0.106); neither remained below 0.05 after adjustment for multiplicity. HPV16 was independently associated with VaIN3+ (adjusted OR 3.84, 95%CI: 1.22–12.93; p = 0.024). Among the 28 paired cases, concordance was complete in 23 (82.1%), partial in four (14.3%), and absent in one (3.6%); 27 (96.4%) shared at least one genotype.
解释与创新
研究结果表明,VaIN2+ 和既往 HPV 相关的下生殖道肿瘤之间存在较高频率的共享 HPV 基因型,这与相同 HPV 基因型的长期持续存在或重新激活一致。
科研与公卫意义
结果支持在宫颈高级病变 治疗 后需要长期 监测 来监测阴道上皮内瘤变的发展。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制,但由于直接基于组织的分析和统计调整,证据的强度中等。

Human papillomavirus genotype distribution and concordance between vaginal and prior lower genital tract neoplasia: a tissue-based study

Authors: Borek Sehnal, Monika Rysava, Jana Drozenova, Petr Waldauf, Nikola Janovska, Radoslav Matej, Anna Babkova, Karolina Robova, Marketa Kalinova, Lukas Rob
Original abstract (English)
Women previously treated for HPV-associated lower genital tract neoplasia remain at increased risk of developing vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia (VaIN). However, data on HPV genotype concordance based on direct tissue analysis remain limited. We evaluated HPV genotype distribution and concordance between HPV-associated VaIN2+ (VaIN2, VaIN3, or invasive vaginal carcinoma) and previous HPV-associated lower genital tract neoplasia using lesion-specific HPV genotyping of archived tissue specimens. This retrospective single-center cohort study included women with histologically confirmed and HPV-associated VaIN2+​. HPV genotyping was performed on formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded tissue using a multiplex real-time PCR assay. HPV genotype distributions were compared between women with and without previous HPV-associated lower genital tract neoplasia. Genotype concordance between paired lesions and factors associated with VaIN3+ were also evaluated. Of 72 eligible women, 39 (54.2%) had previous lower genital tract neoplasia. The vaginal lesion was genotyped in 56 women (30 with, 26 without previous neoplasia); both lesions were evaluable and HPV-positive in 28. HPV16 predominated, detected in 51.8% of the lesions with a valid result. HPV16 and/or HPV18 positivity was more frequent in women with previous neoplasia (66.7% vs. 38.5%; p = 0.059), as was VaIN3+ (66.7% vs. 42.3%; p = 0.106); neither remained below 0.05 after adjustment for multiplicity. HPV16 was independently associated with VaIN3+ (adjusted OR 3.84, 95%CI: 1.22–12.93; p = 0.024). Among the 28 paired cases, concordance was complete in 23 (82.1%), partial in four (14.3%) and absent in one (3.6%); 27 (96.4%) shared at least one genotype. The interval between lesions reached 31.8 years. Direct tissue-based HPV genotyping showed a high frequency of shared HPV genotypes between VaIN2 + and previous HPV-associated LGT neoplasia. This is consistent with long-term persistence or reactivation of the same HPV genotype, although reinfection with the same genotype cannot be excluded and a common clonal origin was not assessed. Women with previous neoplasia more often had HPV16 and/or HPV18 positivity and VaIN3+, but these exploratory differences were imprecise. The findings support long-term surveillance after treatment of cervical high-grade lesions. Institution: University Hospital Královské Vinohrady, Prague, Czech Republic; reference number EK-VP/34/00/2025; approved 7 May 2025.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the distribution of HPV genotypes and their concordance between vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia (VaIN2+) and prior HPV-associated lower genital tract neoplasia, as data on this topic remain limited.
Design and population
A retrospective single-center cohort study was conducted on women with histologically confirmed and HPV-associated VaIN2+ who had previously been treated for lower genital tract neoplasia.
Core methods
HPV genotyping was performed on formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded tissue using a multiplex real-time PCR assay. HPV genotype distributions were compared between women with and without previous HPV-associated lower genital tract neoplasia, and genotype concordance between paired lesions was evaluated.
Main results
Of 72 eligible women, 39 (54.2%) had previous lower genital tract neoplasia. The vaginal lesion was genotyped in 56 women (30 with, 26 without previous neoplasia); both lesions were evaluable and HPV-positive in 28. HPV16 predominated, detected in 51.8% of the lesions with a valid result. HPV16 and/or HPV18 positivity was more frequent in women with previous neoplasia (66.7% vs. 38.5%; p = 0.059), as was VaIN3+ (66.7% vs. 42.3%; p = 0.106); neither remained below 0.05 after adjustment for multiplicity. HPV16 was independently associated with VaIN3+ (adjusted OR 3.84, 95%CI: 1.22–12.93; p = 0.024). Among the 28 paired cases, concordance was complete in 23 (82.1%), partial in four (14.3%), and absent in one (3.6%); 27 (96.4%) shared at least one genotype.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a high frequency of shared HPV genotypes between VaIN2+ and previous HPV-associated lower genital tract neoplasia, consistent with long-term persistence or reactivation of the same HPV genotype.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results support the need for long-term surveillance after treatment of cervical high-grade lesions to monitor for the development of vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is moderate in strength due to the direct tissue-based analysis and statistical adjustments.
