HPV DNA 整合与宫颈上皮内瘤变 2 (CIN2) 的持续或进展相关。

HPV DNA integration is correlated with persistence or progression of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2 (CIN2).
作者： Anne Van Arsdale, Sonia Gallego, Olga Mescheryakova, Bryan E. Harmon, Brian Haas, Eleanor Agosta, Cristina Montagna, Robert Burk, Mark H. Einstein, Jack Lenz
摘要中文翻译目的:确定 2 级宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN2) 病变中相同的 HPV-人类 DNA 整合在进展为鳞状宫颈癌 (SCC) 的过程中是否保留,以及此类整合是否可以预测病变持续或进展。方法:使用针对 225 种 HPV 类型的多重 DNA 杂交捕获分析纵向宫颈样本,然后进行短读长测序以识别 HPV-人类 DNA 整合连接点。在 (i) 进展为 SCC 的 CIN 病变的连续样本和 (ii) 23 名发生 CIN2 的患者的基线 队列研究 中评估整合事件。使用 Fisher 精确检验和多变量贝叶斯逻辑回归通过积分状态比较临床结果(回归与持续/进展),以估计小样本量设置中的效应大小。结果:七个进展为鳞状细胞癌的 CIN 病变的系列样本中保留了相同的 HPV-人类 DNA 整合,支持了它们在致癌过程中的生物学相关性。在 CIN2 队列研究 中,12 名病变消退的患者中均未检测到整合,而 11 名病变持续或进展的患者中有 4 名存在整合(p=0.04)。当在基线 CIN2 诊断 检测到整合时,贝叶斯逻辑回归估计持续/进展的几率高出 22 倍。HPV16 阳性也与持续/进展相关,估计效应较小(几率高 9.4 倍),与之前的流行病学观察结果一致。结论:HPV DNA 整合与 CIN2 持续存在和进展相关,并且在 进化 至 SCC 期间保留了相同的整合。CIN2 诊断 的整合分析可以识别进展风险最高的病变,从而提供生物标志物来完善临床管理并减少不必要的切除手术。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定 CIN2 病变中的 HPV-人类 DNA 整合在进展为鳞状细胞癌期间是否保留,以及这种整合是否可以预测病变的持续或进展。这填补了了解 HPV DNA 整合在宫颈癌发生中的作用的知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究使用多重 DNA 杂交捕获和短读长测序分析了进展为 SCC 的 CIN2 病变的纵向宫颈样本以及 23 名发生 CIN2 患者的基线 队列研究。
核心方法
使用多重 DNA 杂交捕获和短读长测序来分析样本的 HPV 类型,以识别 HPV-人类 DNA 整合连接点。使用费舍尔精确检验和多变量贝叶斯逻辑回归来比较临床结果。
主要结果
七个进展为鳞状细胞癌的 CIN2 病变中保留了相同的 HPV-人类 DNA 整合。在 CIN2 队列研究 中,11 名病变持续或进展的患者中有 4 名出现整合(p=0.04)。当在基线 CIN2 诊断 检测到整合时,贝叶斯逻辑回归估计持续/进展的几率高出 22 倍。HPV16 阳性也与持续/进展相关,估计效应较小(几率高 9.4 倍)。
解释与创新
研究发现,HPV DNA 整合与 CIN2 持续存在和进展相关,并且在 进化 至 SCC 期间保留了相同的整合。这提供了一种生物标志物来识别进展风险最高的病变,从而可能减少不必要的切除手术。
科研与公卫意义
CIN2 诊断 的整合分析可以识别进展风险最高的病变,从而提供生物标志物来完善临床管理并减少不必要的切除手术。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和贝叶斯逻辑回归的使用的限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

HPV DNA integration is correlated with persistence or progression of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2 (CIN2).

Authors: Anne Van Arsdale, Sonia Gallego, Olga Mescheryakova, Bryan E. Harmon, Brian Haas, Eleanor Agosta, Cristina Montagna, Robert Burk, Mark H. Einstein, Jack Lenz
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE(S): To determine whether identical HPV-human DNA integrations in cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 (CIN2) lesions are retained during progression to squamous cervical carcinoma (SCC) and whether such integrations can predict lesion persistence or progression. METHODS: Longitudinal cervical samples were analyzed using multiplexed DNA hybridization-capture targeting 225 HPV types, followed by short-read sequencing to identify HPV-human DNA integration junctions. Integration events were evaluated in (i) serial samples from CIN lesions that progressed to SCC and (ii) a baseline cohort of 23 patients with incident CIN2. Clinical outcomes (regression vs persistence/progression) were compared by integration status using Fisher's Exact Test and multivariable Bayesian logistic regression to estimate effect sizes in the setting of small sample size. RESULTS: Identical HPV-human DNA integrations were preserved across serial samples in seven CIN lesions that progressed to SCC, supporting their biological relevance in carcinogenesis. In the CIN2 cohort, no integrations were detected in any of the 12 patients whose lesions regressed, whereas integrations were present in four of eleven patients whose lesions persisted or progressed (p=0.04). Bayesian logistic regression estimated a 22-fold higher odds of persistence/progression when integration was detected at baseline CIN2 diagnosis. HPV16 positivity was also associated with persistence/progression, with a smaller estimated effect size (9.4-fold higher odds), consistent with prior epidemiologic observations. CONCLUSION(S): HPV DNA integrations were associated with CIN2 persistence and progression, and identical integrations were retained during evolution to SCC. Integration analysis at CIN2 diagnosis may identify lesions at highest risk for progression, providing a biomarker to refine clinical management and reduce unnecessary excisional procedures.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine whether HPV-human DNA integrations in CIN2 lesions are retained during progression to SCC and whether such integrations can predict lesion persistence or progression. This addresses a knowledge gap in understanding the role of HPV DNA integration in cervical carcinogenesis.
Design and population
The study analyzed longitudinal cervical samples from CIN2 lesions that progressed to SCC and a baseline cohort of 23 patients with incident CIN2 using multiplexed DNA hybridization-capture and short-read sequencing.
Core methods
Samples were analyzed for HPV types using multiplexed DNA hybridization-capture followed by short-read sequencing to identify HPV-human DNA integration junctions. Clinical outcomes were compared using Fisher's Exact Test and multivariable Bayesian logistic regression.
Main results
Identical HPV-human DNA integrations were preserved in seven CIN2 lesions that progressed to SCC. In the CIN2 cohort, integrations were present in four of eleven patients whose lesions persisted or progressed (p=0.04). Bayesian logistic regression estimated a 22-fold higher odds of persistence/progression when integration was detected at baseline CIN2 diagnosis. HPV16 positivity was also associated with persistence/progression, with a smaller estimated effect size (9.4-fold higher odds).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that HPV DNA integrations were associated with CIN2 persistence and progression, and identical integrations were retained during evolution to SCC. This provides a biomarker to identify lesions at highest risk for progression, potentially reducing unnecessary excisional procedures.
Scientific and public-health significance
Integration analysis at CIN2 diagnosis may identify lesions at highest risk for progression, providing a biomarker to refine clinical management and reduce unnecessary excisional procedures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and the use of Bayesian logistic regression, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate.

评估资源配置并探索在中国北京推广人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种的措施

Assessing resource allocation and exploring measures to promote human papillomavirus vaccination in Beijing, China
作者： Qian-Li Yuan, Yu-Ping Duan, Lai Wei, Luo-Dan Suo, Rui Yu, Zhi-Qiang Cao, Lu-Zhao Feng, Juan Li
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在全面评估北京市HPV 疫苗接种服务的可用性和资源分配,并确定提高公众对疫苗接受度和使用率的有效策略。
设计与对象
2024年,对北京499家疫苗接种诊所进行了生态研究。
核心方法
采用负二项式回归模型来估计 HPV 发病率 的 发病率 比率 (IRR)。
主要结果
进行家长教育和建立电子排队系统等举措与较高的 疫苗接种 剂量相关。
解释与创新
免疫诊所的平均每周服务时间和人员配备(8 小时,10 名工作人员)低于成人诊所(27 小时,18 名工作人员)。
科研与公卫意义
为了缓解常规免疫诊所 HPV 疫苗接种 资源的短缺,并鉴于目前成人 疫苗接种 诊所的服务能力更强,可能有必要在选定的二级和三级机构进行试点扩展,等待正式的成本效益评估。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Assessing resource allocation and exploring measures to promote human papillomavirus vaccination in Beijing, China

Authors: Qian-Li Yuan, Yu-Ping Duan, Lai Wei, Luo-Dan Suo, Rui Yu, Zhi-Qiang Cao, Lu-Zhao Feng, Juan Li
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to comprehensively assess the availability and resource allocation of HPV vaccination services in Beijing, and to identify effective strategies for improving public vaccine acceptance and uptake. An ecological study was conducted across 499 vaccination clinics in Beijing in 2024. A structured questionnaire was administered to collect data on HPV vaccination service provision at each vaccination clinic. Nearest neighbor analysis, kernel density analysis, hot-spot analysis, and the resource-utilization efficiency matrix were applied to assess the allocation and utilization of vaccination resources. A negative binomial regression model was employed to estimate the incidence rate ratio (IRR) for HPV vaccination. In Beijing, 39.28% of health-care facilities and 93.59% of vaccination clinics provided HPV vaccination services. Among these vaccination clinics, 79.87% were based in primary health-care facilities, while only 4.07% were adult vaccination clinics located in secondary or tertiary hospitals. Mean weekly service hours and staffing were lower in immunization clinics (8 hours, 10 staff) than in adult clinics (27 hours, 18 staff). Electronic queuing systems (IRR = 1.45, 95% CI: 1.21–1.73), offline vaccination education (IRR = 1.43, 95% CI: 1.07–1.92), and ≥4 parental classes monthly (IRR = 2.93, 95% CI: 1.29–6.65) were significantly associated with higher HPV vaccination coverage. To alleviate shortages of HPV vaccination resources at routine immunization clinics and given the stronger service capacity of current adult vaccination clinics, pilot expansion at selected secondary and tertiary facilities may be warranted, pending formal cost-effectiveness evaluation. Initiatives such as conducting parental education and establishing electronic queuing systems were associated with higher vaccination doses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to comprehensively assess the availability and resource allocation of HPV vaccination services in Beijing, and to identify effective strategies for improving public vaccine acceptance and uptake.
Design and population
An ecological study was conducted across 499 vaccination clinics in Beijing in 2024.
Core methods
A negative binomial regression model was employed to estimate the incidence rate ratio (IRR) for HPV vaccination.
Main results
Initiatives such as conducting parental education and establishing electronic queuing systems were associated with higher vaccination doses.
Interpretation and novelty
Mean weekly service hours and staffing were lower in immunization clinics (8 hours, 10 staff) than in adult clinics (27 hours, 18 staff).
Scientific and public-health significance
To alleviate shortages of HPV vaccination resources at routine immunization clinics and given the stronger service capacity of current adult vaccination clinics, pilot expansion at selected secondary and tertiary facilities may be warranted, pending formal cost-effectiveness evaluation.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

本科护生对人乳头瘤病毒的认知及其预防:以健康信念模型为指导的定性研究

Undergraduate nursing students’ perceptions of human papillomavirus and its prevention: a qualitative study guided by the Health Belief Model
作者： Rasha A. Mohamed, Mohammad Aloufi, Hanadi Dakhilallah, Shaimaa Fouad Mohammed, Samah Nasser Abd ElAziz ElShora, Nadia Mohamed Hassan Saleh, Maram Alshahrani, Dalia Ahmed Hosny Ahmed, Esraa Mofreh Nabeih, Asmaa Abdelrahman Rabea Mohammed
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是一个重要的公共卫生问题,也是导致 人乳头瘤病毒 以及其他几种恶性肿瘤的主要原因。尽管它很重要,但在沙特阿拉伯,检验护生对 HPV 及其预防的看法的定性证据有限。本研究旨在以健康信念模型 (HBM) 作为理论框架,调查本科护生对 HPV 及其预防的看法。这项描述性定性研究采用了六次焦点小组讨论 (FGD),涉及来自沙特阿拉伯比沙大学的 36 名符合条件的本科护理生。参与者是通过有目的的抽样招募的。主题分析是基于 HBM 进行的,采用其六种结构作为预定义的总体分析领域,而特定的子主题是根据参与者的账户归纳开发的。六个演绎主题,即感知敏感性、感知严重性、感知利益、感知障碍、行动线索和自我效能,对应于 HBM 结构。归纳开发的子主题包括对 HPV 认识不足、个人易感性较低、对 疫苗接种 安全性和 传播 的误解、耻辱以及讨论敏感性健康问题的挑战。参与者还描述了他们认为的机构和教育对沟通准备、专业相关性和 HPV 知识的影响。这些发现并没有证明特定教学方法的有效性,而是强调了该样本中的教育需求。护理本科生在讨论 HPV 预防时发现了知识差距、误解、耻辱、社会挑战和缺乏信心。研究结果强调了提供有关 HPV 传播、筛查、疫苗接种、生殖健康问题、耻辱和沟通技巧的循证和文化敏感教育的必要性。这些需求可以通过基于案例的学习、角色扮演、模拟和沟通培训来满足,但目前的研究没有评估其有效性,需要进一步研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是一个重要的公共卫生问题,也是导致 人乳头瘤病毒 以及其他几种恶性肿瘤的主要原因。尽管它很重要,但在沙特阿拉伯,检验护生对 HPV 及其预防的看法的定性证据有限。
设计与对象
这项描述性定性研究采用了六次焦点小组讨论 (FGD),涉及来自沙特阿拉伯比沙大学的 36 名符合条件的本科护理生。参与者是通过有目的的抽样招募的。
核心方法
主题分析是基于健康信念模型(HBM)进行的,采用其六种结构作为预定义的总体分析领域,而特定的子主题是根据参与者的账户归纳得出的。
主要结果
六个演绎主题,即感知敏感性、感知严重性、感知利益、感知障碍、行动线索和自我效能,对应于 HBM 结构。归纳开发的子主题包括对 HPV 认识不足、个人易感性较低、对 疫苗接种 安全性和 传播 的误解、耻辱以及讨论敏感性健康问题的挑战。
解释与创新
这些发现并没有证明特定教学方法的有效性,而是强调了该样本中的教育需求。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了提供有关 HPV 传播、筛查、疫苗接种、生殖健康问题、耻辱和沟通技巧的循证和文化敏感教育的必要性。

Undergraduate nursing students’ perceptions of human papillomavirus and its prevention: a qualitative study guided by the Health Belief Model

Authors: Rasha A. Mohamed, Mohammad Aloufi, Hanadi Dakhilallah, Shaimaa Fouad Mohammed, Samah Nasser Abd ElAziz ElShora, Nadia Mohamed Hassan Saleh, Maram Alshahrani, Dalia Ahmed Hosny Ahmed, Esraa Mofreh Nabeih, Asmaa Abdelrahman Rabea Mohammed
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a significant public health concern and the leading cause of cervical cancer as well as several other malignancies. Despite its importance, there is limited qualitative evidence examining nursing students’ perceptions of HPV and its prevention in Saudi Arabia. This study sought to investigate undergraduate nursing students’ perceptions of HPV and its prevention using the Health Belief Model (HBM) as a theoretical framework. This descriptive qualitative study utilized six focus group discussions (FGDs) involving 36 eligible undergraduate nursing students from the University of Bisha, Saudi Arabia. Participants were recruited through purposive sampling. Thematic analysis was conducted based on the HBM, employing its six constructs as predefined overarching analytic domains, while specific subthemes were developed inductively from participants’ accounts. The six deductive themes, perceived susceptibility, perceived severity, perceived benefits, perceived barriers, cues to action, and self-efficacy, correspond to the HBM constructs. Inductively developed subthemes included inadequate understanding of HPV, low perceived personal susceptibility, misconceptions about vaccination safety and transmission, stigma, and challenges in discussing sensitive sexual health issues. Participants also described perceived institutional and educational influences on communication preparedness, professional relevance, and HPV knowledge. These findings do not demonstrate the efficacy of specific teaching methods but highlight educational needs within this sample. Undergraduate nursing students identified knowledge gaps, misunderstandings, stigma, societal challenges, and a lack of confidence when discussing HPV prevention. The findings emphasize the necessity of providing evidence-based and culturally sensitive education on HPV transmission, screening, vaccination, reproductive health issues, stigma, and communication skills. These needs could be met through case-based learning, role-playing, simulation and communication training, but the present study did not evaluate their effectiveness and further research is needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a significant public health concern and the leading cause of cervical cancer as well as several other malignancies. Despite its importance, there is limited qualitative evidence examining nursing students’ perceptions of HPV and its prevention in Saudi Arabia.
Design and population
This descriptive qualitative study utilized six focus group discussions (FGDs) involving 36 eligible undergraduate nursing students from the University of Bisha, Saudi Arabia. Participants were recruited through purposive sampling.
Core methods
Thematic analysis was conducted based on the Health Belief Model (HBM), employing its six constructs as predefined overarching analytic domains, while specific subthemes were developed inductively from participants’ accounts.
Main results
The six deductive themes, perceived susceptibility, perceived severity, perceived benefits, perceived barriers, cues to action, and self-efficacy, correspond to the HBM constructs. Inductively developed subthemes included inadequate understanding of HPV, low perceived personal susceptibility, misconceptions about vaccination safety and transmission, stigma, and challenges in discussing sensitive sexual health issues.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings do not demonstrate the efficacy of specific teaching methods but highlight educational needs within this sample.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the necessity of providing evidence-based and culturally sensitive education on HPV transmission, screening, vaccination, reproductive health issues, stigma, and communication skills.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

超越正常细胞学:高危青少年 HPV 16/18 阳性的临床意义 — 病例报告

Beyond Normal Cytology: Clinical Significance of HPV 16/18 Positivity in a High-Risk Adolescent — A Case Report
作者： Arya Ady Nugroho, Kartiwa Hadi Nuryanto
摘要中文翻译背景:高危人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染是宫颈癌的主要病因。适当的基于风险的管理至关重要,特别是对于细胞学正常但 HR-HPV 检测结果呈阳性的年轻患者。Case presentation: A 17-year old single female with a history of commercial sex work presented with recurrent vaginal discharge and previously treated genital warts.她报告说,在离开性工作后,她有多个性伴侣,但不一致地使用安全套。妇科检查发现子宫颈呈红色,有白色分泌物,而子宫颈抹片检查结果正常。然而,HR HPV 检测呈阳性。阴道镜评估显示 11-12 点钟位置出现醋酸白色变化,卢戈氏试验碘摄取呈阴性。进行了环形电外科切除手术(LEEP)活检,组织病理学结果正在等待中。Discussion: According to the 2019 ASCCP risk-based guidelines,patients with positive result of HR-HPV and normal cytology generally have a low immediate risk of CIN3+and may be managed with 监测.然而,尽管细胞学检查正常,但 高危型HPV 型(尤其是 HPV 16/18)的存在证明了早期阴道镜评估的合理性。The Indonesian Gynecologic Oncology (Himpunan Onkologi Ginekologi Indonesia/HOGI) guidelines similarly recommend colposcopy or visual inspection with acetic acid (VIA) in HRHPV-positive patients,followed by appropriate intervention if lesions are detected.结论:该病例强调了对 HR-HPV 阳性患者进行个体化、基于风险的管理的重要性。Early colposcopic assessment may be warranted even with normal cytology,particularly in high-risk populations,to ensure timely detection and 治疗 of precancerous lesions.
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)感染是宫颈癌的主要病因。适当的基于风险的管理至关重要,特别是对于细胞学正常但 HR-HPV 检测结果呈阳性的年轻患者。
设计与对象
病例报告 是针对一名有商业性工作史的 17 岁单身女性进行的,她出现反复性阴道分泌物,并且之前曾治疗过生殖器疣。
核心方法
患者接受了妇科检查、子宫颈抹片检查、HR-HPV 检测和阴道镜评估。进行了环形电外科切除手术(LEEP)活检,组织病理学结果正在等待中。
主要结果
子宫颈抹片检查结果正常,而 HR-HPV 检测呈阳性。阴道镜评估显示 11-12 点钟位置出现醋酸白色变化,卢戈氏试验碘摄取呈阴性。
解释与创新
根据2019年ASCCP基于风险的指南,HR-HPV阳性且细胞学检查正常的患者一般CIN3+的直接风险较低,可以用监测进行治疗。然而,尽管细胞学检查正常,但 高危型HPV 型(尤其是 HPV 16/18)的存在证明了早期阴道镜评估的合理性。
科研与公卫意义
即使细胞学检查正常,也可能需要进行早期阴道镜评估,特别是在高危人群中,以确保及时发现并及时发现癌前病变。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究结果的统计功效或普遍性。该证据基于单个 病例报告,这将其强度限制为中等。

Beyond Normal Cytology: Clinical Significance of HPV 16/18 Positivity in a High-Risk Adolescent — A Case Report

Authors: Arya Ady Nugroho, Kartiwa Hadi Nuryanto
Original abstract (English)
Background: High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection is a major etiological factor for cervical cancer. Appropriate risk-based management is essential, especially in young patients with normal cytology but positive HR-HPV test results. Case presentation: A 17-year old single female with a history of commercial sex work presented with recurrent vaginal discharge and previously treated genital warts. She reported having multiple sexual partners with inconsistent condom use after leaving sex work. Gynecological examination revealed a reddish cervix with whitish discharge, while Pap smear results were normal. However, HR HPV testing was positive. Colposcopic evaluation showed acetowhite changes at the 11–12 o’clock position with negative iodine uptake on Lugol’s test. A loop electrosurgical excision procedure (LEEP) biopsy was performed, and histopathological results were pending. Discussion: According to the 2019 ASCCP risk-based guidelines, patients with positive result of HR-HPV and normal cytology generally have a low immediate risk of CIN3+and may be managed with surveillance. However, the presence of high-risk HPV types, particularly HPV 16/18, justifies early colposcopic evaluation despite normal cytology. The Indonesian Gynecologic Oncology (Himpunan Onkologi Ginekologi Indonesia/HOGI) guidelines similarly recommend colposcopy or visual inspection with acetic acid (VIA) in HRHPV-positive patients, followed by appropriate intervention if lesions are detected. Conclusions: This case highlights the importance of individualized, risk-based management in HR-HPV-positive patients. Early colposcopic assessment may be warranted even with normal cytology, particularly in high-risk populations, to ensure timely detection and treatment of precancerous lesions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection is a major etiological factor for cervical cancer. Appropriate risk-based management is essential, especially in young patients with normal cytology but positive HR-HPV test results.
Design and population
A case report was conducted on a 17-year-old single female with a history of commercial sex work who presented with recurrent vaginal discharge and previously treated genital warts.
Core methods
The patient underwent gynecological examination, Pap smear, HR-HPV testing, and colposcopic evaluation. A loop electrosurgical excision procedure (LEEP) biopsy was performed, and histopathological results were pending.
Main results
The Pap smear results were normal, while HR-HPV testing was positive. Colposcopic evaluation showed acetowhite changes at the 11–12 o’clock position with negative iodine uptake on Lugol’s test.
Interpretation and novelty
According to the 2019 ASCCP risk-based guidelines, patients with positive result of HR-HPV and normal cytology generally have a low immediate risk of CIN3+ and may be managed with surveillance. However, the presence of high-risk HPV types, particularly HPV 16/18, justifies early colposcopic evaluation despite normal cytology.
Scientific and public-health significance
Early colposcopic assessment may be warranted even with normal cytology, particularly in high-risk populations, to ensure timely detection and treatment of precancerous lesions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the statistical power or generalisability of the findings. The evidence is based on a single case report, which limits its strength to moderate.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

1064 PapillomaVax:跨越 HPV16 E2、E5、E6 和 E7 的紧凑型 9 聚体通用多表位 mRNA 疫苗 驱动原代人 PBMC 中同时多特异性 CD8 + T 细胞启动

1064 PapillomaVax: a compact 9-mer universal polyepitope mRNA vaccine spanning HPV16 E2, E5, E6, and E7 drives simultaneous multi-specific CD8 + T cell priming in primary human PBMCs
作者： Olivia Roy, Sindhu Jayamani, Skylar JW Henry, Padhmavathy Yuvaraj, Oliver Kask, Akshansh Kaushik, Michael P. Gustafson, Ann Eshenaur Spolarich, Seena Patel, Karen S. Anderson
摘要中文翻译背景 治疗性 HPV 疫苗接种 需要针对组成型表达的病毒癌蛋白的广泛、多特异性 CD8 + CTL 反应,以维持肿瘤控制并通过选择性抗原下调来限制免疫逃逸,这在免疫编辑的 HPV 阳性肿瘤中已有记录。27 聚体侧翼序列格式很常用,但其编码负担限制了多蛋白覆盖。我们系统地量化了紧凑 9 聚体格式的免疫成本,并将所得设计原理应用于编码来自 E2、E5、E6 和 E7 蛋白的已知免疫原性表位的 16 表位 HPV16 特异性多表位 mRNA。方法比较了四种 HLA-A02:01 限制性 mRNA 构建体:单 9 聚体、多 9 聚体、单 27 聚体和多 27 聚体,编码在多表位条件下,免疫显性流感 M1 表位 GILGFVFTL 与八个次显性填充表位一起。表位特异性 CD8 + T 细胞反应通过 MHC-I 四聚体染色进行定量,然后用 mRNA 电穿孔的自体树突状细胞进行体外引发,并使用 24 小时 K562/Jurkat NFAT-荧光素酶 TCR 报告基因测定和 IFNγ ELISpot 的正交读数。16 表位 HPV16 9 聚体多表位 mRNA 编码验证了来自 E2、E5、E6 和 E7 的多个 HLA 限制性 CTL 表位、HLA 结合和已发表的人 CTL 反应性,通过 MHC-I 四聚体染色、Jurkat NFAT-荧光素酶 TCR 报告基因激活和 IFNγ 评估 HLA-A02:01+ 原代人 PBMC 中的多特异性引发ELISpot.结果 与 9 聚体相比,27 聚体格式的单表位编码适度增强了 GILGFVFTL-四聚体 + 频率(6.29% 对比 4.72%)。在多表位背景下,9聚体形式相对于27聚体上限减少了约50%(6.37%对3.36%),单表位9聚体中间体(4.72%),证实表位间竞争是紧凑构建体中的主要抑制机制。TCR 报告基因激活反映了这种层次结构:多 27mer 驱动的激活比对照高约 50 倍,而多 9mer 则高出约 25 倍(p < 0.001)。尽管紧凑格式固有的竞争惩罚,16表位通用 PapillomaVax mRNA 在原代 HLA-A*02:01+ PBMC 中驱动可检测到的同时针对 E6 和 E7 特异性的 CD8+ T 细胞反应:PapillomaVax mRNA 引发后 E6-四聚体+ 9.49% 和 E7-四聚体+ 7.96%,而 PapillomaVax mRNA 引发后为 0.42% 和未刺激对照为 0.11%,而同源肽刺激为 6.34% 和 5.35%。IFNγ ELISpot 证实了 PapillomaVax mRNA 的抗原特异性反应,与未刺激对照的~25 SFCs/5×105 相比(p < 0.001),超过了 E7 肽(~500 SFCs/5×105,p < 0.01)和 E6 肽(~550 SFCs/5×105,p < 0.001)再刺激条件。Jurkat NFAT-荧光素酶 TCR 报告基因激活证实了 E6(TIHDIILECV;比对照高 39 倍,p < 0.001)和 E7(YMLDLQPET;比对照高 24 倍,p < 0.001)特异性的 MHC-I 限制性抗原呈递。结论 9 聚体多表位格式能够同时启动跨 HPV16 E2、E5、 E6 和 E7 以量化且可接受的每个表位成本。伦理审批 IRB 批准并可根据要求提供。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 HPV16 特异性多表位 mRNA 疫苗 的紧凑 9 聚体格式的免疫成本及其在原代人 PBMC 中驱动同时多特异性 CD8 + T 细胞启动的能力。这一背景强调了针对 HPV 癌蛋白的广泛、多特异性 CD8 + CTL 反应的需要,以控制 HPV 阳性肿瘤。
设计与对象
该研究比较了四种 HLA-A02:01 限制性 mRNA 构建体:单 9mer、多 9mer、单 27mer 和多 27mer,编码免疫显性流感 M1 表位以及多表位条件下的 8 个次显性填充表位。该研究是在来自 HLA-A02:01+ 供体的原代人类 PBMC 中进行的。
核心方法
表位特异性 CD8 + T 细胞反应通过 MHC-I 四聚体染色进行定量,然后用 mRNA 电穿孔的自体树突状细胞进行体外引发,并使用 24 小时 K562/Jurkat NFAT-荧光素酶 TCR 报告基因测定和 IFNγ ELISpot 的正交读数。
主要结果
相对于单表位 9 聚体中间体的 27 聚体上限,9 聚体形式使 CD8 + T 细胞反应降低约 50%。尽管存在竞争惩罚,但 16 个表位通用 PapillomaVax mRNA 在原代 HLA-A*02:01+ PBMC 中驱动了针对 E6 和 E7 特异性的可检测且同步的 CD8+ T 细胞反应。
解释与创新
该研究得出的结论是,9 聚体多表位格式能够以量化且可接受的每个表位成本同时启动跨 HPV16 E2、E5、E6 和 E7 的多蛋白 CTL。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,9 聚体格式可用于开发紧凑的 HPV16 特异性多表位 mRNA 疫苗,它可以驱动同时多特异性 CD8 + T 细胞启动,这对于治疗 HPV 疫苗接种 和肿瘤控制可能具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括使用单一 HLA-A02:01 限制性构建体以及需要在更大、更多样化的人群中进行进一步验证。由于证据仅具有抽象性质,证据强度中等。

1064 PapillomaVax: a compact 9-mer universal polyepitope mRNA vaccine spanning HPV16 E2, E5, E6, and E7 drives simultaneous multi-specific CD8 + T cell priming in primary human PBMCs

Authors: Olivia Roy, Sindhu Jayamani, Skylar JW Henry, Padhmavathy Yuvaraj, Oliver Kask, Akshansh Kaushik, Michael P. Gustafson, Ann Eshenaur Spolarich, Seena Patel, Karen S. Anderson
Original abstract (English)
Background Therapeutic HPV vaccination requires broad, multi-specific CD8 + CTL responses against constitutively expressed viral oncoproteins to sustain tumor control and limit immune escape through selective antigen downregulation, documented in immune-edited HPV-positive tumors. The 27-mer flanking sequence format is commonly used but its coding burden constrains multi-protein coverage. We systematically quantified the immunological cost of a compact 9-mer format and applied the resulting design principles to a 16-epitope HPV16-specific polyepitope mRNA encoding known immunogenic epitopes from E2, E5, E6, and E7 proteins.Methods Four HLA-A02:01-restricted mRNA constructs were compared: single-9mer, multi-9mer, single-27mer, and multi-27mer, encoding the immunodominant influenza M1 epitope GILGFVFTL alongside eight subdominant filler epitopes in the multi-epitope conditions. Epitope-specific CD8 + T cell responses were quantified by MHC-I tetramer staining following in vitro priming with mRNA-electroporated autologous dendritic cells, with orthogonal readouts from 24-hour K562/Jurkat NFAT-luciferase TCR reporter assays and IFNγ ELISpot. A 16-epitope HPV16 9-mer polyepitope mRNA encoding validated multi HLA-restricted CTL epitopes from E2, E5, E6, and E7, HLA binding, and published human CTL reactivity was evaluated for multi-specific priming in HLA-A02:01+ primary human PBMCs by MHC-I tetramer staining, Jurkat NFAT-luciferase TCR reporter activation, and IFNγ ELISpot.Results Single-epitope encoding in 27-mer format modestly enhanced GILGFVFTL-tetramer + frequency over 9-mer (6.29% versus 4.72%). In the multi-epitope context, the 9-mer format imposed a ~50% reduction relative to the 27-mer ceiling (6.37% versus 3.36%), with the single-epitope 9-mer intermediate (4.72%), confirming inter-epitope competition as the dominant suppressive mechanism in compact constructs. TCR reporter activation mirrored this hierarchy: multi-27mer drove ~50-fold above-control activation versus ~25-fold for multi-9mer (p < 0.001). Despite the competition penalty inherent to the compact format, the 16-epitope universal PapillomaVax mRNA drove detectable and simultaneous CD8+ T cell responses against both E6 and E7 specificities in primary HLA-A*02:01+ PBMCs: E6-tetramer+ 9.49% and E7-tetramer+ 7.96% following PapillomaVax mRNA priming versus 0.42% and 0.11% in unstimulated controls, compared to 6.34% and 5.35% with cognate peptide stimulation. IFNγ ELISpot confirmed antigen-specific responses for PapillomaVax mRNA versus ~25 SFCs/5×105 for unstimulated controls (p < 0.001), exceeding both E7 peptide (~500 SFCs/5×105, p < 0.01) and E6 peptide (~550 SFCs/5×105, p < 0.001) restimulation conditions. Jurkat NFAT-luciferase TCR reporter activation confirmed MHC-I-restricted antigen presentation of both E6 (TIHDIILECV; 39-fold above control, p < 0.001) and E7 (YMLDLQPET; 24-fold above control, p < 0.001) specificities.Conclusions The 9-mer polyepitope format enables simultaneous multi-protein CTL priming across HPV16 E2, E5, E6, and E7 at a quantified and acceptable per-epitope cost.Ethics Approval IRB approved and available on request.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the immunological cost of a compact 9-mer format for HPV16-specific polyepitope mRNA vaccine and its ability to drive simultaneous multi-specific CD8 + T cell priming in primary human PBMCs. The background highlights the need for broad, multi-specific CD8 + CTL responses against HPV oncoproteins to control HPV-positive tumors.
Design and population
The study compared four HLA-A02:01-restricted mRNA constructs: single-9mer, multi-9mer, single-27mer, and multi-27mer, encoding the immunodominant influenza M1 epitope alongside eight subdominant filler epitopes in the multi-epitope conditions. The study was performed in primary human PBMCs from HLA-A02:01+ donors.
Core methods
Epitope-specific CD8 + T cell responses were quantified by MHC-I tetramer staining following in vitro priming with mRNA-electroporated autologous dendritic cells, with orthogonal readouts from 24-hour K562/Jurkat NFAT-luciferase TCR reporter assays and IFNγ ELISpot.
Main results
The 9-mer format imposed a ~50% reduction in CD8 + T cell responses relative to the 27-mer ceiling, with the single-epitope 9-mer intermediate. Despite the competition penalty, the 16-epitope universal PapillomaVax mRNA drove detectable and simultaneous CD8+ T cell responses against both E6 and E7 specificities in primary HLA-A*02:01+ PBMCs.
Interpretation and novelty
The study concluded that the 9-mer polyepitope format enables simultaneous multi-protein CTL priming across HPV16 E2, E5, E6, and E7 at a quantified and acceptable per-epitope cost.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the 9-mer format can be used to develop a compact HPV16-specific polyepitope mRNA vaccine that can drive simultaneous multi-specific CD8 + T cell priming, which could be significant for therapeutic HPV vaccination and tumor control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the use of a single HLA-A02:01-restricted construct and the need for further validation in a larger and more diverse population. The evidence strength is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the evidence.

2020-2025 财年日本和歌山县常规首剂 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 覆盖率趋势

Trends in Routine First-Dose Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Coverage in Wakayama Prefecture, Japan, Fiscal Years 2020–2025
作者： Nobuhiko Suzuki, Kosuke Hiramatsu, Shinichiro Takeda, Motoko Kanno, Sayaka Ikeda, Asami Yagi, Naoki Mizunuma, Keiko Yamada, Masahiko Sakaguchi, Kayoko Katayama
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 在日本,人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的主动建议于 2013 年暂停,并于 2022 财年恢复,同时还有一项追赶计划,其首剂资格于 2024 财年结束。后续常规 疫苗接种 队列研究 的覆盖范围仍不清楚。我们描述了 2020-2025 财年和歌山县首剂 疫苗接种 覆盖率,重点关注 2024 年至 2025 年之间特定年级的差异。方法 这项描述性流行病学研究使用了汇总的行政数据。特定年龄的首次剂量计数是从 2020-2021 财年的 e-Stat 和 2022-2025 财年的和歌山县政府获得的。分母是 2020 年人口普查中特定年龄的女性人口。使用已发布的方法按出生财政年度重建 疫苗接种 计数和人口数据。对 2004 年至 2013 年出生的 队列研究 的年度和累积覆盖率进行了估计。结果 2024 财年至 2025 财年,小学六年级学生的年覆盖率从 3.8% 变为 4.1%,初一年级学生的年覆盖率从 20.6% 变为 15.4%,初二学生的年覆盖率从 2.2%变为 7.7%,初三学生的年覆盖率从 30.5%变为 16.4%,初中生的年覆盖率从 13.1%变为 6.1%。高中一年级学生。2008 年和 2009 年出生的 队列研究 到高中一年级结束时的累计覆盖率分别为 46.5% 和 49.9%。覆盖率最高的年级从高中一年级转移到初三。结论 2020-2025 财年,首剂 疫苗接种 的主要时间转向年轻年级。然而,2025年初中一年级、三年级和高中一年级学生的年度覆盖率低于2024年。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在日本,人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的主动建议于 2013 年暂停,并于 2022 财年恢复,同时还有一项追赶计划,其首剂资格于 2024 财年结束。后续常规 疫苗接种 队列研究 的覆盖范围仍不清楚。
设计与对象
这项描述性流行病学研究使用汇总管理数据来描述 2020-2025 财年和歌山县首剂 疫苗接种 覆盖率,重点关注 2024 年至 2025 年之间的特定年级差异。
核心方法
特定年龄的首次剂量计数是从 2020-2021 财年的 e-Stat 和 2022-2025 财年的和歌山县政府获得的。使用已发布的方法按出生财政年度重建 疫苗接种 计数和人口数据。分母是 2020 年人口普查中特定年龄的女性人口。
主要结果
2024年至2025财年,小学六年级学生的年覆盖率从3.8%变为4.1%,初一年级学生的年覆盖率从20.6%变为15.4%,初二学生的年覆盖率从2.2%变为7.7%,初三学生的年覆盖率从30.5%变为16.4%,初中生的年覆盖率从13.1%变为6.1%。高中一年级学生。2008 年和 2009 年出生的 队列研究 到高中一年级结束时的累计覆盖率分别为 46.5% 和 49.9%。覆盖率最高的年级从高中一年级转移到初三。
解释与创新
在 2020-2025 财年期间,首剂 疫苗接种 的主要时间转向较年轻的年级。然而,2025年初中一年级、三年级和高中一年级学生的年度覆盖率低于2024年。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息,以提高年轻学生中的 HPV 疫苗接种 率,并强调需要持续监测不同年级的 疫苗接种 趋势。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于汇总的行政数据,可能缺乏详细的个人信息。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于描述性流行病学方法,没有进一步的分析或验证。

Trends in Routine First-Dose Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Coverage in Wakayama Prefecture, Japan, Fiscal Years 2020–2025

Authors: Nobuhiko Suzuki, Kosuke Hiramatsu, Shinichiro Takeda, Motoko Kanno, Sayaka Ikeda, Asami Yagi, Naoki Mizunuma, Keiko Yamada, Masahiko Sakaguchi, Kayoko Katayama
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background In Japan, proactive recommendations for human papillomavirus vaccination were suspended in 2013 and resumed in fiscal year 2022 alongside a catch-up program, whose first-dose eligibility ended in fiscal year 2024. Coverage among subsequent routine-vaccination cohorts remains unclear. We described first-dose vaccination coverage in Wakayama Prefecture during fiscal years 2020–2025, focusing on grade-specific differences between 2024 and 2025. Methods This descriptive epidemiological study used aggregated administrative data. Age-specific first-dose counts were obtained from e-Stat for fiscal years 2020–2021 and the Wakayama Prefectural Government for 2022–2025. Denominators were age-specific female populations from the 2020 Population Census. Vaccination counts and population data were reconstructed by birth fiscal year using a published method. Annual and cumulative coverage were estimated for the 2004–2013 birth cohorts. Results Between fiscal years 2024 and 2025, annual coverage changed from 3.8% to 4.1% in sixth-grade elementary school students, 20.6% to 15.4% in first-year junior high school students, 2.2% to 7.7% in second-year junior high school students, 30.5% to 16.4% in third-year junior high school students, and 13.1% to 6.1% in first-year high school students. Cumulative coverage by the end of first-year high school was 46.5% and 49.9% for the 2008 and 2009 birth cohorts, respectively. The highest-coverage grade shifted from first-year high school to third-year junior high school. Conclusions The predominant timing of first-dose vaccination shifted toward younger grades during fiscal years 2020–2025. However, annual coverage in 2025 was lower than in 2024 among first- and third-year junior high school students and first-year high school students.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In Japan, proactive recommendations for human papillomavirus vaccination were suspended in 2013 and resumed in fiscal year 2022 alongside a catch-up program, whose first-dose eligibility ended in fiscal year 2024. Coverage among subsequent routine-vaccination cohorts remains unclear.
Design and population
This descriptive epidemiological study used aggregated administrative data to describe first-dose vaccination coverage in Wakayama Prefecture during fiscal years 2020–2025, focusing on grade-specific differences between 2024 and 2025.
Core methods
Age-specific first-dose counts were obtained from e-Stat for fiscal years 2020–2021 and the Wakayama Prefectural Government for 2022–2025. Vaccination counts and population data were reconstructed by birth fiscal year using a published method. Denominators were age-specific female populations from the 2020 Population Census.
Main results
Between fiscal years 2024 and 2025, annual coverage changed from 3.8% to 4.1% in sixth-grade elementary school students, 20.6% to 15.4% in first-year junior high school students, 2.2% to 7.7% in second-year junior high school students, 30.5% to 16.4% in third-year junior high school students, and 13.1% to 6.1% in first-year high school students. Cumulative coverage by the end of first-year high school was 46.5% and 49.9% for the 2008 and 2009 birth cohorts, respectively. The highest-coverage grade shifted from first-year high school to third-year junior high school.
Interpretation and novelty
The predominant timing of first-dose vaccination shifted toward younger grades during fiscal years 2020–2025. However, annual coverage in 2025 was lower than in 2024 among first- and third-year junior high school students and first-year high school students.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform targeted public health interventions to improve HPV vaccination rates among younger students and highlight the need for ongoing monitoring of vaccination trends in different grade levels.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on aggregated administrative data, which may lack detailed individual-level information. The evidence is moderate as it relies on descriptive epidemiological methods without further analysis or validation.

人乳头瘤病毒 伊拉克乳腺癌女性中 16 型和 18 型感染与 IL-38 基因表达的关联

HUMAN PAPILLOMAVIRUS TYPE 16 AND 18 INFECTION ASSOCIATION WITH IL-38 GENE EXPRESSION IN IRAQI WOMEN WITH BREAST CANCER
作者： Ruqaya Husam Abdulqader, Layla Fouad Ali
摘要中文翻译简介:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 与多种恶性肿瘤的 发病机制 有关,其在乳腺癌中的可能作用仍在研究中。本研究旨在探讨HPV感染与乳腺癌之间的关系,并评估其对白细胞介素38(IL-38)表达的影响。方法:研究人群包括 40 名患者和 39 名对照参与者。通过 ELISA 技术进行 HPV 检测,然后通过 PCR 进行确认,同时使用管家基因标准化在 7 名患者的子集中进行 IL-38 的基因表达分析。结果:ELISA结果显示患者中无阳性病例,而对照样本中有5例呈阳性。相比之下,对 10 名患者和 3 名对照者进行 PCR 分析,结果显示,其中 8 名患者样本的 HPV 18 型和 16 型均呈阳性,而对照者的一份样本仅 HPV-16 呈阳性,另一份样本的 HPV-16 和 -18 均呈阳性。基因表达分析显示,与对照组相比,患者中 IL-38 的表达发生了变化,大多数样本显示倍数变化增加。讨论:总体而言,结果与可能先前暴露或潜伏 HPV 感染的假设一致,并表明 治疗 相关的免疫抑制可能值得进一步研究。IL-38 表达失调可能提示 HPV 相关乳腺癌中存在 免疫应答。为了充分了解 HPV 感染、细胞因子调节和乳腺癌进展之间的分子关系,需要进一步研究更大的样本量。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 与多种恶性肿瘤的 发病机制 有关,其在乳腺癌中的可能作用仍在研究中。
设计与对象
本研究旨在探讨HPV感染与乳腺癌之间的关系,并评估其对白细胞介素38(IL-38)表达的影响。研究人群包括 40 名患者和 39 名对照参与者。
核心方法
通过 ELISA 技术进行 HPV 检测,然后通过 PCR 进行确认,同时使用管家基因标准化在 7 名患者的子集中进行 IL-38 的基因表达分析。对 10 名患者和 3 名对照进行 PCR 分析。
主要结果
ELISA结果显示患者中没有阳性病例,而五个对照样本呈阳性。PCR 分析显示,8 名患者样本的 HPV 18 型和 16 型均呈阳性,而对照样本中的 1 份仅 HPV-16 呈阳性,另一份样本的 HPV-16 和 -18 均呈阳性。基因表达分析显示,与对照组相比,患者中 IL-38 的表达发生了变化,大多数样本显示倍数变化增加。
解释与创新
总体而言,结果与可能先前暴露或潜伏 HPV 感染的假设一致,并表明 治疗 相关的免疫抑制可能值得进一步研究。IL-38 表达失调可能提示 HPV 相关乳腺癌中存在 免疫应答。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,HPV 感染与乳腺癌中的 免疫应答 改变之间存在潜在联系,这可能为未来有关 HPV 相关恶性肿瘤免疫机制的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究结果的普遍性或证据的强度。该研究样本量小、范围有限,可能会影响结果的可靠性。

HUMAN PAPILLOMAVIRUS TYPE 16 AND 18 INFECTION ASSOCIATION WITH IL-38 GENE EXPRESSION IN IRAQI WOMEN WITH BREAST CANCER

Authors: Ruqaya Husam Abdulqader, Layla Fouad Ali
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is implicated in the pathogenesis of several malignancies, and its possible role in breast cancer remains under investigation. This study aimed to explore the relationship between HPV infection and breast cancer and to assess its effect on the expression of interleukin-38 (IL-38). Methodology: The study population consisted of 40 patients and 39 control participants. HPV detection was performed by ELISA technique, followed by PCR for confirmation, while gene expression analysis of IL-38 was carried out in a subset of seven patients using housekeeping gene normalization. Results: ELISA results showed no positive cases among patients, whereas five control samples were positive. In contrast, PCR analysis was performed on 10 patients and 3 controls, the results showed that 8 of the patient samples were positive for both HPV types 18 and 16, while the controls had one positive for only HPV-16 and another sample was positive for both HPV-16 and -18. Gene expression analysis revealed altered expression of IL-38 among patients compared with controls, with most samples showing increased fold change. Discussion: Overall, the results are consistent with the hypothesis of possible prior exposure or latent HPV infection and suggest that treatment-related immune suppression may warrant further investigation. Dysregulation of IL-38 expression may suggest an immune response in HPV-associated breast cancer. Further investigation into a larger sample size is necessary to fully understand the molecular relationship between HPV infection, cytokine regulation and breast cancer progression.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is implicated in the pathogenesis of several malignancies, and its possible role in breast cancer remains under investigation.
Design and population
This study aimed to explore the relationship between HPV infection and breast cancer and to assess its effect on the expression of interleukin-38 (IL-38). The study population consisted of 40 patients and 39 control participants.
Core methods
HPV detection was performed by ELISA technique, followed by PCR for confirmation, while gene expression analysis of IL-38 was carried out in a subset of seven patients using housekeeping gene normalization. PCR analysis was performed on 10 patients and 3 controls.
Main results
ELISA results showed no positive cases among patients, whereas five control samples were positive. PCR analysis revealed that 8 of the patient samples were positive for both HPV types 18 and 16, while the controls had one positive for only HPV-16 and another sample was positive for both HPV-16 and -18. Gene expression analysis revealed altered expression of IL-38 among patients compared with controls, with most samples showing increased fold change.
Interpretation and novelty
Overall, the results are consistent with the hypothesis of possible prior exposure or latent HPV infection and suggest that treatment-related immune suppression may warrant further investigation. Dysregulation of IL-38 expression may suggest an immune response in HPV-associated breast cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest a potential link between HPV infection and immune response alterations in breast cancer, which may inform future research on immune mechanisms in HPV-associated malignancies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the generalisability of the findings or the strength of the evidence. The study has a small sample size and limited scope, which may affect the reliability of the results.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

美国中西部大学自我报告人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种中的性别差异和追赶机会

Sex disparities and catch-up opportunities in self-reported human papillomavirus vaccination at a Midwestern University in the United States
作者： Minjee Lee, Chengchia B. Chen, Mary A. Gerend, Meng-Han Tsai, Jorge E. Villegas
摘要中文翻译目的 评估本科生自我报告的 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 启动和校园追赶机会的性别差异。方法 我们分析了 2023 年 2 月 23 日至 3 月 13 日从美国中西部一所公立大学 18 至 26 岁本科生收集的基线调查数据。主要分析包括 115 名已知 疫苗接种 状态的学生;排除了 50 名身份不明的人。修正泊松回归估计调整后的 患病率 比率。敏感性分析将不确定的反应指定为未接种疫苗或接种疫苗。结果 在已知状况的学生中,115 名学生中有 68 名 (59.1%) 报告开始学习。男学生的调整起始 患病率 低于女学生(调整后 患病率 比率,0.50;95% 置信区间,0.31–0.79)。当所有不确定的反应被指定为未接种疫苗(0.36;0.21-0.61)或接种疫苗(0.80;0.64-0.99)时,估计值仍低于 1.00。在 47 名报告没有 疫苗接种 的学生中,57.4% 的学生表示假设愿意遵循医疗保健专业建议,而 48.9% 的学生表示愿意在校园内提供 疫苗接种;50名学生中,身份不明的相应比例分别为62.0%和52.0%。结论 在各种敏感性情景中,男大学生自我报告的开始程度较低。推荐和校园交付的记录验证和前瞻性评估是有必要的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估本科生自我报告的 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 启动和校园追赶机会中的性别差异。
设计与对象
该研究分析了2023年2月23日至3月13日从美国中西部一所公立大学收集的18-26岁本科生的基线调查数据。主要分析包括115名已知疫苗接种状态的学生,排除了50名状态不确定的学生。
核心方法
使用改进的泊松回归来估计调整后的 患病率 比率,并通过将不确定的响应分配为未接种疫苗或接种疫苗来进行敏感性分析。
主要结果
在已知状况的学生中,115 名学生中的 68 名 (59.1%) 报告了启蒙。男学生的调整起始 患病率 低于女学生(调整后 患病率 比率,0.50;95% 置信区间,0.31–0.79)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,在不同敏感情况下,男性本科生自我报告的感染起始率较低,这突显了有关 HPV 疫苗接种 性别差异的新发现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要采取有针对性的干预措施来解决 HPV 疫苗接种 中的性别差异,并为基于校园的追赶计划提供了机会。
局限与证据强度
该研究因依赖自我报告的数据而受到限制,由于潜在的偏见和排除了不确定 疫苗接种 状态的学生,证据强度中等。

Sex disparities and catch-up opportunities in self-reported human papillomavirus vaccination at a Midwestern University in the United States

Authors: Minjee Lee, Chengchia B. Chen, Mary A. Gerend, Meng-Han Tsai, Jorge E. Villegas
Original abstract (English)
Objective To assess sex disparities in self-reported human papillomavirus vaccination initiation and campus catch-up opportunities among undergraduates. Methods We analyzed baseline survey data collected February 23–March 13, 2023, from undergraduates aged 18–26 years at a public Midwestern university in the United States. The primary analysis included 115 students with known vaccination status; 50 with uncertain status were excluded. Modified Poisson regression estimated adjusted prevalence ratios. Sensitivity analyses assigned uncertain responses as unvaccinated or vaccinated. Results Among students with known status, 68 of 115 (59.1%) reported initiation. Male students had lower adjusted initiation prevalence than female students (adjusted prevalence ratio, 0.50; 95% confidence interval, 0.31–0.79). Estimates remained below 1.00 when all uncertain responses were assigned as unvaccinated (0.36; 0.21–0.61) or vaccinated (0.80; 0.64–0.99). Of 47 students reporting no vaccination, 57.4% expressed hypothetical willingness following healthcare professional advice and 48.9% if vaccination were offered on campus; corresponding proportions among 50 students with uncertain status were 62.0% and 52.0%. Conclusions Self-reported initiation was lower among male undergraduates across sensitivity scenarios. Record verification and prospective evaluation of recommendation and campus delivery are warranted.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess sex disparities in self-reported human papillomavirus vaccination initiation and campus catch-up opportunities among undergraduates.
Design and population
The study analyzed baseline survey data collected from undergraduates aged 18–26 years at a public Midwestern university in the United States between February 23 and March 13, 2023. The primary analysis included 115 students with known vaccination status, while 50 with uncertain status were excluded.
Core methods
Modified Poisson regression was used to estimate adjusted prevalence ratios, and sensitivity analyses were conducted by assigning uncertain responses as either unvaccinated or vaccinated.
Main results
Among students with known status, 68 of 115 (59.1%) reported initiation. Male students had lower adjusted initiation prevalence than female students (adjusted prevalence ratio, 0.50; 95% confidence interval, 0.31–0.79).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that self-reported initiation was lower among male undergraduates across sensitivity scenarios, highlighting a novel finding on sex disparities in HPV vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted interventions to address sex disparities in HPV vaccination and suggest opportunities for campus-based catch-up programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by reliance on self-reported data, and the evidence strength is moderate due to potential biases and the exclusion of students with uncertain vaccination status.

在埃塞俄比亚接受 宫颈癌 筛查的女性中,经典性传播感染和生殖器支原体检测与高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 和宫颈病变的关联

Association of classical sexually transmitted infections and genital mycoplasmas detection with high-risk human papillomavirus and cervical lesions among women undergoing cervical cancer screening in Ethiopia
作者： Habtamu Biazin Kebede, Selamawit Fisseha Mekuria, Nahom Assegid, Ola Forslund, Christer Borgfeldt, Mats Jerkeman, Adane Mihret, Tamrat Abebe
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查埃塞俄比亚接受 宫颈癌 筛查的女性中典型性传播感染 (STI) 和生殖器支原体与高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 感染和宫颈病变之间的关联。
设计与对象
该研究在 885 名接受 宫颈癌 筛查的埃塞俄比亚女性中进行,重点关注 hrHPV 感染、宫颈病变以及性传播感染和生殖器支原体对诊断结果的影响。
核心方法
使用实时 PCR 对阴道自体样本进行 14 种 hrHPV 类型和经典 STI 检测,并通过卡方检验和逻辑回归分析(包括针对组织学结果的 Firth 惩罚多变量逻辑回归)评估关联性。
主要结果
64% 的参与者检测到生殖器支原体(95% CI:60.7–67.1),而 4.3% 的参与者检测到经典性传播感染(95% CI:3.1–5.8)。高危型HPV 感染与生殖器支原体检测、沙眼衣原体感染、人型支原体检测和 HIV 感染相关。CIN2+病变与hrHPV和HIV感染相关,但与经典性传播感染或生殖器支原体无关。STI 和生殖器支原体检测与 VIA/VILI 结果无关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,hrHPV 和 HIV 感染与宫颈病变相关,而 STI 和生殖器支原体与 VIA/VILI 结果无关,强调需要进一步研究这些感染在 宫颈癌 进展中的作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在 宫颈癌 预防策略中应考虑 HPV-HIV 双重感染,特别是在 HPV 患病率 高且筛查资源有限的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对自我报告样本和仅摘要数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差。由于该研究的观察性质且缺乏详细的随访,证据强度中等。

Association of classical sexually transmitted infections and genital mycoplasmas detection with high-risk human papillomavirus and cervical lesions among women undergoing cervical cancer screening in Ethiopia

Authors: Habtamu Biazin Kebede, Selamawit Fisseha Mekuria, Nahom Assegid, Ola Forslund, Christer Borgfeldt, Mats Jerkeman, Adane Mihret, Tamrat Abebe
Original abstract (English)
Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infection is the necessary cause of cervical cancer. A small proportion of HPV infections may progress to high-grade cervical lesions or cancer. Genital mycoplasma and sexually transmitted infections (STIs) may be associated with HPV persistence and progression. These detections or infections may also reduce the diagnostic accuracy of visual inspection-based screening by increasing local inflammation. In Ethiopia, there is still limited evidence on the association of STIs and genital mycoplasmas with hrHPV, cervical lesions, and visual inspection with acetic acid or Lugol’s iodine (VILI/VILI). This study assessed the prevalence of certain classical STIs and genital mycoplasma, their association with hrHPV infection, cervical lesions, and VIA/VILI outcomes among women undergoing HPV-based cervical cancer screening. Vaginal self-samples from 885 Ethiopian women were tested for 14 hrHPV types, C. trachomatis (CT), N. gonorrhoeae (NG), T. vaginalis (TV), and four genital mycoplasmas using real-time PCR. One hundred and fifty-six women who tested positive for hrHPV underwent VIA/VILI, and 116 women underwent histology examinations. Frequencies and percentages were used to summarize descriptive statistics of categorical variables. The prevalence estimates were provided with 95% confidence intervals (CIs), using the Wilson score method. Associations were assessed by the chi-square test and logistic regression analysis between the independent and outcome variables. A Firth-penalized multivariable logistic regression analysis was also used for histological results and independent variables. Among the study participants, 568 women (64.2%) had at least one sexually transmitted microbe (STM) (95% CI: 61–67). Classical STIs were detected in 4.3% (95% CI: 3.1–5.8), and genital mycoplasmas in 64% (95% CI: 60.7–67.1) of participants. parvum was the most frequently detected species. High-risk HPV infection was associated with genital mycoplasma detection (AOR = 1.7, 95% CI: 1.3–2.3, p = 0.001), C. trachomatis infection (AOR = 5.4, 95% CI: 1.4–21, p = 0.014), M. hominis detection (AOR = 2.0, 95% CI: 1.4-3.0, p = 0.001), and HIV infection (AOR = 3.2, 95% CI: 1.8–5.5, p < 0.001). CIN2 + lesions were associated with both hrHPV (AOR = 6.77, 95% CI: 1.2–75.4, P = 0.03) and HIV infection (AOR = 8.92, 95% CI: 1.5–72.2, P = 0.02). Neither STIs nor genital mycoplasma detection were associated with VIA/VILI outcomes. Classical STIs were low among the women included in the study. However, one out of five women were infected with hrHPV, and 64% of the women were harbored genital mycoplasma. HrHPV infection was associated with genital mycoplasma detection, C. trachomatis infection , M. hominis detection, and HIV infection. HrHPV and HIV infections were associated with CIN2 + lesions, whereas classical STIs and genital mycoplasma were not associated with cervical abnormalities. There was no association between STIs or the detection of genital mycoplasma and the outcomes of VIA/VILI. Overall, this study emphasizes the importance of considering HPV–HIV co-infections in cervical cancer prevention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the association between classical sexually transmitted infections (STIs) and genital mycoplasmas with high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) infection and cervical lesions in women undergoing cervical cancer screening in Ethiopia.
Design and population
The study was conducted among 885 Ethiopian women undergoing cervical cancer screening, with a focus on hrHPV infection, cervical lesions, and the impact of STIs and genital mycoplasmas on diagnostic outcomes.
Core methods
Vaginal self-samples were tested for 14 hrHPV types and classical STIs using real-time PCR, and associations were assessed with chi-square tests and logistic regression analyses, including Firth-penalized multivariable logistic regression for histological results.
Main results
Genital mycoplasmas were detected in 64% of participants (95% CI: 60.7–67.1), while classical STIs were detected in 4.3% (95% CI: 3.1–5.8). High-risk HPV infection was associated with genital mycoplasma detection, C. trachomatis infection, M. hominis detection, and HIV infection. CIN2+ lesions were associated with hrHPV and HIV infection, but not with classical STIs or genital mycoplasmas. STIs and genital mycoplasma detection were not associated with VIA/VILI outcomes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that hrHPV and HIV infections are associated with cervical lesions, while STIs and genital mycoplasmas are not linked to VIA/VILI outcomes, highlighting the need for further investigation into the role of these infections in cervical cancer progression.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that HPV–HIV co-infections should be considered in cervical cancer prevention strategies, particularly in regions with high HPV prevalence and limited screening resources.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported samples and abstract-only data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and the lack of detailed follow-up.

852 ABS005,一种新型水凝胶,用于治疗治疗高级别宫颈上皮内瘤变

852 ABS005, a novel hydrogel, for treatment of high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia
作者： Morgan G. Stykel, Gil Covarrubias, Rebekah Garfolo, Daniel Pierson, Avik Som, David H. Altreuter, Stephen W. Linderman
摘要中文翻译背景 宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN 2/3) 的治疗依赖于切除或消融手术。1 这些干预措施虽然有效,但与宫颈狭窄有关,2 会增加早产和流产率,3-5 并且不能解决潜在的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染,从而导致持续存在和复发。Absco Therapeutics 开发了 ABS005,一种用于 CIN2/3 的 治疗。ABS005 含有咪喹莫特,这是一种用于 CIN2/3 标签外治疗的 TLR7 激动剂,对病变和 HPV 的疗效均有限。6 然而,局部制剂因保留性和冲洗性差而受到限制。7 ABS005 旨在用于直接宫颈内注射,形成持续、靶向咪喹莫特递送的储库。ABS005 旨在消除 CIN2/3 病变并教育免疫系统抵抗 HPV 感染。这种方法可以保留宫颈完整性和未来的产科结果,为女性提供非手术 治疗 选择。方法首先对 ABS005 的水凝胶特性和体外释放曲线进行了表征。为了评估体内功效,在 宫颈癌 小鼠模型中评估了单个和多个 ABS005 治疗,其中 HPV 阳性 Tal3 肿瘤细胞接种到 C57BL/6 小鼠胁腹中。8 9 流式细胞术表征肿瘤内反应,分析抗原呈递细胞(CD80+ 和 86+)、树突状细胞 (CD11c+)、抗肿瘤巨噬细胞(F4/80+)、细胞毒性 T 细胞 (CD4+/CD8+) 和 NK 细胞群 (CD45+/CD3-/CD49b+)。通过比较瘤内和全身药物药代动力学特征来确定局部治疗与全身治疗的比率。最后,我们对犬类宫颈内注射后 ABS005 的毒代动力学特征进行了表征。结果 我们配制了一种聚合物,该聚合物在 34°C (±2°C) 下固化成凝胶,并在体外提供超过 48 小时的持续药物释放,在体内提供至少一周的持续药物释放。在小鼠模型中,ABS005 的单个和多个 治疗 均成功阻止了肿瘤生长(图 1),并刺激了与咪喹莫特作用机制一致的瘤内免疫特征。药代动力学分析显示异常的局部滞留,瘤内咪喹莫特浓度超过全身水平 20,000 倍(图 2)。此外,犬宫颈内注射表明 ABS005 具有良好的耐受性,在 治疗 后 30 分钟时血浆 Cmax 峰值为 19.4 ng/mL。结论这些数据表明 ABS005 可以提供咪喹莫特的持续、受控释放,以治疗 HPV 驱动的发育不良,同时保持良好的安全性。下一步包括 GMP 级生产、GLP 毒理学以及 FDA 对新药研究申请的批准。致谢 我们感谢 Giovanni Traverso 教授(麻省理工学院,Absco Therapeutics)和 Avik Som 教授(华盛顿大学,Absco Therapeutics)进行的科学讨论。最初的聚合物输送技术是由麻省理工学院 Traverso 实验室和麻省总医院布里格姆分校开发的。参考世界卫生组织。WHO 癌前病变筛查和 治疗 预防指南。世界卫生组织;2021.Aagaard M、Rogvi JA、Modin F、Nicolaisdóttir D、Siersma V、Brandt Brodersen J。丹麦锥切术的短期并发症以及对生育相关结果的长期影响:基于登记的全国 队列研究 64844)进行。摘要 852 图 1ABS005 成功阻止 Tal3 肿瘤生长 摘要 852 图 2 瘤内和全身咪喹莫特浓度的药代动力学分析
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN 2/3) 的治疗依赖于切除或消融手术,这些手术与宫颈狭窄、早产和流产率增加等并发症相关,并且不能解决潜在的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染,从而导致持续存在和复发。
设计与对象
该研究评估了 ABS005 在 宫颈癌 小鼠模型中的体内功效,其中 HPV 阳性 Tal3 肿瘤细胞被接种到 C57BL/6 小鼠胁腹中。它还表征了犬科动物宫颈内注射后 ABS005 的毒代动力学特征。
核心方法
首先对 ABS005 的水凝胶特性和体外释放曲线进行了表征。为了评估体内功效,在 宫颈癌 的小鼠模型中评估了单个和多个 ABS005 治疗,并进行药代动力学分析以比较瘤内和全身药物分布。
主要结果
ABS005 经配制可在 34°C (±2°C) 下固化成凝胶,并在体外提供超过 48 小时的持续药物释放,在体内提供至少一周的持续药物释放。在小鼠模型中,ABS005 的单个和多个 治疗 均成功阻止了肿瘤生长,并刺激了与咪喹莫特作用机制一致的瘤内免疫特征。药代动力学分析显示,咪喹莫特具有出色的局部滞留性,瘤内浓度超过全身水平 20,000 倍。
解释与创新
这些数据表明 ABS005 可以提供咪喹莫特的持续、受控释放,以治疗 HPV 驱动的发育不良,同时保持良好的安全性。这种方法可以保留宫颈完整性和未来的产科结果,为女性提供非手术 治疗 选择。
科研与公卫意义
它可以为未来关于 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 局部药物输送的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型和犬毒代动力学分析,可能无法完全代表人类生理学。证据是中等的,因为这些发现是基于体外和动物研究,没有进行人体临床试验。

852 ABS005, a novel hydrogel, for treatment of high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia

Authors: Morgan G. Stykel, Gil Covarrubias, Rebekah Garfolo, Daniel Pierson, Avik Som, David H. Altreuter, Stephen W. Linderman
Original abstract (English)
Background Cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN 2/3) management relies on excisional or ablative procedures. 1 While effective, these interventions are associated with cervical stenosis,2 increased rates of preterm birth and miscarriage,3–5 and do not address the underlying human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, leading to persistence and recurrence. Absco Therapeutics has developed ABS005, a treatment for CIN2/3. ABS005 incorporates imiquimod, a TLR7 agonist used off-label for CIN2/3 with modest efficacy against both the lesion and HPV.6 However, topical formulations are limited by poor retention and washout.7 ABS005 is intended for direct intracervical injection, forming a depot for sustained, on-target imiquimod delivery. ABS005 is designed to eliminate CIN2/3 lesions and educate the immune system against HPV infection. This approach may preserve cervical integrity and future obstetric outcomes, providing women non-surgical treatment options.Methods The hydrogel properties and in vitro release profile of ABS005 were first characterized. To evaluate in vivo efficacy, single and multiple ABS005 treatments were assessed in a murine model of cervical cancer where HPV-positive Tal3 tumor cells are inoculated into C57BL/6 mouse flanks.8 9 Flow cytometry characterized the intratumoral response, profiling antigen presenting cells (CD80+ and 86+), dendritic cells (CD11c+), anti-tumor macrophage (F4/80+), cytotoxic T-cell (CD4+/CD8+) and NK-cell populations (CD45+/CD3-/CD49b+). Local-to-systemic therapeutic ratios were defined by comparing intratumoral and systemic drug pharmacokinetic profiles. Finally, we characterized the toxicokinetic profile of ABS005 following intracervical injection in canines.Results We formulated a polymer that solidifies into a gel at 34°C (±2°C) and provides sustained drug release over 48 hours in vitro and at least one week in vivo. In the murine model, both single and multiple treatments of ABS005 successfully halted tumor growth (figure 1) and stimulated an intratumoral immune profile consistent with imiquimod’s mechanism of action. Pharmacokinetic analysis revealed exceptional local retention, with intratumoral imiquimod concentrations exceeding systemic levels by 20,000-fold (figure 2). Furthermore, canine intracervical injections demonstrated that ABS005 was well tolerated, with a peak plasma Cmax of 19.4 ng/mL at 30 minutes post-treatment.Conclusions These data suggest that ABS005 can provide a sustained, controlled release of imiquimod to treat HPV-driven dysplasia while maintaining a favorable safety profile. Next steps include GMP-grade manufacturing, GLP toxicology, and FDA clearance of an investigational new drug application.Acknowledgements We appreciate Prof. Giovanni Traverso (MIT, Absco Therapeutics) and Prof. Avik Som (U Washington, Absco Therapeutics) for scientific discussions. The initial polymer delivery technology was developed at MIT Traverso Lab and Mass General Brigham.References World Health Organization. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN 2/3) management relies on excisional or ablative procedures, which are associated with complications such as cervical stenosis, increased preterm birth and miscarriage rates, and do not address the underlying human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, leading to persistence and recurrence.
Design and population
The study evaluated the in vivo efficacy of ABS005 in a murine model of cervical cancer where HPV-positive Tal3 tumor cells are inoculated into C57BL/6 mouse flanks. It also characterized the toxicokinetic profile of ABS005 following intracervical injection in canines.
Core methods
The hydrogel properties and in vitro release profile of ABS005 were first characterized. To evaluate in vivo efficacy, single and multiple ABS005 treatments were assessed in a murine model of cervical cancer, and pharmacokinetic analysis was performed to compare intratumoral and systemic drug profiles.
Main results
ABS005 was formulated to solidify into a gel at 34°C (±2°C) and provide sustained drug release over 48 hours in vitro and at least one week in vivo. In the murine model, both single and multiple treatments of ABS005 successfully halted tumor growth and stimulated an intratumoral immune profile consistent with imiquimod’s mechanism of action. Pharmacokinetic analysis revealed exceptional local retention, with intratumoral imiquimod concentrations exceeding systemic levels by 20,000-fold.
Interpretation and novelty
These data suggest that ABS005 can provide a sustained, controlled release of imiquimod to treat HPV-driven dysplasia while maintaining a favorable safety profile. This approach may preserve cervical integrity and future obstetric outcomes, providing women non-surgical treatment options.
Scientific and public-health significance
It could inform future studies on localized drug delivery for HPV-associated cervical cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a murine model and canine toxicokinetic analysis, which may not fully represent human physiology. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on in vitro and animal studies without human clinical trials.

浆细胞样树突状细胞和 M2 巨噬细胞与 人乳头瘤病毒 独立外阴鳞状细胞癌的临床结果相关

Plasmacytoid dendritic cells and M2 macrophages are associated with clinical outcomes in human papillomavirus–independent vulvar squamous cell carcinoma
作者： Núria Peñuelas, Lia Sisuashvili, Lorena Marimón, Laia Díez-Ahijado, Núria Carreras-Dieguez, Clement Gonzalez-Serra, Katarzyna Darecka, Juan Muñoz-Hurtado, Adela Saco, Marta del Pino
摘要中文翻译背景 外阴鳞状细胞癌 (VSCC) 是一种罕见的恶性肿瘤,包括 HPV 相关亚型 (HPV-A) 和 HPV 独立亚型 (HPV-I)。HPV-I 与高复发率和较差的生存率相关,但缺乏经过验证的预后生物标志物和治疗分层工具。尽管免疫疗法已经改变了癌症治疗,并强调肿瘤微环境 (TME) 作为临床结果的重要决定因素,但 VSCC 的罕见性限制了其分子特征,而且对 TME 的了解仍然很少。因此,我们的目标是在 HPV 特异性背景下定义 VSCC TME,并确定与临床结果相关的免疫特征。方法 我们使用靶向转录组分析 (HTG EdgeSeq) 分析了 70 例手术治疗的 VSCC(18 例 HPV-A 和 52 例 HPV-I)。免疫和基质细胞组成是通过计算解卷积推断的,主要使用 xCell,并得到 EPIC、quanTIseq 和 MCPcounter 的支持。TME 组成通过 HPV 状态进行比较,在 HPV-I VSCC 内,通过 p53 和 PD-L1 免疫组织化学状态进行比较。使用根据FIGO分期和p53状态调整的Firth惩罚Cox回归评估与无复发和疾病特异性生存的关联,并通过CD123和CD163免疫组织化学评估候选人群。结果 HPV-A 肿瘤表现出较高的免疫和整体微环境评分,并且富集不同的淋巴和骨髓细胞群,而 HPV-I 肿瘤表现出较低的免疫细胞富集。在 HPV-I VSCC 中,浆细胞样树突状细胞 (pDC) 富集在基于去卷积的分析中显示出与有利生存的最一致的关联,免疫组织化学证实 CD123 + 细胞浸润是一个独立的有利预后因素。PD-L1 阳性发生在 44% 的 HPV-I 肿瘤中,并与 CD163 + 巨噬细胞浸润相关,而 CD163 + 巨噬细胞浸润又与不良预后独立相关。结论 TME 分析确定了 HPV-I VSCC 内的两个临床相关免疫群体:CD123 + 细胞浸润与良好结果相关,而 CD163 + 巨噬细胞浸润与 PD-L1 阳性和较差结果相关。这些发现为进一步研究 TME 作为预后和潜在治疗生物标志物的来源提供了理论基础。在确定临床实用性之前,需要在更大、独立的 队列研究 中进行验证。

Plasmacytoid dendritic cells and M2 macrophages are associated with clinical outcomes in human papillomavirus–independent vulvar squamous cell carcinoma

Authors: Núria Peñuelas, Lia Sisuashvili, Lorena Marimón, Laia Díez-Ahijado, Núria Carreras-Dieguez, Clement Gonzalez-Serra, Katarzyna Darecka, Juan Muñoz-Hurtado, Adela Saco, Marta del Pino
Original abstract (English)
Background Vulvar squamous cell carcinoma (VSCC) is a rare malignancy comprising HPV-associated (HPV-A) and HPV-independent (HPV-I) subtypes. HPV-I is associated with high recurrence rates and poor survival, yet lacks validated prognostic biomarkers and therapeutic stratification tools. Although immunotherapy has transformed cancer care and highlighted the tumor microenvironment (TME) as an important determinant of clinical outcome, the rarity of VSCC has limited its molecular characterization, and the TME remains poorly understood. We therefore aimed to define the VSCC TME in an HPV-specific context and identify immune features associated with clinical outcomes. Methods We analyzed 70 surgically treated VSCC (18 HPV-A and 52 HPV-I) using targeted transcriptomic profiling (HTG EdgeSeq). Immune and stromal cell composition was inferred by computational deconvolution, primarily with xCell and supported by EPIC, quanTIseq and MCPcounter. TME composition was compared by HPV status and, within HPV-I VSCC, by p53 and PD-L1 immunohistochemical status. Associations with recurrence-free and disease-specific survival were assessed using Firth-penalized Cox regression adjusted for FIGO stage and p53 status, and candidate populations were evaluated by CD123 and CD163 immunohistochemistry. Results HPV-A tumors showed higher immune and overall microenvironment scores, with enrichment of diverse lymphoid and myeloid populations, whereas HPV-I tumors showed lower immune-cell enrichment. Within HPV-I VSCC, plasmacytoid dendritic cell (pDC) enrichment showed the most consistent association with favorable survival across deconvolution-based analyses, with immunohistochemistry confirming CD123 + cell infiltration as an independent favorable prognostic factor. PD-L1 positivity occurred in 44% of HPV-I tumors and was associated with CD163 + macrophage infiltration, which, in turn, was independently associated with poor outcomes. Conclusions TME profiling identified two clinically relevant immune populations within HPV-I VSCC: CD123 + cell infiltration was associated with favorable outcomes, whereas CD163 + macrophage infiltration was associated with PD-L1 positivity and poorer outcomes. These findings provide a rationale for further investigation of the TME as a source of prognostic and potentially therapeutic biomarkers. Validation in larger, independent cohorts is required before clinical utility can be established.

人脐带间充质干细胞来源的小细胞外囊泡传递 CERS1 通过重塑鞘磷脂代谢促进 HPV16 阳性宫颈上皮内瘤变的鳞状细胞分化

Human umbilical cord mesenchymal stem cell-derived small extracellular vesicles delivering CERS1 promote squamous differentiation in HPV16-positive cervical intraepithelial neoplasia via remodeling sphingomyelin metabolism
作者： Huan Yang, Wei Chen, Lixiang Liu, Mengxiong Li, Wei Liao, Yunxia He, Ruohong Pan, Yaoyao Xu, Changlin Zhang, Tian Li
摘要中文翻译宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 是 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 驱动的 宫颈癌 的前兆,对女性健康构成严重威胁。高危型HPV 感染会导致 CI​​N 进展,但仍缺乏明确的干预目标和有效的非侵入性治疗。在此,我们使用新方法(NAM),特别是 HPV16 阳性宫颈外器官作为人类相关疾病模型,评估了源自人脐带间充质干细胞的工程化无细胞小细胞外囊泡(hucMSC-sEV)的治疗潜力。我们发现hucMSC-sEV显着促进S12细胞和HPV16阳性宫颈外器官的鳞状分化。从机制上讲,该 NAM 平台内的综合多组学分析表明,hucMSC-sEV 传递神经酰胺合酶 1 (CERS1) 上调 C18 神经酰胺 (C18 Cer) 合成,激活非经典 p53 信号传导以驱动鳞状细胞分化。在携带皮下 S12 细胞异种移植物的 NCG 小鼠中,hucMSC-sEV 有效抑制 S12 异种移植物生长,并通过调节 C18 神经酰胺代谢来调节鳞状细胞分化,而这种治疗作用在 CERS1 敲除的 hucMSC-sEV 中被消除。该研究阐明了hucMSC-sEV重塑鞘脂代谢促进宫颈鳞状上皮分化并抑制S12异种移植物发育的分子机制,为CIN干预提供新的潜在靶点和治疗策略。

Human umbilical cord mesenchymal stem cell-derived small extracellular vesicles delivering CERS1 promote squamous differentiation in HPV16-positive cervical intraepithelial neoplasia via remodeling sphingomyelin metabolism

Authors: Huan Yang, Wei Chen, Lixiang Liu, Mengxiong Li, Wei Liao, Yunxia He, Ruohong Pan, Yaoyao Xu, Changlin Zhang, Tian Li
Original abstract (English)
Cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN), a precursor of human papillomavirus (HPV)-driven cervical cancer, poses a severe threat to women’s health. High-risk HPV infection drives CIN progression, whereas well-defined intervention targets and effective non-invasive therapies remain lacking. Herein, Using New Approach Methodologies (NAMs), specifically HPV16-positive ectocervical organoids as a human-relevant disease model, we evaluated the therapeutic potential of engineered cell-free small extracellular vesicles derived from human umbilical cord mesenchymal stem cells (hucMSC-sEV). We found that hucMSC-sEV significantly promoted squamous differentiation in S12 cells and HPV16-positive ectocervical organoids. Mechanistically, integrated multi-omics analyses within this NAMs platform revealed that hucMSC-sEV delivered ceramide synthase 1 (CERS1) to upregulate C18 ceramide (C18 Cer) synthesis, activating the non-canonical p53 signaling to drive squamous differentiation. In NCG mice bearing subcutaneous xenografts of S12 cells, hucMSC-sEV effectively inhibited S12 xenograft growth and modulated squamous differentiation via regulation of C18 ceramide metabolism, while this therapeutic effect was abrogated in CERS1-knockdown hucMSC-sEV. This study delineates the molecular mechanism by which hucMSC-sEV remodel sphingolipid metabolism to promote cervical squamous epithelial differentiation and suppress S12 xenograft development, providing novel potential targets and therapeutic strategies for CIN intervention.
View research seven-element analysis

621 Pelarorep 和 atezolizumab 诱导的血液中 HPV-16 特异性 T 细胞的扩增与鳞状细胞肛门癌的肿瘤反应相关

621 Pelareorep and atezolizumab-induced expansion of HPV-16-specific T cells in the blood is associated with tumor responses in squamous cell anal cancer
作者： Richard J. Trauger, Thomas Charles Heineman, Hendrik Schürmann, Jens Thomas Siveke, Dirk Arnold
摘要中文翻译背景 肛门鳞状细胞癌 (SCAC) 是一种罕见疾病,约占胃肠道癌症的 2.6%。人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是 SCAC 的主要原因。≥2L SCAC 的 治疗 选项有限。检查点抑制剂(CPI)被推荐用于 1L 化疗失败的免疫治疗初治患者,但缓解率较低。溶瘤呼肠孤病毒 pelareorep (pela) 与 atezolizumab (atezo) 联合治疗在该人群中显示出 ≥2L 治疗 的前景。在接受 pela 和 atezo 治疗的胰腺癌患者中,肿瘤反应与血液中肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞 (TIL) 和 mRAS 克隆扩张相关。在这里,我们报告了 SCAC 肿瘤反应、TIL 浸润和血液中 HPV-16 特异性克隆扩增之间的关联。我们还报告了治疗后血浆分析物水平的变化,这些变化与检查点抑制剂治疗的反应相关。方法正在进行一项 1/2 期、Simon 2 阶段研究,以评估 pela 与 atezo 联合作为≥2L 疗法治疗不可切除 SCAC 患者的安全性和有效性 (NCT07280377)。患者每周接受 pela 4.5 x 1010 TCID50 静脉注射治疗,每两周接受 atezo 840 mg 治疗,周期为 28 天。通过 T 细胞受体测序(Adaptive Biotechnologies,西雅图,华盛顿州)获取 4 名患者的 治疗 前肿瘤,以鉴定 TIL,并分析这些患者的血液中 TIL 克隆从基线到 3 个周期的扩增情况。通过 T 细胞抗原特异性多重鉴定分析(MIRA 分析,Adaptive Biotechnologies)对 14 名患者的血液进行了 HPV-16 特异性克隆扩增分析。一个周期的治疗后,通过 Olink 测定法测量了 8 名患者的血浆分析物,包括 CXCL10、IFN-γ 和 sPD-L1。结果 在 7/8 名受试者中观察到 CXCL10、IFN-γ 和 sPD-L1 增加(> 2 倍)。3 个周期后,所有 4 名患者均观察到血液中 TIL 克隆扩增,并且与肿瘤大小减小相关(每个 RECIST 中有 2 例完全缓解和 2 例部分缓解)。这些患者还表现出至少一种 HPV-16 特异性克隆显着增加。在评估的 14 名患者中,6 名出现 HPV-16 克隆扩增,其中 4 名显示肿瘤大小减小。结论 Pela + atezo 可以在≥2 线 SCAC 中取得良好的肿瘤反应。这些反应与血液中 HPV-16 特异性和 TIL 克隆扩增有关。血浆分析物水平的有利变化也与肿瘤的免疫控制有关。总之,这些数据支持对 pela 和 CPI 作为 SCAC 免疫疗法的进一步评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肛门鳞状细胞癌 (SCAC) 是一种罕见疾病,约占胃肠道癌症的 2.6%。人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是 SCAC 的主要原因。
设计与对象
一项 1/2 期、Simon 2 阶段研究正在进行,以评估 pela 联合 atezo 作为 ≥2L 疗法治疗不可切除 SCAC 患者的安全性和有效性 (NCT07280377)。患者每周接受 pela 4.5 x 1010 TCID50 静脉注射治疗,每两周接受 atezo 840 mg 治疗,周期为 28 天。
核心方法
通过 T 细胞受体测序(Adaptive Biotechnologies,西雅图,华盛顿州)获取 4 名患者的 治疗 前肿瘤,以鉴定 TIL,并分析这些患者的血液中 TIL 克隆从基线到 3 个周期的扩增情况。通过 T 细胞抗原特异性多重鉴定分析(MIRA 分析,Adaptive Biotechnologies)对 14 名患者的血液进行了 HPV-16 特异性克隆扩增分析。在一个治疗周期后,通过 Olink 检测对 8 名患者的血浆分析物(包括 CXCL10、IFN-γ 和 sPD-L1)进行了测量。
主要结果
在 7/8 的受试者中观察到 CXCL10、IFN-γ 和 sPD-L1 增加(> 2 倍)。3 个周期后,所有 4 名患者均观察到血液中 TIL 克隆扩增,并且与肿瘤大小减小相关(每个 RECIST 中有 2 例完全缓解和 2 例部分缓解)。这些患者还表现出至少一种 HPV-16 特异性克隆显着增加。在接受评估的 14 名患者中,6 名出现 HPV-16 克隆扩增,其中 4 名患者的肿瘤大小有所缩小。
解释与创新
溶瘤呼肠孤病毒 pelareorep (pela) 与 atezolizumab (atezo) 联合治疗在该人群中显示出 ≥2L 治疗 的前景。这些反应与血液中 HPV-16 特异性和 TIL 克隆扩增有关。
科研与公卫意义
血浆分析物水平的有利变化也与肿瘤的免疫控制有关。总之,这些数据支持对 pela 和 CPI 作为 SCAC 免疫疗法的进一步评估。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究的局限性或证据强度。

621 Pelareorep and atezolizumab-induced expansion of HPV-16-specific T cells in the blood is associated with tumor responses in squamous cell anal cancer

Authors: Richard J. Trauger, Thomas Charles Heineman, Hendrik Schürmann, Jens Thomas Siveke, Dirk Arnold
Original abstract (English)
Background Squamous cell anal cancer (SCAC) is a rare disease representing approximately 2.6% of GI cancers. Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the main cause of SCAC. Treatment options for ≥2L SCAC are limited. Checkpoint inhibitors (CPI) are recommended for immunotherapy naïve patients who failed 1L chemotherapy, but response rates are low. The oncolytic reovirus pelareorep (pela) combined with atezolizumab (atezo) has shown promise as a ≥2L treatment in this population. In pancreatic cancer patients treated with pela and atezo, tumor responses are associated with tumor-infiltrating lymphocytes (TIL) and mRAS clonal expansion in the blood. Here, we report the association between SCAC tumor responses, TIL infiltration, and HPV-16-specific clonal expansion in the blood. We also report post-therapy changes in the levels of plasma analytes that are associated with responses to checkpoint inhibitor therapyMethods A phase 1/2, Simon 2-stage study is being conducted to assess the safety and efficacy of pela in combination with atezo as a ≥2L therapy in patients with unresectable SCAC ( NCT07280377). Patients were treated IV with pela 4.5 x 1010 TCID50 weekly and atezo 840 mg biweekly on a 28-day cycle. Pre-treatment tumors were available from 4 patients to identify TILs by T-cell receptor sequencing (Adaptive Biotechnologies, Seattle, WA), and blood from these patients was analyzed for the expansion of TIL clones from baseline through 3 cycles. Blood from 14 patients was analyzed for HPV-16-specific clonal expansion by the Multiplexed Identification of T-cell Antigen Specificity assay (MIRA assay, Adaptive Biotechnologies). Plasma analytes including CXCL10, IFN-γ, and sPD-L1 were measured by the Olink assay in 8 patients after one cycle of therapy.Results Increases (> 2-fold) in CXCL10, IFN-γ, and sPD-L1 were observed in 7/8 subjects. TIL clonal expansion in the blood was observed after 3 cycles in all 4 patients and was associated with reductions in tumor size (2 complete and 2 partial responses per RECIST). These patients also showed a significant increase in at least one HPV-16-specific clone. Of the 14 patients evaluated, 6 had HPV-16 clonal expansion, and 4 of these showed a reduction in tumor size.Conclusions Pela + atezo can achieve favorable tumor responses in ≥2 nd-line SCAC. These responses are associated with HPV-16-specific and TIL clonal expansion in the blood. The favorable changes in plasma analyte levels were also associated with the immune control of tumors. Together, these data support further evaluation of pela and CPI as an immunotherapy for SCAC.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Squamous cell anal cancer (SCAC) is a rare disease representing approximately 2.6% of GI cancers. Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the main cause of SCAC.
Design and population
A phase 1/2, Simon 2-stage study is being conducted to assess the safety and efficacy of pela in combination with atezo as a ≥2L therapy in patients with unresectable SCAC (NCT07280377). Patients were treated IV with pela 4.5 x 1010 TCID50 weekly and atezo 840 mg biweekly on a 28-day cycle.
Core methods
Pre-treatment tumors were available from 4 patients to identify TILs by T-cell receptor sequencing (Adaptive Biotechnologies, Seattle, WA), and blood from these patients was analyzed for the expansion of TIL clones from baseline through 3 cycles. Blood from 14 patients was analyzed for HPV-16-specific clonal expansion by the Multiplexed Identification of T-cell Antigen Specificity assay (MIRA assay, Adaptive Biotechnologies). Plasma analytes including CXCL10, IFN-γ, and sPD-L1 were measured by the Olink assay in 8 patients after one cycle of therapy.
Main results
Increases (> 2-fold) in CXCL10, IFN-γ, and sPD-L1 were observed in 7/8 subjects. TIL clonal expansion in the blood was observed after 3 cycles in all 4 patients and was associated with reductions in tumor size (2 complete and 2 partial responses per RECIST). These patients also showed a significant increase in at least one HPV-16-specific clone. Of the 14 patients evaluated, 6 had HPV-16 clonal expansion, and 4 of these showed a reduction in tumor size.
Interpretation and novelty
The oncolytic reovirus pelareorep (pela) combined with atezolizumab (atezo) has shown promise as a ≥2L treatment in this population. These responses are associated with HPV-16-specific and TIL clonal expansion in the blood.
Scientific and public-health significance
The favorable changes in plasma analyte levels were also associated with the immune control of tumors. Together, these data support further evaluation of pela and CPI as an immunotherapy for SCAC.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength for the study.

波兰妇女关于 人乳头瘤病毒 自我抽样的知识、态度和实践:一项横断面在线调查

Women's knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding human papillomavirus self-sampling in Poland: a cross-sectional online survey
作者： Joanna P. Kacperczyk-Bartnik, Zoia Razumova, Natalia Zawrotna, Jakub Wabiński, Joanna Sozańska, Edyta Rola, Jacek Sieńko, Krzysztof Czajkowski, Paweł Derlatka, Ewa Romejko-Wolniewicz
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人们越来越多地推荐基于 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的客观筛查,包括自我采样,但有关波兰女性对自我采样态度的证据仍然很少。
设计与对象
本研究评估了波兰女性对 宫颈癌 预防知识、筛查史以及对 HPV 自我采样的态度。
核心方法
2018 年 5 月至 2019 年 4 月期间,对波兰 1189 名成年女性进行了一项匿名横断面在线调查。
主要结果
在 1189 名受访者中,523 名 (44.00%) 表示倾向于自我采样(从未接受过筛查的女性为 67.00%,之前接受过筛查的女性为 36.22%)。
解释与创新
Previous cytology (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] 0.44, 95% confidence interval [CI] “0.32, 0.62”) and regular gynaecological attendance (aOR 0.35, 95% CI “0.26, 0.46”) were associated with lower preference.
科研与公卫意义
有必要在有组织的筛查中进行清晰的沟通和经过验证的自我采样的整合。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告本研究的局限性。

Women's knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding human papillomavirus self-sampling in Poland: a cross-sectional online survey

Authors: Joanna P. Kacperczyk-Bartnik, Zoia Razumova, Natalia Zawrotna, Jakub Wabiński, Joanna Sozańska, Edyta Rola, Jacek Sieńko, Krzysztof Czajkowski, Paweł Derlatka, Ewa Romejko-Wolniewicz
Original abstract (English)
Objective Human papillomavirus (HPV)-based screening, including self-sampling, is increasingly recommended, but evidence on Polish women's attitudes toward self-sampling remains scarce. This study assessed knowledge of cervical cancer prevention, screening history, and attitudes toward HPV self-sampling among women in Poland. Methods An anonymous cross-sectional online survey of a 1189 adult women in Poland was conducted between May 2018 and April 2019. The primary outcome was a hypothetical preference for home self-sampling, assuming equivalent diagnostic performance. Participants also rated shame, pain, fear, discomfort, ease, and self-confidence for clinician-collected versus self-collected sampling. Results Of 1189 respondents, 523 (44.00%) stated a preference for self-sampling (67.00% of never-screened vs 36.22% of previously screened women). Previous cytology (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] 0.44, 95% confidence interval [CI] “0.32, 0.62”) and regular gynaecological attendance (aOR 0.35, 95% CI “0.26, 0.46”) were associated with lower preference. Higher education was associated with higher preference (aOR 1.56, 95% CI “1.12, 2.17”). Self-sampling was rated less shameful and uncomfortable, though anticipated pain and fear were slightly higher. Conclusions In this pre-implementation sample, hypothetical interest in HPV self-sampling was considerable, particularly among women outside routine gynaecological care. Clear communication and integration of validated self-sampling within organised screening are warranted.

Question and background
Objective Human papillomavirus (HPV)-based screening, including self-sampling, is increasingly recommended, but evidence on Polish women's attitudes toward self-sampling remains scarce.
Design and population

Core methods
An anonymous cross-sectional online survey of a 1189 adult women in Poland was conducted between May 2018 and April 2019.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty
Previous cytology (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] 0.44, 95% confidence interval [CI] “0.32, 0.62”) and regular gynaecological attendance (aOR 0.35, 95% CI “0.26, 0.46”) were associated with lower preference.
Scientific and public-health significance
Clear communication and integration of validated self-sampling within organised screening are warranted.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.
