A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

人乳头瘤病毒 的扩展基因分型和人工智能辅助宫颈视觉评估在九种资源有限环境中预测宫颈癌前病变 (PAVE):一项横断面研究

Extended genotyping of human papillomavirus and AI-assisted cervical visual evaluation for prediction of cervical precancer in nine resource-limited settings (PAVE): a cross-sectional study
作者： Silvia de Sanjosé, Federica Inturrisi, Rebecca B. Perkins, Didem Egemen, Ana Cecilia Rodríguez, Brian Befano, Diego Guillén, José Jerónimo, Nicole G. Campos, Ana Ribeiro
摘要中文翻译背景 在资源有限的环境中进行有效的 宫颈癌 筛选需要准确、可扩展且高效的方法。我们的目的是评估一种新型宫颈筛查和分诊策略,该策略将自采样 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 检测与基于人工智能的自动视觉评估 (AVE) 相结合,用于检测 3 级或更严重的宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN3+)。方法 在这项横断面 HPV 自动视觉评估 (PAVE) 研究中,我们针对资源有限的环境设计了一种新的宫颈筛查和分诊策略,该策略整合了自采集阴道标本的 HPV 分级扩展基因分型、感染 HPV 女性的 AVE 以及用于有效医疗资源管理的风险分层。2022年11月14日至2025年6月5日期间,从巴西、萨尔瓦多、洪都拉斯、多米尼加共和国、柬埔寨、斯威士兰、马拉维、尼日利亚和坦桑尼亚的临床中心前瞻性招募了以前没有接受过宫颈癌的非孕妇(年龄为30-49岁,或感染艾滋病毒[WLHIV]的女性年龄为25-49岁)。使用新的、快速且简化的扩展基因分型 HPV 检测对自行收集的阴道样本进行测试,该检测可产生四个致癌风险组(HPV16 [最高风险];HPV18 或 45 [高风险];HPV31、33、35、52 或 58 [中等风险];以及 HPV39、51、56、59 或 68 [最低风险])。患有 HPV 的女性进行了宫颈成像和活检以诊断 CIN3+,并由专家远程病理学证实。使用移动阴道镜拍摄宫颈图像,并通过先前验证的深度学习 AVE 算法将其分类为严重、不确定或正常。根据 HIV 状态,在组合的四个 HPV 基因型组和三个 AVE 类别的每个层中计算 CIN3+ 和 95% CI 的绝对风险。计算接受者操作分析以估计该策略的曲线下面积 (AUC)。结果 在 49 074 名具有有效 HPV 结果的女性中,13 992 名 (28·5%) HPV 呈阳性,其中 11 169 名 (79·8%) 参加了分诊。在 7535 名患有 HPV 且具有完整 AVE 和病理数据的女性中,6113 名 HIV 阴性女性中检测到 359 例 (5·9%) CIN3+ 病例,在 1422 名 WLHIV 中检测到 174 例 (12·2%) CIN3+ 病例。CIN3+ 风险在 HPV 和 AVE 分级中系统性增加,其中最高的 CIN3+ 风险与 HPV16 相关(19·6% [95% CI 17·3–21·9];1129 名参与者中的 221 名)和严重 AVE 分类(​​12·7% [11·5–14·0];2871 名参与者中的 366 名)。使用 HPV 基因分型和 AVE 评估评估的 CIN3+ 风险支持了测试的独立效用。在 509 名未感染 HIV 的女性中,在 162 名 (31·8% [95% CI 27·8–35·9]) CIN3+ 病例中检测到具有严重 AVE 分类的 HPV16,在 101 名 WLHIV 中检测到 43 名 (42·6% [32·9–52·2]) CIN3+ 病例。对于通过宫颈外活检诊断的 CIN3+,最大 AUC 为 0·82 (95% CI 0·80–0·84)。解释 我们的研究结果支持整合自我采样、HPV 扩展基因分型和 AVE 作为 宫颈癌 风险预测策略的有效性。实施前需要进行成本效益分析。资助 NCI 癌症治愈登月计划和无国界医生基金会。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 在资源有限的环境中进行有效的 宫颈癌 筛选需要准确、可扩展且高效的方法。
设计与对象
在这项横断面 HPV 自动视觉评估 (PAVE) 研究中,于 11 月 14 日期间前瞻性地从巴西、萨尔瓦多、洪都拉斯、多米尼加共和国、柬埔寨、斯威士兰、马拉维、尼日利亚和坦桑尼亚的临床中心招募了既往没有 宫颈癌 的非孕妇(年龄为 30-49 岁,或年龄为 25-49 岁的 HIV 感染者 [WLHIV])。2022年和2025年6月5日。
核心方法
使用新的、快速且简化的扩展基因分型 HPV 检测对自行收集的阴道样本进行测试,该检测可产生四个致癌风险组(HPV16 [最高风险];HPV18 或 45 [高风险];HPV31、33、35、52 或 58 [中等风险];以及 HPV39、51、56、59 或 68 [最低风险])。使用移动阴道镜拍摄宫颈图像,并通过先前验证的深度学习 AVE 算法将其分类为严重、不确定或正常。
主要结果
在 49 074 名具有有效 HPV 结果的女性中,13 992 名 (28·5%) HPV 阳性,其中 11 169 名 (79·8%) 接受了分诊。在 7535 名患有 HPV 且具有完整 AVE 和病理数据的女性中,6113 名 HIV 阴性女性中检测到 359 例 (5·9%) CIN3+ 病例,在 1422 名 WLHIV 中检测到 174 例 (12·2%) CIN3+ 病例。
解释与创新
解释 我们的研究结果支持整合自我采样、HPV 扩展基因分型和 AVE 作为 宫颈癌 风险预测策略的有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将 HPV 扩展基因分型与基于人工智能的视觉评估相结合可以改善资源有限环境中的 宫颈癌 风险预测,支持开发可扩展且高效的筛查策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Extended genotyping of human papillomavirus and AI-assisted cervical visual evaluation for prediction of cervical precancer in nine resource-limited settings (PAVE): a cross-sectional study

Authors: Silvia de Sanjosé, Federica Inturrisi, Rebecca B. Perkins, Didem Egemen, Ana Cecilia Rodríguez, Brian Befano, Diego Guillén, José Jerónimo, Nicole G. Campos, Ana Ribeiro
Original abstract (English)
Background Effective cervical cancer screening in resource-limited settings requires accurate, scalable, and efficient approaches. We aimed to evaluate a novel cervical screening and triage strategy that combines self-sampled human papillomavirus (HPV) testing with AI-based automated visual evaluation (AVE) for the detection of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 or worse (CIN3+). Methods In this cross-sectional HPV-Automated Visual Evaluation (PAVE) study we designed a new cervical screening and triage strategy for resource-limited settings that integrated HPV hierarchical extended genotyping of self-collected vaginal specimens, AVE of women with HPV, and risk stratification for efficient health-care resource management. Between Nov 14, 2022, and June 5, 2025, non-pregnant women (aged 30–49 years or aged 25–49 years for women living with HIV [WLHIV]) with no previous cervical cancer were prospectively enrolled from clinical sites in Brazil, El Salvador, Honduras, the Dominican Republic, Cambodia, Eswatini, Malawi, Nigeria, and Tanzania. Self-collected vaginal samples were tested using a new, rapid, and simplified extended genotyping HPV assay that yields four oncogenic risk groups (HPV16 [highest risk]; HPV18 or 45 [high risk]; HPV31, 33, 35, 52, or 58 [moderate risk]; and HPV39, 51, 56, 59, or 68 [lowest risk]). Women with HPV had cervical imaging and biopsy to diagnose CIN3+, which was confirmed by expert telepathology. Cervical images were captured with a mobile colposcope and classified by a previously validated deep-learning AVE algorithm as severe, indeterminate, or normal. Absolute risks of CIN3+ and 95% CIs were computed within each stratum of the combined four HPV genotype groups and the three AVE categories by HIV status. Receiver operated analysis was computed to estimate area under the curve (AUC) for the strategy. Findings Among 49 074 women with valid HPV results, 13 992 (28·5%) were HPV positive, of whom 11 169 (79·8%) attended triage. Among 7535 women with HPV and complete AVE and pathology data, 359 (5·9%) CIN3+ cases were detected among 6113 HIV-negative women and 174 (12·2%) CIN3+ cases were detected among 1422 WLHIV. CIN3+ risk increased systematically across the hierarchical HPV and AVE categories, with the highest CIN3+ risk associated with HPV16 (19·6% [95% CI 17·3–21·9]; 221 of 1129 participants) and severe AVE classification (12·7% [11·5–14·0]; 366 of 2871 participants). Risk of CIN3+ assessed using HPV genotyping and AVE assessment supported the independent utility of the tests. HPV16 with severe AVE classification was detected in 162 (31·8% [95% CI 27·8–35·9]) CIN3+ cases among 509 women without HIV and 43 (42·6% [32·9–52·2]) CIN3+ cases were among 101 WLHIV. The maximum AUC was 0·82 (95% CI 0·80–0·84) for CIN3+ diagnosed with ectocervical biopsy. Interpretation Our findings support the efficacy of integrating self-sampling, HPV extended genotyping, and AVE as a cervical cancer risk prediction strategy. Cost-effectiveness analysis will be required before implementation. Funding NCI Cancer Cures Moonshot Initiative and Médecins Sans Frontiers Foundation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Background Effective cervical cancer screening in resource-limited settings requires accurate, scalable, and efficient approaches.
Design and population
In this cross-sectional HPV-Automated Visual Evaluation (PAVE) study, non-pregnant women (aged 30–49 years or aged 25–49 years for women living with HIV [WLHIV]) with no previous cervical cancer were prospectively enrolled from clinical sites in Brazil, El Salvador, Honduras, the Dominican Republic, Cambodia, Eswatini, Malawi, Nigeria, and Tanzania between Nov 14, 2022, and June 5, 2025.
Core methods
Self-collected vaginal samples were tested using a new, rapid, and simplified extended genotyping HPV assay that yields four oncogenic risk groups (HPV16 [highest risk]; HPV18 or 45 [high risk]; HPV31, 33, 35, 52, or 58 [moderate risk]; and HPV39, 51, 56, 59, or 68 [lowest risk]). Cervical images were captured with a mobile colposcope and classified by a previously validated deep-learning AVE algorithm as severe, indeterminate, or normal.
Main results
Among 49 074 women with valid HPV results, 13 992 (28·5%) were HPV positive, of whom 11 169 (79·8%) attended triage. Among 7535 women with HPV and complete AVE and pathology data, 359 (5·9%) CIN3+ cases were detected among 6113 HIV-negative women and 174 (12·2%) CIN3+ cases were detected among 1422 WLHIV.
Interpretation and novelty
Interpretation Our findings support the efficacy of integrating self-sampling, HPV extended genotyping, and AVE as a cervical cancer risk prediction strategy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that combining HPV extended genotyping with AI-based visual evaluation could improve cervical cancer risk prediction in resource-limited settings, supporting the development of scalable and efficient screening strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

Alpha 和 Beta 人乳头瘤病毒 感染对墨西哥 HIV 感染者口腔微生物组的影响

Implications of Alpha and Beta Human Papillomaviruses Infection in the Oral Microbiome from Mexican Subjects Living with HIV
作者： José Adán Vizcaíno Résendiz, Tonatiuh Abimael Baltazar-Díaz, Felipe De Jesús Bustos-Rodríguez, Luis Felipe Jave‐Suárez, Juan Carlos Vázquez-Limón, Pedro Martínez-Ayala, Dana Alejandra Figueroa-González, Miriam Ruth Bueno-Topete, Luz Alicia González-Hernández, Jaime Federico Andrade-Villanueva
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染和口腔微生物群改变与 HIV 感染者 (PLHIV) 的口腔疾病独立相关。然而,α-HPV (α-HPV)、β-HPV (β-HPV) 和口腔微生物组之间的关系仍知之甚少。这项横断面研究旨在描述墨西哥 PLHIV 口腔 HPV 感染与口腔和口咽微生物群之间的关联,并持续病毒学控制和免疫重建。通过冲洗和漱口获得口腔和口咽样本,将参与者分为 HPV 阴性组、α-HPV(PGMY 引物)和 β-HPV(FAP 引物)组。使用 Illumina MiSeq 平台对 16S rRNA 基因的 V3–V4 区域进行测序。最终分析共纳入 65 名参与者:19 名 HPV 阴性、16 名 -α-HPV 阳性(PGMY 引物)和 β-HPV 阳性(FAP 引物)。α-HPV 阳性组和 β-HPV 阳性组口腔微生物组的细菌 Alpha 多样性均显着高于 HPV 阴性组,其中 α-HPV 阳性组的多样性最高。α-HPV 组还表现出较高的梭杆菌门/拟杆菌门比率以及梭杆菌门相关类群的富集。此外,预测的功能分析显示α-HPV(PGMY引物)组中与赖氨酸和丙酮酸发酵为丁酸相关的途径富集。这些发现表明,α-和 β-HPV 感染可能与 PLHIV 中不同的口腔微生物生态系统有关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染和口腔微生物群改变与 HIV 感染者 (PLHIV) 的口腔疾病独立相关。α-HPV (α-HPV)、β-HPV (β-HPV) 和口腔微生物群之间的关系仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究旨在描述墨西哥 PLHIV 口腔 HPV 感染与口腔和口咽微生物群之间的关联,并持续病毒学控制和免疫重建。通过冲洗和漱口获得口腔和口咽样本,将参与者分为 HPV 阴性组、α-HPV(PGMY 引物)和 β-HPV(FAP 引物)组。
核心方法
使用 Illumina MiSeq 平台对 16S rRNA 基因的 V3–V4 区域进行测序。最终分析共纳入 65 名参与者:19 名 HPV 阴性、16 名 -α-HPV 阳性(PGMY 引物)和 β-HPV 阳性(FAP 引物)。
主要结果
α-HPV 阳性组和 β-HPV 阳性组口腔微生物组的细菌 Alpha 多样性均显着高于 HPV 阴性组,其中 α-HPV 阳性组的多样性最高。α-HPV 组还表现出较高的梭杆菌门/拟杆菌门比率以及梭杆菌门相关类群的富集。此外,预测的功能分析显示α-HPV(PGMY引物)组中与赖氨酸和丙酮酸发酵为丁酸相关的途径富集。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,α-和 β-HPV 感染可能与 PLHIV 中不同的口腔微生物生态系统有关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来关于特定 HPV 类型在改变 PLHIV 口腔微生物组中的作用的研究提供信息,从而可能有助于制定针对口腔健康的有针对性的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了建立因果关系的能力。该证据基于口腔微生物组的测序和功能分析,这可能无法完全捕捉到 宿主 与病毒相互作用的复杂性。

Implications of Alpha and Beta Human Papillomaviruses Infection in the Oral Microbiome from Mexican Subjects Living with HIV

Authors: José Adán Vizcaíno Résendiz, Tonatiuh Abimael Baltazar-Díaz, Felipe De Jesús Bustos-Rodríguez, Luis Felipe Jave‐Suárez, Juan Carlos Vázquez-Limón, Pedro Martínez-Ayala, Dana Alejandra Figueroa-González, Miriam Ruth Bueno-Topete, Luz Alicia González-Hernández, Jaime Federico Andrade-Villanueva
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and oral microbiota alterations have been independently associated with oral disease in people living with HIV (PLHIV). However, the relationship between alpha-HPV (α-HPV), beta-HPV (β-HPV), and the oral microbiome remains poorly understood. This cross-sectional study aimed to describe the association between oral HPV infection and the oral and oropharyngeal microbiome in Mexican PLHIV with sustained virologic control and immune reconstitution. Oral and oropharyngeal samples were obtained by rinsing and gargling, and participants were classified into HPV-negative, α-HPV (PGMY primers), and β-HPV (FAP primers) groups. The V3–V4 region of the 16S rRNA gene was sequenced using the Illumina MiSeq platform. A total of 65 participants were included in the final analysis: 19 HPV-negative, 16 -α-HPV positive (PGMY primers), and β-HPV positive (FAP primers). Bacterial Alpha diversity of the oral microbiome was significantly higher in both α-HPV- positive and β-HPV positive groups than in the HPV-negative group, with the highest diversity observed in the α-HPV positive group. The α-HPV group also showed a higher Fusobacteriota/Bacteroidota ratio and enrichment of Fusobacteriota-related taxa. In addition, predicted functional analysis showed enrichment of pathways related to lysine and pyruvate fermentation to butyrate in the α-HPV(PGMY primers) group. These findings suggest that α- and β-HPV infections may be associated with different oral microbial ecosystems in PLHIV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and oral microbiota alterations have been independently associated with oral disease in people living with HIV (PLHIV). The relationship between alpha-HPV (α-HPV), beta-HPV (β-HPV), and the oral microbiome remains poorly understood.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study aimed to describe the association between oral HPV infection and the oral and oropharyngeal microbiome in Mexican PLHIV with sustained virologic control and immune reconstitution. Oral and oropharyngeal samples were obtained by rinsing and gargling, and participants were classified into HPV-negative, α-HPV (PGMY primers), and β-HPV (FAP primers) groups.
Core methods
The V3–V4 region of the 16S rRNA gene was sequenced using the Illumina MiSeq platform. A total of 65 participants were included in the final analysis: 19 HPV-negative, 16 -α-HPV positive (PGMY primers), and β-HPV positive (FAP primers).
Main results
Bacterial Alpha diversity of the oral microbiome was significantly higher in both α-HPV positive and β-HPV positive groups than in the HPV-negative group, with the highest diversity observed in the α-HPV positive group. The α-HPV group also showed a higher Fusobacteriota/Bacteroidota ratio and enrichment of Fusobacteriota-related taxa. In addition, predicted functional analysis showed enrichment of pathways related to lysine and pyruvate fermentation to butyrate in the α-HPV (PGMY primers) group.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that α- and β-HPV infections may be associated with different oral microbial ecosystems in PLHIV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future studies on the role of specific HPV types in altering the oral microbiome of PLHIV, potentially contributing to the development of targeted interventions for oral health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is cross-sectional, limiting the ability to establish causality. The evidence is based on sequencing and functional analysis of the oral microbiome, which may not fully capture the complexity of host-virus interactions.

睾丸肿瘤患者精道中的人乳头瘤病毒 患病率 HPV 感染对睾丸肿瘤的发生有一定的病因作用吗?

Human Papillomavirus Prevalence in Seminal Tract Among the Patients with Testicular Tumor; Dose HPV Infection Have an Etiological Role for the Development of Testicular Tumor?
作者： Shingo Takada, Kazuyoshi Shigehara, Hiroki Nakata, Naoaki Sakatoku, Kotaro Fukukawa, Rei Shinzawa, Yuki Kato, Shohei Kawaguchi, Takahiro Nohara, Kouji Izumi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是否在睾丸肿瘤的发生中发挥病因作用。这一背景强调需要了解 HPV 与睾丸癌之间的潜在关联,而睾丸癌通常与 HPV 无关。
设计与对象
该研究对 116 名接受根治性腹股沟睾丸切除术的日本患者进行,分析睾丸肿瘤、附睾和精索标本,以评估 HPV 患病率 和组织定位。
核心方法
使用巢式聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 分析福尔马林固定、石蜡包埋的组织,进行 HPV-DNA 检测,然后进行基于测序的 HPV 基因分型。通过原位杂交 (ISH) 进一步评估 HPV-DNA 定位,对 HPV 阳性标本进行 E6/E7 mRNA ISH。
主要结果
HPV-DNA was detected in three patients (3/116, 2.6%), including one testicular tumor specimen (1/116, 0.9%) and two spermatic cord specimens (2/116, 1.7%). All HPV-positive specimens contained HPV16-DNA.
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HPV16-DNA 存在于睾丸肿瘤和精索组织中,但它们没有提供转录活性 HPV 癌基因或在睾丸肿瘤发生中的因果作用的证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为未来关于 HPV 在睾丸癌中的潜在作用的研究提供信息,尽管它们并未建立与疾病因果关系的直接联系。
局限与证据强度
该研究无法提供具有转录活性的 HPV 癌基因的证据,限制了确定因果关系的能力。证据是中等的,因为它仅显示 HPV16-DNA 的存在,而没有功能确认。

Human Papillomavirus Prevalence in Seminal Tract Among the Patients with Testicular Tumor; Dose HPV Infection Have an Etiological Role for the Development of Testicular Tumor?

Authors: Shingo Takada, Kazuyoshi Shigehara, Hiroki Nakata, Naoaki Sakatoku, Kotaro Fukukawa, Rei Shinzawa, Yuki Kato, Shohei Kawaguchi, Takahiro Nohara, Kouji Izumi
Original abstract (English)
This study investigated the prevalence, genotypes, and tissue localization of human papillomavirus (HPV)-DNA in testicular tumor including epididymis and spermatic cord specimens from 116 Japanese patients who underwent radical inguinal orchiectomy. Formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded tissues were analyzed by nested polymerase chain reaction (PCR), followed by sequencing-based HPV genotyping. HPV-DNA localization was assessed by in situ hybridization (ISH), and E6/E7 mRNA ISH was performed in HPV-positive specimens. HPV-DNA was detected in three patients (3/116, 2.6%), including one testicular tumor specimen (1/116, 0.9%) and two spermatic cord specimens (2/116, 1.7%). All HPV-positive specimens contained HPV16-DNA. HPV-DNA ISH signals were observed in seminoma cells in one testicular specimen and in the vas deferens epithelium in one spermatic cord specimen. On the other hand, ISH signals were not present in epididymis tissue in the HPV-positive testicular tumor tissue, suggesting that HPV-DNA detection rate of epididymis was 0%. E6/E7 mRNA signals were not detected in any HPV-positive specimen. These findings demonstrate that HPV16-DNA was surely present in testicular tumor and the vas deferens epithelium. However we could not provide evidence of transcriptionally active HPV oncogenes, and could not establish a causal role in testicular tumorigenesis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate whether human papillomavirus (HPV) infection could play an etiological role in the development of testicular tumors. The background highlights the need to understand the potential association between HPV and testicular cancer, which is not typically linked to HPV.
Design and population
The study was conducted on 116 Japanese patients who underwent radical inguinal orchiectomy, analyzing testicular tumor, epididymis, and spermatic cord specimens to assess HPV prevalence and tissue localization.
Core methods
Formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded tissues were analyzed using nested polymerase chain reaction (PCR) for HPV-DNA detection, followed by sequencing-based HPV genotyping. HPV-DNA localization was further assessed by in situ hybridization (ISH), with E6/E7 mRNA ISH performed on HPV-positive specimens.
Main results
HPV-DNA was detected in three patients (3/116, 2.6%), including one testicular tumor specimen (1/116, 0.9%) and two spermatic cord specimens (2/116, 1.7%). All HPV-positive specimens contained HPV16-DNA.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HPV16-DNA is present in testicular tumor and spermatic cord tissues, but they do not provide evidence of transcriptionally active HPV oncogenes or a causal role in testicular tumorigenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform future studies on the potential role of HPV in testicular cancer, though they do not establish a direct link to disease causation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study could not provide evidence of transcriptionally active HPV oncogenes, limiting the ability to establish a causal role. The evidence is moderate as it only shows presence of HPV16-DNA without functional confirmation.

接受根治性膀胱切除术的膀胱癌男性下尿路 人乳头瘤病毒 感染的解剖分布

Anatomical Distribution of Human Papillomavirus Infection in the Lower Urinary Tract of Men with Bladder Cancer Undergoing Radical Cystectomy
作者： Kotaro Fukukawa, Kazuyoshi Shigehara, Rei Shinzawa, Hiroki Nakata, Naoaki Sakatoku, Shingo Takada, Yuki Kato, Shohei Kawaguchi, Takahiro Nohara, Kouji Izumi
摘要中文翻译我们研究了接受根治性膀胱切除术的男性膀胱癌患者下泌尿道中人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染的解剖分布情况。总共从102名患者中收集了79份前尿道样本、99份前列腺尿道标本、74份膀胱肿瘤组织标本以及87份非肿瘤膀胱组织标本。对每份福尔马林固定石蜡包埋组织标本进行DNA提取后,采用嵌套聚合酶链反应(PCR)检测HPV-DNA,并通过桑格测序确定HPV基因型。在阳性样本中使用原位杂交(ISH)技术定位HPV-DNA,并通过E6/E7 mRNA ISH评估转录活性。在102名患者中,有16名(15.7%)在任何部位检测到HPV-DNA,具体部位的检测率分别为:非肿瘤膀胱黏膜8.0%(7/87)、前尿道5.1%(4/79)、膀胱肿瘤组织4.1%(3/74)以及前列腺尿道4.0%(4/99)。检测到HPV16的样本有11例,HPV18的样本有7例。在18例HPV阳性标本中,观察到HPV-DNA ISH信号的有12例(66.7%),E6/E7 mRNA信号的有2例。HPV-DNA在下泌尿道的多个部位被检测到。膀胱癌标本中E6/E7 mRNA的表达需要进一步研究高危型HPV感染的生物学意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们调查了接受根治性膀胱切除术的膀胱癌男性下尿路 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染的解剖学分布。
设计与对象
总共收集了 102 名患者的 79 份前尿道标本、99 份前列腺尿道标本、74 份膀胱肿瘤标本和 87 份非肿瘤膀胱标本。
核心方法
从每个福尔马林固定石蜡包埋的组织标本中提取DNA后,通过巢式聚合酶链式反应检测HPV-DNA,并通过桑格测序确定HPV基因型。
主要结果
HPV-DNA在102名患者中的任何部位被检测到的有16例(15.7%),具体部位的检测率分别为:非肿瘤膀胱黏膜8.0%(7/87)、前尿道5.1%(4/79)、膀胱肿瘤组织4.1%(3/74)以及前列腺尿道4.0%(4/99)。检测到HPV16的样本有11例,HPV18的样本有7例。在18例HPV阳性标本中,观察到HPV-DNA ISH信号的有12例(66.7%),E6/E7 mRNA信号的有2例。
解释与创新
该研究为膀胱癌男性下尿路 HPV 感染的解剖学分布提供了新的见解,强调需要进一步研究 HR-HPV 感染的生物学相关性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来关于 HPV 在膀胱癌中的作用及其对 诊断 和 治疗 策略的潜在影响的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单一中心和仅限摘要的证据,这可能会影响普遍性。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

Anatomical Distribution of Human Papillomavirus Infection in the Lower Urinary Tract of Men with Bladder Cancer Undergoing Radical Cystectomy

Authors: Kotaro Fukukawa, Kazuyoshi Shigehara, Rei Shinzawa, Hiroki Nakata, Naoaki Sakatoku, Shingo Takada, Yuki Kato, Shohei Kawaguchi, Takahiro Nohara, Kouji Izumi
Original abstract (English)
We investigated the anatomical distribution of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in the lower urinary tract of men with bladder cancer undergoing radical cystectomy. In total, 79 anterior urethral samples, 99 prostatic urethral specimens, 74 bladder tumor specimens, and 87 non-tumor bladder specimens were collected from 102 patients. After DNA extraction from each formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded tissue specimen, HPV-DNA was examined by nested polymerase chain reaction, and HPV genotypes were determined by Sanger sequencing. In situ hybridization (ISH) was used to localize HPV-DNA in positive samples, and E6/E7 mRNA ISH was performed to assess transcriptional activity. HPV-DNA was detected at any site in 16 (15.7%) of 102 patients, with site-specific detection rates of 8.0% (7/87) in non-tumor bladder mucosa, 5.1% (4/79) in the anterior urethra, 4.1% (3/74) in bladder tumor tissue, and 4.0% (4/99) in the prostatic urethra. HPV16 was detected in 11 samples, and HPV18 in 7 samples. HPV-DNA ISH signals were observed in 12 (66.7%) of 18 HPV-positive specimens, and E6/E7 mRNA signals were present in 2 of 18 specimens. HPV-DNA was detected at various sites of the lower urinary tract. E6/E7 mRNA expression in a bladder cancer specimen warrants further research on the biological relevance of HR-HPV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
We investigated the anatomical distribution of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in the lower urinary tract of men with bladder cancer undergoing radical cystectomy.
Design and population
In total, 79 anterior urethral samples, 99 prostatic urethral specimens, 74 bladder tumor specimens, and 87 non-tumor bladder specimens were collected from 102 patients.
Core methods
After DNA extraction from each formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded tissue specimen, HPV-DNA was examined by nested polymerase chain reaction, and HPV genotypes were determined by Sanger sequencing.
Main results
HPV-DNA was detected at any site in 16 (15.7%) of 102 patients, with site-specific detection rates of 8.0% (7/87) in non-tumor bladder mucosa, 5.1% (4/79) in the anterior urethra, 4.1% (3/74) in bladder tumor tissue, and 4.0% (4/99) in the prostatic urethra. HPV16 was detected in 11 samples, and HPV18 in 7 samples. HPV-DNA ISH signals were observed in 12 (66.7%) of 18 HPV-positive specimens, and E6/E7 mRNA signals were present in 2 of 18 specimens.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides novel insights into the anatomical distribution of HPV infection in the lower urinary tract of men with bladder cancer, highlighting the need for further research on the biological relevance of HR-HPV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future studies on the role of HPV in bladder cancer and its potential implications for diagnosis and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single center and abstract-only evidence, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

新辅助纳武单抗联合卡铂和紫杉醇治疗局部晚期可切除头颈鳞状细胞癌患者:一项 II 期单臂试验。

Neoadjuvant Nivolumab plus Carboplatin and Paclitaxel in Patients with Locally Advanced Resectable Squamous Cell Carcinoma of the Head and Neck: A Phase II, Single-Arm Trial.
作者： Ralph G Zinner, Eric V. Mastrolonardo, Jennifer Maria Johnson, Kathryn L. Nunes, Pablo Llerena, Zachary T. Elliott, Madalina Tuluc, Joseph M Curry, Christopher E Fundakowski, Andrew Yampolsky
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的:在局部晚期可切除的头颈部鳞状细胞癌(SCCHN)患者中,接受新辅助化疗并获得主要病理反应(MPR)或病理完全反应(pCR)的患者,其总体生存率(OS)优于病理反应较差的患者。为了提高疗效,我们向新辅助化疗中加入了PD-1抑制剂纳武利尤单抗,并评估了联合治疗的效果。患者和方法:本项单臂、开放标签II期试验纳入了新诊断的口腔、口咽、下咽和喉部的局部晚期HPV阴性SCCHN患者,或口咽部II至III期HPV阳性SCCHN患者。患者接受新辅助治疗(卡铂和紫杉醇,6周)联合纳武利尤单抗(每两周一次),随后进行手术和辅助放疗±化疗。主要终点是原发部位的pCR。结果:共纳入34名患者并接受新辅助治疗。33名患者接受了手术(R0切除率为100%)。疾病部位包括口腔(79%)、口咽(12%)、下咽(6%)和喉部(3%)。28名(85%)患者为IV期疾病。术后随访中位数为35.3个月。14名(41%)患者出现3/4级人乳头瘤病毒相关不良事件。27名(82%)患者完成了所有新辅助治疗周期。24名(73%)患者在原发部位至少达到MPR,其中15名(45%)达到pCR。在一项未计划的3年分析中,无病生存率和总体生存率分别为74%和83%。结论:本项II期试验评估了未接受过治疗的局部晚期可切除SCCHN患者的新辅助纳武利尤单抗联合卡铂和紫杉醇治疗,该治疗方案耐受性良好,并达到了原发部位pCR的主要终点。需要在晚期、可切除的HPV阴性SCCHN患者中开展III期确认性研究。参见Anderson和Sikora的评论文章,第p页。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在局部晚期可切除头颈鳞状细胞癌(SCCHN)中,接受新辅助化疗且获得主要病理缓解(MPR)或病理完全缓解(pCR)的患者的总生存(OS)率优于病理缓解较差的患者。
设计与对象
这项单臂、开放标签 II 期试验评估了新辅助纳武单抗联合卡铂和紫杉醇治疗新诊断的 III 期至 IV 期 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 口腔、口咽、下咽和喉部阴性 SCCHN 或 II 期至 III 期 HPV 阳性口咽 SCCHN 的患者。
核心方法
患者接受新辅助卡铂和紫杉醇(6周)加纳武单抗(每隔一周),然后接受手术和辅助放疗±化疗。主要终点是原发部位的 pCR。
主要结果
Thirty-four patients were enrolled and received neoadjuvant therapy. Twenty-four (73%) patients had at least an MPR at the primary site, and 15 (45%) had a pCR. Twenty-seven (82%) patients completed all cycles of neoadjuvant therapy.
解释与创新
该研究表明,新辅助纳武单抗联合卡铂和紫杉醇实现了显着的 pCR 率,这是 治疗 局部晚期可切除 SCCHN 的新发现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,这种联合疗法可以改善 HPV 阴性 SCCHN 患者的预后,可能为未来的 治疗 策略和临床试验提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项单臂试验,没有比较组,这限制了对 治疗 疗效得出明确结论的能力。

Neoadjuvant Nivolumab plus Carboplatin and Paclitaxel in Patients with Locally Advanced Resectable Squamous Cell Carcinoma of the Head and Neck: A Phase II, Single-Arm Trial.

Authors: Ralph G Zinner, Eric V. Mastrolonardo, Jennifer Maria Johnson, Kathryn L. Nunes, Pablo Llerena, Zachary T. Elliott, Madalina Tuluc, Joseph M Curry, Christopher E Fundakowski, Andrew Yampolsky
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Purpose: In locally advanced resectable squamous cell carcinoma of the head and neck (SCCHN), patients treated with neoadjuvant chemotherapy with major pathologic response (MPR) or pathologic complete response (pCR) have overall survival (OS) rates superior to those of patients with poorer pathologic responses. To improve efficacy, we added the PD-1 inhibitor nivolumab to neoadjuvant chemotherapy and evaluated the combination. Patients and Methods: In this single-arm, open-label phase II trial, patients with newly diagnosed stage III to IV human papillomavirus (HPV)–negative SCCHN in the oral cavity, oropharynx, hypopharynx, and larynx or stage II to III HPV-positive oropharyngeal SCCHN received neoadjuvant carboplatin and paclitaxel (6 weeks) plus nivolumab (every other week), followed by surgery and adjuvant radiotherapy ± chemotherapy. The primary endpoint was pCR at the primary site. Results: Thirty-four patients were enrolled and received neoadjuvant therapy. Thirty-three patients received surgery (R0 resection = 100%). Disease sites included the oral cavity (79%), oropharynx (12%), hypopharynx (6%), and larynx (3%). Twenty-eight (85%) patients had stage IVA disease. The median postsurgical follow-up was 35.3 months. Fourteen (41%) patients had grade 3/4 treatment-related adverse events. Twenty-seven (82%) patients completed all cycles of neoadjuvant therapy. Twenty-four (73%) patients had at least an MPR at the primary site, and 15 (45%) had a pCR. In an unplanned analysis at 3 years, recurrence-free survival and OS were 74% and 83%, respectively. Conclusions: This phase II trial of neoadjuvant nivolumab plus carboplatin and paclitaxel in previously untreated, locally advanced, resectable SCCHN was well tolerated and reached its primary endpoint of pCR at the primary site. A phase III confirmatory study is warranted in advanced-stage, resectable HPV-negative SCCHN. See related commentary by Anderson and Sikora, p.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In locally advanced resectable squamous cell carcinoma of the head and neck (SCCHN), patients treated with neoadjuvant chemotherapy with major pathologic response (MPR) or pathologic complete response (pCR) have overall survival (OS) rates superior to those of patients with poorer pathologic responses.
Design and population
This single-arm, open-label phase II trial evaluated neoadjuvant nivolumab plus carboplatin and paclitaxel in patients with newly diagnosed stage III to IV human papillomavirus (HPV)–negative SCCHN in the oral cavity, oropharynx, hypopharynx, and larynx or stage II to III HPV-positive oropharyngeal SCCHN.
Core methods
Patients received neoadjuvant carboplatin and paclitaxel (6 weeks) plus nivolumab (every other week), followed by surgery and adjuvant radiotherapy ± chemotherapy. The primary endpoint was pCR at the primary site.
Main results
Thirty-four patients were enrolled and received neoadjuvant therapy. Twenty-four (73%) patients had at least an MPR at the primary site, and 15 (45%) had a pCR. Twenty-seven (82%) patients completed all cycles of neoadjuvant therapy.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that the combination of neoadjuvant nivolumab with carboplatin and paclitaxel achieved a significant pCR rate, which is a novel finding in the treatment of locally advanced resectable SCCHN.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this combination therapy could improve outcomes for patients with HPV-negative SCCHN, potentially informing future treatment strategies and clinical trials.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a single-arm trial with no comparator group, which limits the ability to draw definitive conclusions about the efficacy of the treatment.

生产力还是变革? HPV DNA 和 E6/E7 转录本的上皮区室化如何影响宫颈筛查检测性能。

Productive or Transforming? How Epithelial Compartmentalization of HPV DNA and E6/E7 Transcripts Shapes Cervical Screening Assay Performance.
作者： Konstanze Schichl, John Doorbar
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 生命周期与上皮细胞分化相关,病毒 基因组 扩增、E6/E7 转录和病毒体组装在宫颈上皮内空间划分。该组织在病变转化过程中被破坏。这些基本观察结果决定了我们能够对宫颈脱落物进行“大量”核酸扩增测试(NAAT)。基于 DNA 的检测可灵敏地报告 高危型HPV 基因组 的存在,其中包括短暂感染和病毒颗粒。基于 RNA 的检测以转录活跃疾病中的 E6/E7 转录本为目标,这解释了它们对高级鳞状上皮内病变的临床特异性略高。然而,没有单一的检测方法可以同时定义病毒的存在和疾病的重要性。细胞学、p16/Ki-67 双染色、扩展基因分型和 宿主 细胞甲基化以强有力的生物学原理询问风险的互补轴。空间信息的丢失限制了批量 NAAT 方法,这在混合级别病变和免疫功能低下人群中最为重要。新兴的空间方法、人工智能辅助细胞学和宫颈细胞提升术可恢复生物分辨率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解宫颈上皮细胞内 HPV DNA 和 E6/E7 转录本的空间划分如何影响宫颈筛查检测的性能。
设计与对象
该研究的重点是宫颈上皮内 HPV 的组织,检查其对病变转化的破坏及其对诊断方法的影响。
核心方法
该研究分析了 HPV DNA 和 E6/E7 转录本的空间分布,将检测病毒存在的基于 DNA 的检测与针对转录活跃疾病的基于 RNA 的检测进行了比较。
主要结果
基于 DNA 的检测可灵敏地报告 高危型HPV 基因组 的存在,包括短暂感染和病毒颗粒,而基于 RNA 的检测对高级鳞状上皮内病变具有稍高的临床特异性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,上皮内 HPV 的空间组织决定了批量核酸扩增测试的有效性,强调需要补充方法来捕获生物学分辨率。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了空间信息在评估 HPV 相关疾病风险中的重要性,为更准确的宫颈筛查测定的开发提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认批量 NAAT 方法会丢失空间信息,这在混合级别病变和免疫功能低下人群中最为关键,这表明由于其仅抽象的性质,证据强度适中。

Productive or Transforming? How Epithelial Compartmentalization of HPV DNA and E6/E7 Transcripts Shapes Cervical Screening Assay Performance.

Authors: Konstanze Schichl, John Doorbar
Original abstract (English)
The human papillomavirus (HPV) life cycle is linked to epithelial cell differentiation, with viral genome amplification, E6/E7 transcription, and virion assembly spatially compartmentalized within the cervical epithelium. This organization is disrupted in transforming lesions. These fundamental observations determine what 'bulk' nucleic acid amplification tests (NAATs) of cervical exfoliates allow us to establish. DNA-based assays sensitively report high-risk HPV genome presence, which includes transient infections and virus particles. RNA-based assays target the E6/E7 transcripts in transcriptionally active disease, which accounts for their slightly higher clinical specificity for high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions. No single assay defines both viral presence and disease significance, however. Cytology, p16/Ki-67 dual stain, extended genotyping and host-cell methylation interrogate complementary axes of risk with strong biological rationale. The loss of spatial information that limits bulk NAAT approaches matters most in mixed-grade lesions and immunocompromised populations. Emerging spatial methods, AI-assisted cytology and cervical cell-lifts offer recovery of biological resolution.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how the spatial compartmentalization of HPV DNA and E6/E7 transcripts within cervical epithelial cells influences the performance of cervical screening assays.
Design and population
The research focused on the organization of HPV within cervical epithelium, examining its disruption in transforming lesions and its implications for diagnostic methods.
Core methods
The study analyzed the spatial distribution of HPV DNA and E6/E7 transcripts, comparing DNA-based assays that detect viral presence with RNA-based assays that target transcriptionally active disease.
Main results
DNA-based assays sensitively report high-risk HPV genome presence, including transient infections and virus particles, while RNA-based assays have slightly higher clinical specificity for high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the spatial organization of HPV within the epithelium determines the effectiveness of bulk nucleic acid amplification tests, highlighting the need for complementary approaches to capture biological resolution.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of more accurate cervical screening assays by emphasizing the importance of spatial information in assessing HPV-related disease risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges the loss of spatial information in bulk NAAT approaches, which is most critical in mixed-grade lesions and immunocompromised populations, suggesting moderate evidence strength due to its abstract-only nature.

1990-2023 年 宫颈癌 的全球、区域和国家负担以及到 2050 年的预测:全球疾病负担研究。

Global, regional and national burden of cervical cancer in 1990-2023 with forecasts to 2050: a Global Burden of Disease study.
作者： GBD 2023 Cervical Cancer Collaborators., Natalie Pritchett, Miranda L. May, Jonathan M. Kocarnik, Kayleigh Bhangdia, Louise Penberthy, Bhoomadevi A, Hasan Aalruz, Hazim S. Ababneh, Hedayat Abbastabar
摘要中文翻译尽管高度可预防,宫颈癌仍然是全球女性中最常见的恶性肿瘤之一。作为全球疾病负担研究的一部分,我们估计了宫颈癌、发病率和失能调整生命年(DALYs)在1990年至2023年间以及204个国家和地区,对未来至2050年进行了预测。2023年,全球估计有887,000例新发病例(95%不确定性区间为682,000-1,170,000),369,000例死亡(292,000-475,000)以及13.3百万DALYs(10.1-17.4)。年龄标准化的发病率率(ASIR)和年龄标准化的死亡率率(ASMR)在低收入国家比高收入国家高出四倍和七倍以上。1990年至2023年间,全球ASIR基本保持稳定(-0.4% [-26.6到34.7]),而ASMR变化为-17.5% [-36.2到13.4]。所有宫颈癌的DALYs均可归因于人乳头瘤病毒感染,全球烟草使用贡献了5.9%(3.2-9.3)。到2050年,全球ASIR和ASMR预计将分别比2024年下降12.5%(-25.7到3.5)和9.0%(-21.5到6.6),但绝对病例数和死亡数预计将分别上升20.2%(4.6-38.5)和39.3%(23.6-57.3)。ASIR和ASMR预计仍将保持在低收入国家最高。需要加强行动以加快进展。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管 宫颈癌 是高度可预防的,但它仍然是全世界女性中最常见的恶性肿瘤之一。该研究旨在估计 1990 年至 2023 年 宫颈癌 的全球、区域和国家负担,并预测到 2050 年。
设计与对象
该研究是全球疾病负担研究的一部分,估算了 1990 年至 2023 年 204 个国家和地区的 宫颈癌、发病率、死亡率 和伤残调整生命年 (DALY),并预测到 2050 年。
核心方法
该研究采用综合方法来估计 宫颈癌 负担,包括从多个来源收集数据和统计模型来预测 2050 年之前的趋势。
主要结果
2023年,全球估计有887,000例新发病例(95%不确定性区间为682,000-1,170,000),369,000例死亡(292,000-475,000)以及13.3百万DALYs(10.1-17.4)。年龄标准化的发病率率(ASIR)和年龄标准化的死亡率率(ASMR)在低收入国家比高收入国家高出四倍和七倍以上。
解释与创新
作者解释说,宫颈癌 的负担在 发病率 方面基本稳定,但在 死亡率 中有所下降,所有 DALY 均归因于 人乳头瘤病毒 感染。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要加强行动来解决 宫颈癌,特别是在负担仍然最高的低收入国家。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对汇总数据和预测的依赖的限制,可能存在不确定性。由于这些限制,证据被认为是中等的。

Global, regional and national burden of cervical cancer in 1990-2023 with forecasts to 2050: a Global Burden of Disease study.

Authors: GBD 2023 Cervical Cancer Collaborators., Natalie Pritchett, Miranda L. May, Jonathan M. Kocarnik, Kayleigh Bhangdia, Louise Penberthy, Bhoomadevi A, Hasan Aalruz, Hazim S. Ababneh, Hedayat Abbastabar
Original abstract (English)
Although highly preventable, cervical cancer remains one of the most common malignancies among women worldwide. As part of the Global Burden of Disease study, we estimated cervical cancer incidence, mortality and disability-adjusted life-years (DALYs) across 204 countries and territories from 1990 to 2023, with forecasts to 2050. In 2023, 887,000 new cases (95% uncertainty interval 682,000-1,170,000), 369,000 deaths (292,000-475,000) and 13.3 million DALYs (10.1-17.4) were estimated globally. Age-standardized incidence rate (ASIR) and age-standardized mortality rates (ASMR) were more than four and seven times higher in low- versus high-income countries. Between 1990 and 2023, global ASIR was largely stable (-0.4% (-26.6 to 34.7)), whereas ASMR changed by -17.5% (-36.2 to 13.4). All cervical cancer DALYs were attributable to human papillomavirus infection, with tobacco use contributing 5.9% (3.2-9.3) globally. By 2050, global ASIR and ASMR are forecast to change by -12.5% (-25.7 to 3.5) and -9.0% (-21.5 to 6.6) from 2024, yet absolute cases and deaths are forecast to rise by 20.2% (4.6-38.5) and 39.3% (23.6-57.3). ASIR and ASMR are forecast to remain highest in low-income countries. Intensified action is needed to accelerate progress.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains one of the most common malignancies among women worldwide, despite being highly preventable. The study aimed to estimate the global, regional and national burden of cervical cancer in 1990-2023 with forecasts to 2050.
Design and population
The study was part of the Global Burden of Disease study, estimating cervical cancer incidence, mortality and disability-adjusted life-years (DALYs) across 204 countries and territories from 1990 to 2023, with forecasts to 2050.
Core methods
The study used a comprehensive approach to estimate cervical cancer burden, including data collection from multiple sources and statistical modeling to forecast trends up to 2050.
Main results
In 2023, 887,000 new cases (95% uncertainty interval 682,000-1,170,000), 369,000 deaths (292,000-475,000) and 13.3 million DALYs (10.1-17.4) were estimated globally. Age-standardized incidence rate (ASIR) and age-standardized mortality rates (ASMR) were more than four and seven times higher in low- versus high-income countries.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the burden of cervical cancer is largely stable in terms of incidence but has decreased in mortality, with all DALYs attributable to human papillomavirus infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for intensified action to address cervical cancer, particularly in low-income countries where the burden remains highest.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on aggregated data and forecasts, which may have uncertainties. The evidence is considered moderate due to these limitations.

人乳头瘤病毒 在口咽鳞状细胞癌 发病机制 中的作用以及 疫苗接种 对疾病预防的影响:叙述性综述

The role of human papillomavirus in the pathogenesis of oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma and the impact of vaccination on disease prevention: a narrative review
作者： Muonga Chiyokoma Kabaso, Mahati Iyyappan, Rahma Islam, Deethya Balamurali, Shogo Tajima, Ellie McDougall, Zunair Rahman
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述探讨了 HPV 发病机制 以及 疫苗接种 对疾病预防的影响。
证据基础与方法
方法 检索 PubMed、Embase 和 Medline 发表至 2025 年 12 月的文章,结合术语包括口咽癌、鳞状细胞癌、HPV、疫苗、发病机制 和磷酸肌醇 3 激酶。
共识与结论
结果 人乳头瘤病毒 16 E6 和 E7 癌蛋白降解 p53 和视网膜母细胞瘤蛋白,调节磷酸肌醇 3 激酶、蛋白激酶 B 和雷帕霉素信号传导的机制靶标,并导致基因组不稳定。
争议与缺口
p16 是一种有用但不完善的替代生物标志物。
科研与实践启示
疫苗接种 可以降低口腔 HPV 患病率,并且预计会降低 发病率,尽管男性和贫困地区的吸收率仍然不理想。

The role of human papillomavirus in the pathogenesis of oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma and the impact of vaccination on disease prevention: a narrative review

Authors: Muonga Chiyokoma Kabaso, Mahati Iyyappan, Rahma Islam, Deethya Balamurali, Shogo Tajima, Ellie McDougall, Zunair Rahman
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objective Oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma accounts for over a third of head and neck cancers, with the cost of treatment in the UK reaching £14.3 billion over 15 years. Human papillomavirus (HPV) has surpassed smoking and alcohol as the leading aetiological driver. This review examines HPV pathogenesis and the impact of vaccination on disease prevention. Methods PubMed, Embase and Medline were searched for articles published to December 2025, combining terms including oropharyngeal, squamous cell carcinoma, HPV, vaccines, pathogenesis and phosphoinositide 3 kinase. Gynaecological HPV malignancies were excluded, and reference lists were hand-searched. Results Human papillomavirus 16 E6 and E7 oncoproteins degrade p53 and retinoblastoma protein, dysregulate phosphoinositide 3 kinase, protein kinase B and mechanistic target of rapamycin signalling and drive genomic instability. p16 is a useful but imperfect surrogate biomarker. Vaccination reduces oral HPV prevalence and is projected to lower incidence, although uptake remains suboptimal among males and in deprived areas. Conclusion Gender-neutral vaccination, improved uptake and the development of therapeutic vaccines and phosphoinositide 3 kinase-targeted therapies offer realistic strategies to reduce disease burden.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review examines HPV pathogenesis and the impact of vaccination on disease prevention.
Evidence base and method
Methods PubMed, Embase and Medline were searched for articles published to December 2025, combining terms including oropharyngeal, squamous cell carcinoma, HPV, vaccines, pathogenesis and phosphoinositide 3 kinase.
Consensus and conclusions
Results Human papillomavirus 16 E6 and E7 oncoproteins degrade p53 and retinoblastoma protein, dysregulate phosphoinositide 3 kinase, protein kinase B and mechanistic target of rapamycin signalling and drive genomic instability.
Controversies and gaps
p16 is a useful but imperfect surrogate biomarker.
Research and practice implications
Vaccination reduces oral HPV prevalence and is projected to lower incidence, although uptake remains suboptimal among males and in deprived areas.

治疗 与咪喹莫特和 Polyphenon E 治疗尖锐湿疣的复发率:系统评价。

Recurrence Rate of Genital Warts Between Treatment With Imiquimod and Polyphenon E: A Systematic Review.
作者： Fatemeh Rashidi, Hedyeh Riazi, Masoumeh Simbar, Mahrokh Dolatian, Ali Montazeri
摘要中文翻译目的:尖锐湿疣是 人乳头瘤病毒 的常见表现,通常采用 5% 咪喹莫特或 Polyphenon E(10% 或 15%)治疗,两者的复发率不同。本系统评价旨在调查使用这些药物 治疗 后尖锐湿疣的复发率。方法:本研究根据 PRISMA 指南系统地回顾了临床试验。截至 2025 年 3 月,我们使用关键词“生殖器疣”、“复发”、“咪喹莫特”和“Polyphenon E”以及布尔运算符“AND”和“OR”在电子数据库 PubMed、Web of Science、Scopus 和 Embase 中进行了全面检索。两名评审员使用 Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) 关键评估工具 (2023) 独立评估纳入试验的方法学质量。结果:在初步检索中确定的 605 项研究中,有 12 项符合本次系统评价的纳入标准。根据表格数据,在咪喹莫特 5% 研究中,复发率估计范围为 0% 至 28.6%;大多数研究报告的比例为 12.5%-19%,41% 的估计值来自唯一一项随访 12 个月的研究。在 Polyphenon E 研究中,10% 剂量的范围为 4.1%-11.8%,15% 剂量的范围为 6.5%-10.6%。结论:咪喹莫特 5% 和 Polyphenon E(10% 或 15%)的研究报告的复发率各不相同。由于证据是间接的且异质的,无法得出比较结论;需要进行面对面的试验。

Recurrence Rate of Genital Warts Between Treatment With Imiquimod and Polyphenon E: A Systematic Review.

Authors: Fatemeh Rashidi, Hedyeh Riazi, Masoumeh Simbar, Mahrokh Dolatian, Ali Montazeri
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: Genital warts, a frequent manifestation of human papillomavirus, are commonly treated with Imiquimod 5% or Polyphenon E (10% or 15%), which differ in recurrence rates. This systematic review aimed to investigate the recurrence rates of genital warts following treatment with these agents. METHODS: This study systematically reviewed clinical trials according to PRISMA guidelines. A comprehensive search was performed in the electronic databases PubMed, Web of Science, Scopus, and Embase up to the end of March 2025 using the keywords "genital warts," "recurrence," "Imiquimod," and "Polyphenon E," combined with the Boolean operators "AND" and "OR." Two reviewers independently assessed the methodological quality of included trials using the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) critical appraisal tool (2023). RESULTS: Of the 605 studies identified in the initial search, 12 met the inclusion criteria for this systematic review. Based on the tabulated data, in the Imiquimod 5% studies, recurrence estimates ranged from 0% to 28.6%; most studies reported 12.5%-19%, and the 41% estimate was from the only study with 12-month follow-up. In the Polyphenon E studies, the ranges were 4.1%-11.8% for the 10% dose and 6.5%-10.6% for the 15% dose. CONCLUSIONS: Reported recurrence rates varied across studies of imiquimod 5% and Polyphenon E (10% or 15%). Because the evidence was indirect and heterogeneous, no comparative conclusion can be drawn; head-to-head trials are needed.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Genital warts, a frequent manifestation of human papillomavirus, are commonly treated with Imiquimod 5% or Polyphenon E (10% or 15%), which differ in recurrence rates.
Evidence base and method
This study systematically reviewed clinical trials according to PRISMA guidelines. A comprehensive search was performed in the electronic databases PubMed, Web of Science, Scopus, and Embase up to the end of March 2025 using the keywords "genital warts," "recurrence," "Imiquimod," and "Polyphenon E," combined with the Boolean operators "AND" and "OR." Two reviewers independently assessed the methodological quality of included trials using the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) critical appraisal tool (2023).
Consensus and conclusions
Reported recurrence rates varied across studies of imiquimod 5% and Polyphenon E (10% or 15%).
Controversies and gaps
Because the evidence was indirect and heterogeneous, no comparative conclusion can be drawn; head-to-head trials are needed.
Research and practice implications
This systematic review aimed to investigate the recurrence rates of genital warts following treatment with these agents.

尼日利亚侵袭性 人乳头瘤病毒 女性的 人乳头瘤病毒 基因型:范围界定审查

Human Papillomavirus Genotypes Among Women With Invasive Cervical Cancer in Nigeria: A Scoping Review
作者： Nkiruka Obodoechina, Jennifer S. Smith, Peter Kalulu, Olufunto A. Olusanya, Onyekachukwu Anikamadu, Ucheoma Nwaozuru, Temitope Ojo, Ifeoluwatoolami Olaolorun, Folahanmi Tomiwa Akinsolu, Chika Kingsley Onwuamah
摘要中文翻译目的:侵袭性 宫颈癌 (ICC) 病例仍然是尼日利亚妇女的主要公共卫生问题。本次范围审查的目的是评估尼日利亚妇女 ICC 病例中常见 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 基因型的报告。方法:我们检索了五个数据库,包括 Embase、Ovid Medline、Scopus、Cochrane 系统评价数据库和 Google Scholar,以确定报告尼日利亚 ICC 病例中发现的 HPV 基因型的研究。研究选择和数据提取是根据系统评价的首选报告项目和范围界定评价指南的荟萃分析扩展进行的。两名研究人员从每篇符合条件的文章中提取数据。结果:共鉴定出 668 条记录,筛选出 422 篇研究,6 篇文章符合入选标准。纳入的研究报告了 ICC 在尼日利亚妇女中检测到的 HPV 基因型。在所有研究中,大约 50% 的 ICC 病例中发现了 高危型HPV (hrHPV) 16、18、35 和 45 型,而其他检测到的基因型包括 hrHPV 31、33、51 和 52 型。值得注意的是,除了全球主要的 hrHPV 16 和 18 型外,hrHPV 35 和 45 型也经常被检测到,表明它们的基因型尼日利亚妇女的国际刑事法院负担做出了重大贡献。结论:经过广泛搜索,本研究对尼日利亚 ICC 女性的总体 患病率 和 hrHPV 基因型分布进行了全面评估。在尼日利亚 ICC 中检测到的最常见 hrHPV 基因型为 16、18、35、45、31、33、51 和 52。hrHPV 16 和 18 型占大多数病例,但在尼日利亚和世界各地的非洲裔女性中,hrHPV 45 和 35 型在 ICC 病例中也显着存在。

Human Papillomavirus Genotypes Among Women With Invasive Cervical Cancer in Nigeria: A Scoping Review

Authors: Nkiruka Obodoechina, Jennifer S. Smith, Peter Kalulu, Olufunto A. Olusanya, Onyekachukwu Anikamadu, Ucheoma Nwaozuru, Temitope Ojo, Ifeoluwatoolami Olaolorun, Folahanmi Tomiwa Akinsolu, Chika Kingsley Onwuamah
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Invasive cervical cancer (ICC) cases remain a major public health concern for women in Nigeria. The purpose of this scoping review is to assess the reporting of common human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes found in ICC cases among Nigerian women. METHODS: We searched five databases, including Embase, Ovid Medline, Scopus, Cochrane Database of Systematic Reviews, and Google Scholar, to identify studies reporting HPV genotypes found in ICC cases in Nigeria. Study selection and data extraction were conducted in accordance with the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic reviews and Meta-Analyses extension for Scoping Reviews guidelines. Two researchers extracted data from each eligible article. RESULTS: A total of 668 records were identified, of which 422 studies were screened and six articles met the eligibility criteria. The included studies reported on HPV genotypes detected in ICC among Nigerian women. Across the studies, high-risk HPV (hrHPV) types 16, 18, 35, and 45 were identified in approximately 50% of ICC cases, whereas other detected genotypes included hrHPV types 31, 33, 51, and 52. Notably, in addition to globally predominant hrHPV types 16 and 18, hrHPV types 35 and 45 were also frequently detected, indicating their significant contribution to the ICC burden among Nigerian women. CONCLUSION: After an extensive search, this study provides a comprehensive assessment of the overall prevalence and distribution of hrHPV genotypes in Nigerian women with ICC. The most common hrHPV genotypes detected in ICC in Nigeria are 16, 18, 35, 45, 31, 33, 51, and 52. hrHPV types 16 and 18 account for the majority of cases, but in Nigeria and among women of African descent worldwide, hrHPV types 45 and 35 are also significantly present in ICC cases.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The purpose of this scoping review is to assess the reporting of common human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes found in ICC cases among Nigerian women.
Evidence base and method
Study selection and data extraction were conducted in accordance with the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic reviews and Meta-Analyses extension for Scoping Reviews guidelines.
Consensus and conclusions
The most common hrHPV genotypes detected in ICC in Nigeria are 16, 18, 35, 45, 31, 33, 51, and 52.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
hrHPV types 16 and 18 account for the majority of cases, but in Nigeria and among women of African descent worldwide, hrHPV types 45 and 35 are also significantly present in ICC cases.

人乳头瘤病毒 筛查项目中女性自我抽样的经验:范围界定审查

Women's Experience of Human Papillomavirus Self‐Sampling in Cervical Cancer Screening Programs: A Scoping Review
作者： Cecilia Gabriel Holst, Birgitte Nørgaard, Michael Marcussen, Brigitte Norgaard
摘要中文翻译摘要 本研究旨在绘制、识别和综合 人乳头瘤病毒 筛查计划中女性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 自我采样的经验。该研究作为范围界定审查进行,并根据 PRISMA-ScR 进行报告。纳入了涉及接受 HPV 自我采样的所有国籍女性的研究,采用任何定性研究设计,并在任何日期以英语或斯堪的纳维亚语言发表。于 2023 年 5 月、2024 年 9 月和 2025 年 8 月在 PubMed、CINAHL、PsycInfo 和 EMBASE 数据库中进行了系统检索。根据研究特征系统地绘制了数据图表,并以叙述方式报告了主要发现。总共纳入了 25 项报告定性或混合方法的研究。综合结果从女性的角度产生了三个主题:女性对 HPV 自我采样的看法、女性对卫生专业人员 HPV 采样的经验以及女性对社区 HPV 采样的看法。女性对自我取样的接受程度很高,因为她们有对自己身体的控制感、手术过程的舒适感和节省时间的好处。一些女性对自己采集样本缺乏信心。这种经历受到与卫生专业人员的关系的影响,例如,由于以前的创伤经历,以及妇女的直接社区,例如与其他妇女或伴侣的关系。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
摘要 本研究旨在绘制、识别和综合 人乳头瘤病毒 筛查计划中女性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 自我采样的经验。
证据基础与方法
该研究作为范围界定审查进行,并根据 PRISMA-ScR 进行报告。于2023年5月、2024年9月和2025年8月在PubMed、CINAHL、PsycInfo和EMBASE数据库中进行了系统检索。

总共纳入了 25 项报告定性或混合方法的研究。综合结果从女性的角度产生了三个主题:女性对 HPV 自我采样的看法、女性对卫生专业人员 HPV 采样的经验以及女性对社区 HPV 采样的看法。女性对自我取样的接受程度很高,因为她们有对自己身体的控制感、手术过程的舒适感和节省时间的好处。

科研与实践启示
该研究强调了解决女性问题并提供支持以增强她们对自我采样的信心的重要性。未来的研究应探讨不同抽样方法的影响以及卫生专业人员在改善女性体验方面的作用。

Women's Experience of Human Papillomavirus Self‐Sampling in Cervical Cancer Screening Programs: A Scoping Review

Authors: Cecilia Gabriel Holst, Birgitte Nørgaard, Michael Marcussen, Brigitte Norgaard
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT This study aimed to map, identify, and synthesize women's experiences with human papillomavirus (HPV) self‐sampling in cervical cancer screening programs. The study was conducted as a scoping review, reported in accordance with PRISMA‐ScR. Studies involving women of all nationalities who had been offered HPV self‐sampling were included, of any qualitative study design, and published at any date in either English or a Scandinavian language. Systematic searches were conducted in the PubMed, CINAHL, PsycInfo, and EMBASE databases in May 2023, in September 2024, and in August 2025. Data were systematically charted by study characteristics, and the main findings were reported narratively. A total of 25 studies reporting qualitative or mixed methods were included. The synthesis resulted in three themes generated from the women's perspective: Women's perceptions of HPV self‐sampling , Women's experiences of health professionals’ HPV sampling , and Women's perceptions of community HPV sampling . Women experienced self‐sampling to be highly acceptable with feelings of control over their own bodies, comfort in the procedure, and time‐saving benefits. Some women experienced a lack of confidence in taking the sample themselves. The experience was influenced by the relationship with health professionals, for example, due to previous traumatic experiences, and by the women's immediate community, for example, relationships with other women or partners.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
ABSTRACT This study aimed to map, identify, and synthesize women's experiences with human papillomavirus (HPV) self‐sampling in cervical cancer screening programs.
Evidence base and method
The study was conducted as a scoping review, reported in accordance with PRISMA‐ScR. Systematic searches were conducted in the PubMed, CINAHL, PsycInfo, and EMBASE databases in May 2023, in September 2024, and in August 2025.
Consensus and conclusions
A total of 25 studies reporting qualitative or mixed methods were included. The synthesis resulted in three themes generated from the women's perspective: Women's perceptions of HPV self‐sampling, Women's experiences of health professionals’ HPV sampling, and Women's perceptions of community HPV sampling. Women experienced self‐sampling to be highly acceptable with feelings of control over their own bodies, comfort in the procedure, and time‐saving benefits.
Controversies and gaps
Some women experienced a lack of confidence in taking the sample themselves. The experience was influenced by the relationship with health professionals, for example, due to previous traumatic experiences, and by the women's immediate community, for example, relationships with other women or partners.
Research and practice implications
The study highlights the importance of addressing women's concerns and providing support to enhance their confidence in self‐sampling. Future research should explore the impact of different sampling methods and the role of health professionals in improving women's experiences.

了解家庭教士如何预防人类乳头瘤病毒 (VPH),位于玛丽亚社区。尼加拉瓜马那瓜,2025 年第二学期 家长对尼加拉瓜马那瓜康塞普西翁德玛丽亚社区 疫苗 预防 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的看法 - 2025 年第二学期

Percepción de los padres de familia sobre la vacuna para la prevención del Virus del Papiloma Humano (VPH), en el Barrio Concepción de María. Managua, Nicaragua, II semestre de 2025 Parents ́ perception regarding the vaccine for the prevention of the Human Papillomavirus (HPV) in the Concepción de María neighborhood, Managua, Nicaragua - second semester of 2025
作者： Alejandro José Hurtado Hernández, Arellys Del Carmen Sotomayor Rivera, Magdyel Sarahí Jiménez Silva, Margarita Guzmán López
摘要中文翻译2025 年下半年,针对家长对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的看法的研究在马那瓜 Concepción de María 社区进行。该研究采用定量、描述性和横断面的方法,对大约 3,010 名居民进行了调查。选择了 93 名家长的方便样本。结果表明,主要年龄组在 31 岁至 35 岁之间,其中女性参与率较高,且文化程度为中学。关于 疫苗接种 覆盖率,82% 的女孩接种了 HPV 疫苗,尽管 52% 的女孩只接种了第一剂。人们对 疫苗 的积极看法普遍认为安全有效,而社会或宗教背景并未显示出对疫苗接种决定的重大影响。然而,人们仍然担心 疫苗接种 的潜在副作用和现有的神话,包括所谓的不孕或性活动过早发生。大多数家长认识到获得有关 疫苗 的充分信息的重要性,并表示有兴趣了解更多有关其效果、益处和给药时间表的信息。结果表明,教育和传播可靠的科学信息是减少误解的重要因素,直接有助于提高 HPV 疫苗 的接受度。

该研究采用定量、描述性和横断面的方法,对大约 3,010 名居民进行了调查。

Percepción de los padres de familia sobre la vacuna para la prevención del Virus del Papiloma Humano (VPH), en el Barrio Concepción de María. Managua, Nicaragua, II semestre de 2025 Parents ́ perception regarding the vaccine for the prevention of the Human Papillomavirus (HPV) in the Concepción de María neighborhood, Managua, Nicaragua - second semester of 2025

Authors: Alejandro José Hurtado Hernández, Arellys Del Carmen Sotomayor Rivera, Magdyel Sarahí Jiménez Silva, Margarita Guzmán López
Original abstract (English)
The research on parents' perceptions of the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine was conducted in the Concepción de María neighborhood of Managua during the second half of 2025. The study employed a quantitative, descriptive, and cross-sectional approach, surveying a population of approximately 3,010 residents. A convenience sample of 93 parents was selected. The results indicate that the predominant age group is between 31 and 35 years, with a higher participation rate among females and a secondary school education level. Regarding vaccination coverage, 82% of girls have been vaccinated against HPV, although 52% have only received the first dose. A positive perception of the vaccine, considered safe and effective, prevailed, while social or religious background did not show a significant influence on the decision to vaccinate. However, concerns persisted regarding potential side effects and existing myths about vaccination, including supposed infertility or early onset of sexual activity. Most parents recognized the importance of receiving adequate information about the vaccine and expressed interest in learning more about its effects, benefits, and administration schedule. The results reflect that education, and the dissemination of reliable scientific information are essential factors in reducing misconceptions, directly contributing to greater acceptance of the HPV vaccine.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The research on parents' perceptions of the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine was conducted in the Concepción de María neighborhood of Managua during the second half of 2025.
Evidence base and method
The study employed a quantitative, descriptive, and cross-sectional approach, surveying a population of approximately 3,010 residents.
Consensus and conclusions
A positive perception of the vaccine, considered safe and effective, prevailed, while social or religious background did not show a significant influence on the decision to vaccinate.
Controversies and gaps
Concerns persisted regarding potential side effects and existing myths about vaccination, including supposed infertility or early onset of sexual activity.
Research and practice implications
The results reflect that education, and the dissemination of reliable scientific information are essential factors in reducing misconceptions, directly contributing to greater acceptance of the HPV vaccine.

尼日利亚阿夸伊博姆州南部参议院区的看护者和青春期女孩对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 的知识、看法、疫苗 的吸收和报告的障碍

Knowledge, Perceptions, Vaccine Uptake, and Reported Barriers to Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccination Among Caregivers and Adolescent Girls in the Southern Senatorial District of Akwa Ibom State, Nigeria
作者： Stella Obot, Nelson Osuchukwu, Uduak E. Obot
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 仍然是尼日利亚的一个重大公共卫生挑战,宫颈癌 是女性中第二常见的癌症。疫苗接种 是一种行之有效的预防策略,但采用率仍然不理想。本研究调查了尼日利亚阿夸伊博姆州南部参议院区的护理人员和青春期女孩的知识、认知、疫苗 的吸收情况,并报告了 HPV 疫苗接种 的障碍。材料和方法:对通过多阶段随机抽样选出的 500 名受访者(250 名护理人员和 250 名青春期女孩)进行了描述性横断面调查。进行了结构化、经过验证的调查问卷。使用社会科学统计软件包 (SPSS) 第 23 版对数据进行分析,并使用卡方检验在 95% 置信水平下进行假设检验。结果:HPV知识水平较低:44.0%的照顾者和26.0%的青春期女孩听说过HPV,只有25.2%的照顾者和8.0%的青春期女孩表现出较高的知晓率。在了解 HPV 的人群中,22.7% 的护理人员和 35.4% 的青春期女孩接受了 疫苗。知识、感知或人口统计变量与 疫苗 吸收之间没有发现统计学上显着的关联(所有 p > 0.05)。护理人员面临的主要障碍包括成本高(58.2%)、无法获得服务(66.4%)、安全问题(35.5%)和需要更多信息(80.9%),这些问题采用了多种回答。结论:研究地区的护理人员和青春期女孩对 HPV 及其 疫苗 的了解普遍较差,尽管存在普遍积极的认识,但 疫苗 的吸收率仍然较低。迫切建议将 HPV 疫苗 纳入国家免疫规划、社区宣传和改善医疗保健提供者沟通。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 仍然是尼日利亚的一个重大公共卫生挑战,宫颈癌 是女性中第二常见的癌症。该研究旨在调查尼日利亚阿夸伊博姆州南部参议院区的护理人员和青春期女孩对 HPV 疫苗接种 的了解、看法、疫苗 的吸收情况以及报告的障碍。
证据基础与方法
对通过多阶段随机抽样选出的 500 名受访者(250 名护理人员和 250 名青春期女孩)进行了描述性横断面调查。该研究使用了经过验证的结构化调查问卷,并使用社会科学统计软件包 (SPSS) 23 版分析数据,并在 95% 的置信水平下进行了卡方检验。
共识与结论
对HPV的了解程度较低:44.0%的照顾者和26.0%的青春期女孩听说过HPV,只有25.2%的照顾者和8.0%的青春期女孩表现出较高的知晓率。在了解 HPV 的人群中,22.7% 的护理人员和 35.4% 的青春期女孩接受了 疫苗。知识、感知或人口统计变量与 疫苗 的吸收之间没有发现统计学上显着的关联。护理人员面临的主要障碍包括成本高(58.2%)、无法获得服务(66.4%)、安全问题(35.5%)和需要更多信息(80.9%)。
争议与缺口
研究发现,研究地区的护理人员和青春期女孩对 HPV 及其 疫苗 的了解普遍较差,尽管存在普遍积极的认识,但 疫苗 的采用率仍然很低。该研究没有发现知识、感知或人口统计变量与 疫苗 的吸收之间存在统计学上的显着关联,这表明其他因素可能会影响 疫苗 的吸收。
科研与实践启示
迫切建议将 HPV 疫苗 纳入国家免疫规划、社区宣传和改善医疗保健提供者沟通。这些措施有助于提高 疫苗 的使用率并提高公众对 HPV 及其 疫苗 的了解。

Knowledge, Perceptions, Vaccine Uptake, and Reported Barriers to Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccination Among Caregivers and Adolescent Girls in the Southern Senatorial District of Akwa Ibom State, Nigeria

Authors: Stella Obot, Nelson Osuchukwu, Uduak E. Obot
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) remains a significant public health challenge in Nigeria, where cervical cancer ranks as the second most frequent cancer among women. Vaccination is a proven preventive strategy, yet uptake remains suboptimal. This study examined the knowledge, perceptions, vaccine uptake, and reported barriers to HPV vaccination among caregivers and adolescent girls in the Southern Senatorial District of Akwa Ibom State, Nigeria. Materials and Methods: A descriptive cross-sectional survey was conducted among 500 respondents (250 caregivers and 250 adolescent girls) selected through multi-stage random sampling. A structured, validated questionnaire was administered. Data were analyzed using the Statistical Package for the Social Sciences (SPSS) version 23, and chi-square tests were used for hypothesis testing at the 95% confidence level. Results: Knowledge of HPV was low: 44.0% of caregivers and 26.0% of adolescent girls had heard of HPV, and only 25.2% of caregivers and 8.0% of adolescent girls demonstrated high knowledge. Among those aware of HPV, 22.7% of caregivers and 35.4% of adolescent girls had received the vaccine. No statistically significant associations were found between knowledge, perception, or demographic variables and vaccine uptake (all p > 0.05). Key barriers among caregivers included high cost (58.2%), unavailability (66.4%), safety concerns (35.5%), and need for more information (80.9%), which applied multiple responses. Conclusion: Knowledge of HPV and its vaccine is broadly poor among both caregivers and adolescent girls in the study area, and vaccine uptake remains low despite generally positive perceptions where awareness existed. Integration of the HPV vaccine into the national immunization programme, community sensitization, and improved healthcare provider communication are urgently recommended.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Human papillomavirus (HPV) remains a significant public health challenge in Nigeria, where cervical cancer ranks as the second most frequent cancer among women. The study aimed to examine the knowledge, perceptions, vaccine uptake, and reported barriers to HPV vaccination among caregivers and adolescent girls in the Southern Senatorial District of Akwa Ibom State, Nigeria.
Evidence base and method
A descriptive cross-sectional survey was conducted among 500 respondents (250 caregivers and 250 adolescent girls) selected through multi-stage random sampling. The study used a structured, validated questionnaire and analyzed data using the Statistical Package for the Social Sciences (SPSS) version 23, with chi-square tests at the 95% confidence level.
Consensus and conclusions
Knowledge of HPV was low: 44.0% of caregivers and 26.0% of adolescent girls had heard of HPV, and only 25.2% of caregivers and 8.0% of adolescent girls demonstrated high knowledge. Among those aware of HPV, 22.7% of caregivers and 35.4% of adolescent girls had received the vaccine. No statistically significant associations were found between knowledge, perception, or demographic variables and vaccine uptake. Key barriers among caregivers included high cost (58.2%), unavailability (66.4%), safety concerns (35.5%), and need for more information (80.9%).
Controversies and gaps
The study found that knowledge of HPV and its vaccine is broadly poor among both caregivers and adolescent girls in the study area, and vaccine uptake remains low despite generally positive perceptions where awareness existed. The study did not find statistically significant associations between knowledge, perception, or demographic variables and vaccine uptake, suggesting that other factors may influence vaccine uptake.
Research and practice implications
Integration of the HPV vaccine into the national immunization programme, community sensitization, and improved healthcare provider communication are urgently recommended. These measures could help increase vaccine uptake and improve public understanding of HPV and its vaccine.

De Pilotos Locais a Programas Escaláveis:在拉丁美洲 人乳头瘤病毒 实施癌症宫颈癌基地

De Pilotos Locais a Programas Escaláveis: Implementando o Rastreamento do Câncer Cervical Baseado em Human Papillomavirus na América Latina
作者： Renan Garcia Artus, Leonardo Storch, Pedro Melo, Pedro Henrique Ribeiro Teixeira, Rafael Amengual Duarte, Lucas Guimarães, Henrique Da Rocha Froncheti
摘要中文翻译目的 宫颈癌 是拉丁美洲女性癌症死亡的主要原因,该地区许多国家都属于低收入和中等收入国家,并且 发病率 和 死亡率 发病率是全球最高的。世卫组织启动了一项消除 宫颈癌 这一公共卫生问题的全球战略,设定了到 2030 年 90-70-90 的目标,其中包括在 35 岁和 45 岁通过高性能检测实现 70% 的筛查覆盖率,最好是 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 检测。这篇叙述性综述综合了拉丁美洲基于 HPV 的 宫颈癌 筛查的最新经验,重点介绍了当地试点和规划实施、关键障碍和促进因素,以及可能产生全球影响的教训。方法 我们利用 PubMed 和全球主要卫生资源,对 2010 年至 2026 年拉丁美洲国家有关 HPV 筛查的出版物和报告进行了叙述性回顾。我们优先考虑描述现实世界实施或大型研究平台的研究,例如国际癌症研究机构主导的ESTAMPA研究、阿根廷和墨西哥的计划性HPV检测以及厄瓜多尔农村的自我采样举措,包括对障碍、促进因素和可接受性的定量和定性评估。结果 在整个拉丁美洲,HPV 检测已通过 ESTAMPA 等举措大规模引入或进行了大型试点,该计划评估多个地点有组织的基于 HPV 的筛查和分诊策略,并为扩大规模的方法提供信息。阿根廷是该地区的先行者:胡胡伊省是第一个实施 HPV 检测作为初级筛查方法的国家,其中包括社区卫生工作者自行采集,目前已实施第二轮 HPV 筛查。九个拉丁美洲国家的经验表明,HPV 检测是可行的,并且是减少 宫颈癌 负担的有前途的工具,尽管较高的检测成本和卫生系统的限制仍然是挑战。在厄瓜多尔农村地区,诊断研究表明,阴道和尿液自我采样的敏感性和特异性与临床医生采集的样本相当,而社区试点报告的可接受性很高,具有舒适、隐私和节省时间等明显优势。结论 拉丁美洲的经验表明,基于 HPV 的筛查和自我采样可以在不同的卫生系统中有效实施,特别是在与初级保健相结合并得到社区外展支持时。这些当地的实施“实验室”揭示了常见的障碍和关键的促进因素,并为其他环境提供了实践经验教训。

范围与问题
这篇叙述性综述综合了拉丁美洲基于 HPV 的 宫颈癌 筛查的最新经验,重点介绍了当地试点和规划实施、主要障碍和促进因素,以及具有全球影响潜力的经验教训。

方法 我们利用 PubMed 和全球主要卫生资源,对 2010 年至 2026 年拉丁美洲国家有关 HPV 筛查的出版物和报告进行了叙述性回顾。
共识与结论
结果 在整个拉丁美洲,HPV 检测已通过 ESTAMPA 等举措大规模引入或进行了大型试点,该计划评估多个地点有组织的基于 HPV 的筛查和分诊策略,并为扩大规模的方法提供信息。

九个拉丁美洲国家的经验表明,HPV 检测是可行的,并且是减少 宫颈癌 负担的有前途的工具,尽管较高的检测成本和卫生系统的限制仍然是挑战。

De Pilotos Locais a Programas Escaláveis: Implementando o Rastreamento do Câncer Cervical Baseado em Human Papillomavirus na América Latina

Authors: Renan Garcia Artus, Leonardo Storch, Pedro Melo, Pedro Henrique Ribeiro Teixeira, Rafael Amengual Duarte, Lucas Guimarães, Henrique Da Rocha Froncheti
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE Cervical cancer is a leading cause of cancer death among women in Latin America, where many countries are classified as low- and middle-income and experience some of the highest incidence and mortality rates globally. The WHO has launched a global strategy to eliminate cervical cancer as a public health problem, setting 90-70-90 targets by 2030, including 70% screening coverage with a high-performance test, preferably human papillomavirus (HPV) testing, at ages 35 and 45. This narrative review synthesizes recent experience with HPV-based cervical cancer screening in Latin America, highlighting local pilots and programmatic implementations, key barriers and facilitators, and lessons with potential for global impact. METHODS We conducted a narrative review of publications and reports from 2010 to 2026 on HPV-based screening in Latin American countries, using PubMed and major global health sources. We prioritized studies describing real-world implementation or large research platforms such as the International Agency for Research on Cancer-led ESTAMPA study, programmatic HPV testing in Argentina and Mexico, and self-sampling initiatives in rural Ecuador, including quantitative and qualitative assessments of barriers, facilitators, and acceptability. RESULTS Across Latin America, HPV testing has been introduced at scale or in large pilots through initiatives such as ESTAMPA, which evaluates organized HPV-based screening and triage strategies in multiple sites and informs approaches for scale-up. Argentina has been a regional pioneer: the province of Jujuy was the first to implement HPV testing as the primary screening method, including community health worker–delivered self-collection, and has now implemented a second round of HPV-based screening. Experience from nine Latin American countries shows that HPV testing is feasible and a promising tool to reduce cervical cancer burden, although higher test costs and health system constraints remain challenges. In rural Ecuador, diagnostic studies showed that vaginal and urine self-sampling have sensitivity and specificity comparable to clinician-collected samples, while community-based pilots reported high acceptability, with perceived advantages such as comfort, privacy, and time savings. CONCLUSION Latin American experiences demonstrate that HPV-based screening and self-sampling can be effectively implemented in diverse health systems, particularly when integrated with primary care and supported by community outreach. These local “laboratories” of implementation reveal common barriers and key facilitators and offer practical lessons for other settings.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This narrative review synthesizes recent experience with HPV-based cervical cancer screening in Latin America, highlighting local pilots and programmatic implementations, key barriers and facilitators, and lessons with potential for global impact.
Evidence base and method
METHODS We conducted a narrative review of publications and reports from 2010 to 2026 on HPV-based screening in Latin American countries, using PubMed and major global health sources.
Consensus and conclusions
RESULTS Across Latin America, HPV testing has been introduced at scale or in large pilots through initiatives such as ESTAMPA, which evaluates organized HPV-based screening and triage strategies in multiple sites and informs approaches for scale-up.
Controversies and gaps
Experience from nine Latin American countries shows that HPV testing is feasible and a promising tool to reduce cervical cancer burden, although higher test costs and health system constraints remain challenges.
Research and practice implications
CONCLUSION Latin American experiences demonstrate that HPV-based screening and self-sampling can be effectively implemented in diverse health systems, particularly when integrated with primary care and supported by community outreach.

HPV 相关口咽癌:流行病学特征的变化和预后影响

HPV-Associated oropharyngeal cancer: Changes in epidemiological profile and prognostic implications
作者： Marina Ribeiro Fernandes Oliveira, Mariana Alves Lima, Rachel Vainchelboim
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌改变了 流行病学 和头颈癌的预后解释。这篇叙述性批判性评论探讨了 发病率 和人口分布的变化、HPV 相关肿瘤分类的有效性,以及病毒状态对生存和 治疗 决策的影响。截至 2026 年,PubMed/MEDLINE 索引的文献与专业指南和机构癌症预防资源一起进行了审查。基于人群的研究表明,在一些高收入环境中,HPV 相关口咽癌的发病率显着增加,其中老年男性的负担日益重要,而各地区的估计值仍然存在差异。HPV 阳性肿瘤通常比 HPV 阴性肿瘤具有更好的生存率,但吸烟暴露、疾病程度以及 p16 和 HPV 特异性检测之间的不一致改变了这一优势。分期修订承认了不同的疾病表型,包括 2026 年引入的改进。随机试验表明,良好的预后不能证明不加区别地降低 治疗 的合理性,尽管选定的术后去强化方法很有希望。疫苗接种 可减少 疫苗 型口腔 HPV 感染,而对口咽癌的影响则需要更长时间的观察。预后评估应整合病毒归因、解剖分期、患者特征、治疗 质量和长期功能结果。

HPV-Associated oropharyngeal cancer: Changes in epidemiological profile and prognostic implications

Authors: Marina Ribeiro Fernandes Oliveira, Mariana Alves Lima, Rachel Vainchelboim
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma has altered the epidemiology and prognostic interpretation of head and neck cancer. This narrative critical review examines changes in incidence and demographic distribution, the validity of HPV-related tumor classification, and the implications of viral status for survival and treatment decisions. Literature indexed in PubMed/MEDLINE through 2026 was examined alongside professional guidelines and institutional cancer-prevention resources. Population-based studies document a marked increase in HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer in several high-income settings, with an increasingly important burden among older men, while estimates remain heterogeneous across regions. HPV-positive tumors generally have more favorable survival than HPV-negative tumors, but smoking exposure, disease extent, and discordance between p16 and HPV-specific tests modify this advantage. Staging revisions acknowledge the distinct disease phenotype, including refinements introduced in 2026. Randomized trials demonstrate that favorable prognosis cannot justify indiscriminate treatment reduction, although selected postoperative de-intensification approaches are promising. Vaccination reduces vaccine-type oral HPV infection, whereas an effect on incident oropharyngeal cancer will require longer observation. Prognostic assessment should integrate viral attribution, anatomical stage, patient characteristics, treatment quality, and long-term functional outcomes.
View review five-element analysis

老年男性肛门生殖器疣:临床和公共卫生观点

Anogenital Warts in an Elderly Male: Clinical and Public Health Perspectives
作者： Pati Aji Achdiat, Rifqy Wahyu Mochamad Ihsan, Laila Tsaqilah, Oki Suwarsa, Diah Puspitosari, Debora Natalia Ndaparoka, Retno Hesty Maharani
摘要中文翻译摘要: 肛门生殖器疣(AGWS)是由 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)引起的,是最常见的性传播感染(STI)之一。尽管 AGWS 在年轻人中更常见,但老年人病例的报道也越来越多。一名 76 岁男性,生殖器部位(阴茎基部、阴囊和左侧腹股沟皱襞)出现逐渐增大的棕色丘疹。组织病理学检查显示表皮增生、乳头状瘤病、棘皮症和空细胞增多。PCR检查证实低危HPV 6型和11型。患者自述有多个性伴侣史,性交时未使用安全套,根据问卷调查,对性传播感染传播的了解有限。该患者接受了冷冻疗法和病灶切除术。在最终观察时,病变完全消退,没有观察到新的病变。总之,该病例强调了保持老年人对 AGW 临床认识的重要性。全面的诊断评估、性健康教育、定期性传播感染筛查和咨询应纳入老年人的日常护理中。关键词:肛门生殖器疣,人乳头瘤病毒,老年人,性传播感染,性传播感染知识,公共卫生
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 引起的肛门生殖器疣 (AGWS),并检查其在老年男性人群中的发生情况。核心问题是 AGWS 对老年人的临床和公共卫生意义,特别是与性行为、传播 知识和 治疗 结果相关的意义。
证据基础与方法
该综述基于一名 76 岁 AGWS 男性的单一案例研究。证据包括临床表现、组织病理学结果、HPV 6 型和 11 型 PCR 确认以及患者报告的性行为和知识。所使用的方法是描述性的,并且基于直接的患者评估和诊断测试。
共识与结论
原始证据未报告
争议与缺口
该审查不报告争议或相互矛盾的发现。然而,它强调了在了解老年人群中 AGWS 的 患病率 和 传播 动态以及该人群中预防措施的有效性方面存在潜在的证据差距。
科研与实践启示
该审查建议将彻底的诊断评估、性健康教育和定期性传播感染筛查纳入老年人的日常护理中。这也意味着需要对 流行病学 和老年人 AGWS 的预防进行进一步研究。

Anogenital Warts in an Elderly Male: Clinical and Public Health Perspectives

Authors: Pati Aji Achdiat, Rifqy Wahyu Mochamad Ihsan, Laila Tsaqilah, Oki Suwarsa, Diah Puspitosari, Debora Natalia Ndaparoka, Retno Hesty Maharani
Original abstract (English)
Abstract: Anogenital warts (AGWS), caused by human papillomavirus (HPV), are among the most common sexually transmitted infections (STIs). Although AGWS is more commonly found in younger adults, cases in older individuals are increasingly being reported. A 76-year-old man presented with progressively enlarging brown papules in the genital area (penile base, scrotum, and left inguinal fold). Histopathological examination showed epidermal hyperplasia, papillomatosis, acanthosis, and koilocytosis. PCR examination confirmed low-risk HPV types 6 and 11. The patient reported a history of multiple sexual partners, did not use condoms during sexual intercourse, and had limited knowledge regarding STIs transmission based on a questionnaire. The patient was treated with cryotherapy and lesion excision. At the final observation, complete lesion resolution was achieved with no new lesions observed. In conclusion, this case highlights the importance of maintaining clinical awareness of AGWs in older adults. Thorough diagnostic evaluation, sexual health education, regular STIs screening, and counseling should be integrated into routine care for the elderly population. Keywords: anogenital warts, human papillomavirus, elderly, sexually transmitted infections, STIs knowledge, public health
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on anogenital warts (AGWS), which are caused by human papillomavirus (HPV), and examines their occurrence in an elderly male population. The central question is the clinical and public health significance of AGWS in older adults, particularly in relation to sexual behavior, transmission knowledge, and treatment outcomes.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on a single case study of a 76-year-old man with AGWS. The evidence includes clinical presentation, histopathological findings, PCR confirmation of HPV types 6 and 11, and patient-reported sexual behavior and knowledge. The methods used are descriptive and based on direct patient evaluation and diagnostic testing.
Consensus and conclusions
The case study confirms that AGWS can occur in elderly individuals, with PCR identifying low-risk HPV types 6 and 11. The patient's history of multiple sexual partners and lack of condom use is associated with the condition, and treatment with cryotherapy and lesion excision resulted in complete resolution. The review concludes that AGWS should be considered in older adults and emphasizes the need for clinical awareness.
Controversies and gaps
The review does not report controversies or conflicting findings. However, it highlights a potential evidence gap in understanding the prevalence and transmission dynamics of AGWS in elderly populations, as well as the effectiveness of preventive measures in this demographic.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests integrating thorough diagnostic evaluation, sexual health education, and regular STI screening into routine care for the elderly. It also implies the need for further research on the epidemiology and prevention of AGWS in older adults.

HPV 相关的口咽非唾液腺癌

HPV-Associated Non-Salivary Adenocarcinoma of the Oropharynx
作者： Chayanan Puriwat, Brendan Conn, Nora Katabi, Anne Chambers, Charlotte C. Currie, Doreen N. Palsgrove, Lisa M. Rooper, William H. Westra, Justin A. Bishop, Paris Tamiolakis
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关的口咽非唾液腺癌极为罕见且定义不明确。我们报告了来自 6 个三级中心的 12 例多机构系列病例。对组织学、免疫组织化学、HPV 检测、分子和随访数据进行了审查。在有可用性别数据的 11 名患者中,全部为男性;中位年龄为 62 岁(范围:46 岁至 70 岁)。队列研究 包括 9 例原发性口咽腺癌和 3 例转移性腺癌,其中包括假定口咽起源的右颈转移瘤和先前记录的 HPV 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌患者的 2 例远处转移瘤。主要部位包括腭扁桃体或扁桃体腭区 (n=7)、舌根 (n=1) 和口咽,未另有说明 (n=1)。在具有 T 类别的病例中,6 例中有 5 例为 T3/T4。所有肿瘤均显示高级腺癌形态,没有可识别的鳞状成分,具有浸润性腺体、导管或筛状结构。所有病例均存在弥漫性p16表达,并通过聚合酶链式反应或原位杂交证实了高危型HPV。在病例 1 至 6、8、9、11 和 12 中,p40 在肿瘤细胞群中呈阴性,而病例 7 显示 p40 和 CK5/6 表达仅限于近腔细胞。2 例病例的分子谱分析发现了涉及 PIK3CA 和 PIK3R1 的改变或变异。对 6 名原发性肿瘤患者的随访显示,3 名患者出现远处转移,1 名患者出现疾病特异性死亡。HPV 相关的口咽非唾液腺癌是一种诊断上具有挑战性的肿瘤,它扩大了 HPV 相关口咽癌的形态谱。识别需要排除唾液型和转移性腺癌并确认 HPV 相关性。

范围与问题
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关的口咽非唾液腺癌极为罕见且定义不明确。
