共识与结论
所有肿瘤均显示高级腺癌形态,没有可识别的鳞状成分,具有浸润性腺体、导管或筛状结构。所有病例均存在弥漫性p16表达,并通过聚合酶链式反应或原位杂交证实了高危型HPV。对 6 名原发性肿瘤患者的随访显示,3 名患者出现远处转移,1 名患者出现疾病特异性死亡。
争议与缺口
在具有 T 类别的病例中,6 例中有 5 例为 T3/T4。在病例 1 至 6、8、9、11 和 12 中,p40 在肿瘤细胞群中呈阴性,而病例 7 显示 p40 和 CK5/6 表达仅限于近腔细胞。
科研与实践启示
HPV 相关的口咽非唾液腺癌是一种诊断上具有挑战性的肿瘤,它扩大了 HPV 相关口咽癌的形态谱。识别需要排除唾液型和转移性腺癌并确认 HPV 相关性。

HPV-Associated Non-Salivary Adenocarcinoma of the Oropharynx

Authors: Chayanan Puriwat, Brendan Conn, Nora Katabi, Anne Chambers, Charlotte C. Currie, Doreen N. Palsgrove, Lisa M. Rooper, William H. Westra, Justin A. Bishop, Paris Tamiolakis
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated non-salivary adenocarcinoma of the oropharynx is exceptionally rare and poorly defined. We report a multi-institutional series of 12 cases from 6 tertiary centers. Histologic, immunohistochemical, HPV testing, molecular, and follow-up data were reviewed. Among 11 patients with available sex data, all were men; the median age was 62 years (range: 46 to 70 y). The cohort comprised 9 primary oropharyngeal adenocarcinomas and 3 metastatic adenocarcinomas, including a right neck metastasis of presumed oropharyngeal origin and 2 distant metastases in patients with previously documented HPV-associated oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma. Primary sites included the palatine tonsil or tonsil-palate region (n=7), base of tongue (n=1), and oropharynx, not otherwise specified (n=1). Among cases with available T-category, 5 of 6 were T3/T4. All tumors showed high-grade adenocarcinoma morphology without a recognizable squamous component, with infiltrative glandular, ductal, or cribriform architecture. Diffuse p16 expression was present in all cases, and high-risk HPV was confirmed by polymerase chain reaction or in situ hybridization. p40 was negative in the tumor cell population in cases 1 to 6, 8, 9, 11, and 12, whereas case 7 showed p40 and CK5/6 expression confined to abluminal cells. Molecular profiling in 2 cases identified alterations or variants involving PIK3CA and PIK3R1 . Follow-up in 6 patients with primary tumors showed distant metastases in 3 and disease-specific death in 1. HPV-associated nonsalivary adenocarcinoma of the oropharynx is a diagnostically challenging tumor that expands the morphologic spectrum of HPV-related oropharyngeal carcinoma. Recognition requires exclusion of salivary-type and metastatic adenocarcinoma and confirmation of HPV association.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated non-salivary adenocarcinoma of the oropharynx is exceptionally rare and poorly defined.
Evidence base and method
We report a multi-institutional series of 12 cases from 6 tertiary centers. Histologic, immunohistochemical, HPV testing, molecular, and follow-up data were reviewed. Primary sites included the palatine tonsil or tonsil-palate region (n=7), base of tongue (n=1), and oropharynx, not otherwise specified (n=1).
Consensus and conclusions
All tumors showed high-grade adenocarcinoma morphology without a recognizable squamous component, with infiltrative glandular, ductal, or cribriform architecture. Diffuse p16 expression was present in all cases, and high-risk HPV was confirmed by polymerase chain reaction or in situ hybridization. Follow-up in 6 patients with primary tumors showed distant metastases in 3 and disease-specific death in 1.
Controversies and gaps
Among cases with available T-category, 5 of 6 were T3/T4. p40 was negative in the tumor cell population in cases 1 to 6, 8, 9, 11, and 12, whereas case 7 showed p40 and CK5/6 expression confined to abluminal cells.
Research and practice implications
HPV-associated nonsalivary adenocarcinoma of the oropharynx is a diagnostically challenging tumor that expands the morphologic spectrum of HPV-related oropharyngeal carcinoma. Recognition requires exclusion of salivary-type and metastatic adenocarcinoma and confirmation of HPV association.

全球范围内 宫颈癌 HPV 筛查后获得后续护理的障碍:系统评价

Barriers limiting access to follow-up care after HPV screening for cervical cancer in global settings: A systematic review
作者： Jessica E. Beetch, Lorna Bittencourt, Rodolfo Gutierrez, Deisy Canon Lovera, Rebekah J. Pratt
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 筛查结果呈阳性后的后续护理对于预防 人乳头瘤病毒 至关重要,但许多人没有完成诊断评估或 治疗。我们进行了一项系统回顾,以综合不同国际环境中 HPV 筛查结果呈阳性后后续护理的障碍。该协议已在 PROSPERO 中注册(CRD420251062691)。我们检索了 MEDLINE、Embase、Scopus 和 Web of Science 2015 年至 2025 年发表的英语语言研究。两位审稿人独立筛选研究,并对研究结果进行叙述性综合。十五项研究符合纳入标准,并在非洲、欧洲、北美和南美的 12 个地点进行。出现了五个障碍组:卫生系统障碍(缺乏当地 治疗、等待时间长、后续能力有限)、知识和沟通障碍(对 HPV 结果和后续步骤的困惑)、心理和情感障碍(对 治疗 的恐惧、围绕 HPV 的耻辱、对医疗系统的不信任)、社会和关系障碍(伴侣影响、性别角色、缺乏同伴支持)以及结构和后勤障碍(交通、成本、工作冲突)。在各个环境中,HPV 筛查呈阳性后错过随访似乎与卫生系统和结构性障碍的关系比缺乏动力更密切。加强转诊途径、患者跟踪系统、结果沟通、导航支持和及时的 治疗 访问可能会改善随访完成情况并提高基于 HPV 筛查计划的有效性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查的重点是在 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 筛查结果呈阳性后限制获得后续护理的障碍,核心问题是这些障碍如何影响全球环境中的 人乳头瘤病毒 预防。
证据基础与方法
该综述是一项系统综述,在 MEDLINE、Embase、Scopus 和 Web of Science 上检索了 2015 年至 2025 年的研究。两名评审员独立筛选研究,并对研究结果进行叙述性综合。
共识与结论
确定了五个障碍组:卫生系统障碍、知识和沟通障碍、心理和情感障碍、社会和关系障碍以及结构和后勤障碍。HPV 筛查阳性后错过随访与卫生系统和结构性障碍的关系比缺乏动力更密切。
争议与缺口
证据没有报告这些障碍影响的具体定量措施,也没有报告旨在解决这些障碍的干预措施的有效性。
科研与实践启示
改善转诊途径、患者跟踪系统、结果沟通、导航支持和及时的 治疗 访问可能会提高后续完成情况和基于 HPV 的筛查计划的有效性。

Barriers limiting access to follow-up care after HPV screening for cervical cancer in global settings: A systematic review

Authors: Jessica E. Beetch, Lorna Bittencourt, Rodolfo Gutierrez, Deisy Canon Lovera, Rebekah J. Pratt
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Follow-up care after a positive human papillomavirus (HPV) screening result is essential for cervical cancer prevention, yet many individuals do not complete diagnostic evaluation or treatment. We conducted a systematic review to synthesize barriers to follow-up care after a positive HPV screening result across diverse international settings. The protocol was registered in PROSPERO (CRD420251062691). We searched MEDLINE, Embase, Scopus, and Web of Science for English language studies published from 2015 to 2025. Two reviewers independently screened studies, and findings were narratively synthesized. Fifteen studies met inclusion criteria, and were conducted across 12 locations in Africa, Europe, North America, and South America. Five barrier groups emerged: health system barriers (lack of local treatment, long wait times, limited follow-up capacity), knowledge and communication barriers (confusion about HPV results and next steps), psychological and emotional barriers (fear of treatment, stigma surrounding HPV, distrust in medical systems), social and relational barriers (partner influence, gender roles, lack of peer support), and structural and logistical barriers (transportation, costs, work conflicts). Across settings, missed follow-up after positive HPV screening appeared more strongly linked to health-system and structural barriers than lack of motivation. Strengthening referral pathways, patient tracking systems, communication of results, navigation support, and timely treatment access may improve follow-up completion and enhance the effectiveness of HPV-based screening programs.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on barriers limiting access to follow-up care after a positive human papillomavirus (HPV) screening result, with the central question being how these barriers affect cervical cancer prevention in global settings.
Evidence base and method
The review was conducted as a systematic review, with studies searched from 2015 to 2025 in MEDLINE, Embase, Scopus, and Web of Science. Two reviewers independently screened studies, and findings were narratively synthesized.
Consensus and conclusions
Five barrier groups were identified: health system barriers, knowledge and communication barriers, psychological and emotional barriers, social and relational barriers, and structural and logistical barriers. Missed follow-up after positive HPV screening was more strongly linked to health-system and structural barriers than lack of motivation.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence does not report on specific quantitative measures of the impact of these barriers or on the effectiveness of interventions aimed at addressing them.
Research and practice implications
Improving referral pathways, patient tracking systems, communication of results, navigation support, and timely treatment access may enhance follow-up completion and the effectiveness of HPV-based screening programs.

高危人群肛门癌筛查的决策需求:系统评价。

Decisional needs for anal cancer screening among high-risk populations: A systematic review.
作者： Qian Zhang, Jun-Qiang Zhao, Yu-Fei Jia, Mei-Qi Meng, Deniz Naghibi, Ze-Yang Chen, Zhao-Min Xu
摘要中文翻译目的:本系统评价旨在确定和综合高危人群肛门癌筛查的决策需求(即对决策质量产生不利影响的因素)。方法:对 MEDLINE、Embase、CINAHL 和 PsycINFO 进行检索,检索时间为创建年份(分别为 1946 年、1974 年、1937 年和 1806 年)至 2025 年 12 月 14 日。检查国际肛门肿瘤协会确定的高危人群中肛门癌筛查决策需求的研究符合条件。两名审稿人独立进行标题/摘要、全文筛选和数据提取。使用混合方法评估工具评估研究质量。渥太华决策支持框架使用定向内容分析来综合决策需求。结果:纳入了 22 项研究,涉及 6522 名参与者。在美国、加拿大、澳大利亚和法国进行了 12 项横断面研究和 10 项定性研究。报告最多的决策需求是知识不足(19 项研究)、支持或资源不足(15 项研究)以及对个人对肛门癌或 人乳头瘤病毒 易感性的不切实际的期望(7 项研究)。知识差距涉及肛门癌风险、筛查程序和结果解释。支持需求包括指导不足、情感担忧和访问受限。结论:高危人群缺乏肛门癌筛查决策的信息和支持。实施应结合量身定制的风险沟通、非污名化的讨论、价值观澄清和明确的后续路径。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本系统综述旨在确定和综合高危人群中肛门癌筛查的决策需求(即对决策质量产生不利影响的因素)。
证据基础与方法
在美国、加拿大、澳大利亚和法国进行了 12 项横断面研究和 10 项定性研究。
共识与结论
报告最多的决策需求是知识不足(19 项研究)、支持或资源不足(15 项研究)以及对个人对肛门癌或 人乳头瘤病毒 易感性的不切实际的期望(7 项研究)。
争议与缺口
支持需求包括指导不足、情感担忧和访问受限。
科研与实践启示
实施应结合量身定制的风险沟通、非污名化的讨论、价值观澄清和明确的后续路径。

Decisional needs for anal cancer screening among high-risk populations: A systematic review.

Authors: Qian Zhang, Jun-Qiang Zhao, Yu-Fei Jia, Mei-Qi Meng, Deniz Naghibi, Ze-Yang Chen, Zhao-Min Xu
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: This systematic review aimed to identify and synthesize decisional needs (i.e., factors that adversely affect decision quality) of anal cancer screening among high-risk populations. METHODS: MEDLINE, Embase, CINAHL, and PsycINFO were searched from inception years (1946, 1974, 1937, and 1806 respectively) to December 14, 2025. Studies examining anal cancer screening decisional needs among high-risk populations identified by the International Anal Neoplasia Society were eligible. Two reviewers conducted title/abstract, full-text screening and data extraction independently. Study quality was assessed using the Mixed Methods Appraisal Tool. Directed content analysis was used to synthesize decisional needs by the Ottawa Decision Support Framework. RESULTS: Twenty-two studies involving 6522 participants were included. Twelve cross-sectional and 10 qualitative studies were conducted in the United States, Canada, Australia, and France. The most reported decisional needs were inadequate knowledge (19 studies), inadequate support or resources (15 studies), and unrealistic expectations regarding personal susceptibility to anal cancer or human papillomavirus (seven studies). Knowledge gaps concerned anal cancer risk, screening procedures, and results interpretation. Support needs included insufficient guidance, emotional concerns and limited access. CONCLUSIONS: High-risk populations lack the information and support for anal cancer screening decisions. Implementation should combine tailored risk communication, non-stigmatizing discussion, values clarification, and clear follow-up pathways.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review aimed to identify and synthesize decisional needs (i.e., factors that adversely affect decision quality) of anal cancer screening among high-risk populations.
Evidence base and method
Twelve cross-sectional and 10 qualitative studies were conducted in the United States, Canada, Australia, and France.
Consensus and conclusions
The most reported decisional needs were inadequate knowledge (19 studies), inadequate support or resources (15 studies), and unrealistic expectations regarding personal susceptibility to anal cancer or human papillomavirus (seven studies).
Controversies and gaps
Support needs included insufficient guidance, emotional concerns and limited access.
Research and practice implications
Implementation should combine tailored risk communication, non-stigmatizing discussion, values clarification, and clear follow-up pathways.

对安全网环境中 HPV 自我采样情况上游领域的实施前评估:范围界定审查

Pre-implementation assessment of upstream domains on the uptake of HPV self-sampling in safety-net settings: a scoping review
作者： Lynsey G. Huppe, Cassandra A. Okechukwu, Thelma Nanbigne, Grace Wang
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 筛查可用作 宫颈癌 的早期检测器。美国食品和药物管理局新批准的 HPV 自采样检测可供美国的安全网实践使用,以帮助减轻目前患者中出现的筛查差异。由于患者的可接受性和能力已经建立,本次范围审查从安全网提供者和领导层的角度综合了实施 HPV 自我采样的障碍和促进因素,以及当前研究中尚未探讨的上游因素。遵循 PRISMA-ScR 指南,进行了文献检索,探索同行评审的出版物和灰色文献,以确定 HPV 自我采样的当前状况。调查结果是使用实施研究综合框架进行组织的。从纳入的 57 项工作中发现,安全网实践能够利用当前的工作流程和团队成员来实施 HPV 自我采样。然而,外部制定的指导方针和政策严重影响实施过程,可能导致安全网实践的延迟。最近的指南认可和即将推出的保险范围解决了其中一些障碍,使安全网设置的批准后实施研究成为当务之急。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本次范围界定审查研究了在安全网环境中实施 HPV 自我采样的障碍和促进因素,重点关注影响其采用的上游因素。该审查以 HPV 类型和 HPV 相关疾病为中心,特别是 宫颈癌,并探讨了提供者和领导者在实施中的作用。
证据基础与方法
该审查遵循 PRISMA-ScR 指南,对同行评审的出版物和灰色文献进行文献检索。研究结果是使用实施研究综合框架来组织的,以确定关键的实施因素。
共识与结论
安全网实践能够使用现有的工作流程和团队成员实施 HPV 自我采样。然而,外部指导方针和政策极大地影响了实施过程,可能会延迟其在这些环境中的采用。
争议与缺口
关于影响安全网环境中 HPV 自我采样实施的具体上游因素的证据有限。该审查强调需要进一步研究来解决这些差距并了解政策变化如何加速采用。
科研与实践启示
安全网环境中的批准后实施研究是当务之急,特别是因为最近的指南认可和潜在的保险范围可能有助于克服现有障碍。这项研究应侧重于了解政策和外部指导方针对实施成功的影响。

Pre-implementation assessment of upstream domains on the uptake of HPV self-sampling in safety-net settings: a scoping review

Authors: Lynsey G. Huppe, Cassandra A. Okechukwu, Thelma Nanbigne, Grace Wang
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) screening can be used as an early detector for cervical cancer. The newly US Food and Drug Administration-approved HPV self-sampling tests can be used by safety net practices in the USA to help mitigate the current screening disparities seen in patients. As patient acceptability and capability have already been established, this scoping review synthesizes the barriers and facilitators of implementing HPV self-sampling from the perspective of safety net providers and leadership, and the upstream factors not yet explored in current research. Following PRISMA-ScR guidelines, a literature search was conducted exploring peer-reviewed publications and grey literature to establish the current landscape of HPV self-sampling. Findings were organized using the Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research. From the 57 works included, it was found that safety net practices are capable of implementing HPV self-sampling by utilizing current workflows and team members. However, outer setting guidelines and policies heavily influence the implementation process, likely causing a delay in uptake by safety net practices. Recent guideline endorsements and forthcoming insurance coverage address several of these barriers, making post-approval implementation research in safety net settings an urgent priority.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This scoping review examines the barriers and facilitators of implementing HPV self-sampling in safety-net settings, focusing on upstream factors that influence its uptake. The review is centered on HPV types and HPV-associated diseases, particularly cervical cancer, and explores the role of provider and leadership perspectives in implementation.
Evidence base and method
The review followed PRISMA-ScR guidelines, conducting a literature search across peer-reviewed publications and grey literature. The findings were organized using the Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research to identify key implementation factors.
Consensus and conclusions
Safety net practices are capable of implementing HPV self-sampling using existing workflows and team members. However, external guidelines and policies significantly influence the implementation process, potentially delaying its adoption in these settings.
Controversies and gaps
There is limited evidence on the specific upstream factors affecting HPV self-sampling implementation in safety-net settings. The review highlights the need for further research to address these gaps and understand how policy changes can accelerate uptake.
Research and practice implications
Post-approval implementation research in safety-net settings is an urgent priority, particularly as recent guideline endorsements and potential insurance coverage may help overcome existing barriers. This research should focus on understanding the impact of policy and external guidelines on implementation success.

宫颈癌 中竞争性内源核糖核酸网络:机制和临床意义。

Competing Endogenous Ribonucleic Acid Networks in Cervical Cancer: Mechanisms and Clinical Implications.
作者： Lijuan Shi, Misbah Ullah Khan
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌 (CC) 仍然是女性癌症相关 死亡率 的主要原因,高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 的持续感染是其主要病因。长非编码 RNA (lncRNA)、环状 RNA (circRNA) 和假基因可以通过与信使 RNA (mRNA) 竞争与 microRNA (miRNA) 的结合,充当竞争性内源 RNA (ceRNA),从而减少 miRNA 介导的靶 mRNA 抑制。本综述将 CC 中报道的 ceRNA 轴分类为致癌轴或肿瘤抑制轴,并检查了它们在主要信号通路上的收敛性,包括 Wnt/β-连环蛋白、磷酸肌醇 3-激酶 (PI3K)/Akt、丝裂原激活蛋白激酶 (MAPK)、Hippo 和 Notch,以及支持证据的强度。还评估了这些网络在预后、顺铂和放疗耐药性以及对生物活性化合物的反应中的作用。大多数报告的轴主要由细胞系实验支持,而绝对 RNA 定量通常无法实现,并且患者水平的验证仍然有限。因此,ceRNA 网络代表了潜在的生物标志物和治疗靶点,尽管需要额外的机制和临床证据来确定其临床适用性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述探讨了竞争性内源 RNA (ceRNA) 网络在 宫颈癌 (CC) 中的作用,重点关注其机制和临床意义。CC的主要病因是高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)的持续感染。该综述旨在将 ceRNA 轴分类为致癌轴或肿瘤抑制轴,并检查它们在关键信号通路上的收敛性。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 HPV 相关疾病中 ceRNA 网络的系统分析,特别是 宫颈癌。它包括对长非编码 RNA (lncRNA)、环状 RNA (circRNA) 和假基因(如 ceRNA)的作用的研究。该综述评估了这些网络与主要信号通路及其对预后和 治疗 耐药性的影响相关的证据。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,ceRNA 网络参与了 宫颈癌 中 Wnt/β-catenin、PI3K/Akt、MAPK、Hippo 和 Notch 等关键信号通路的调节。这些网络被归类为致癌网络或肿瘤抑制网络,它们在疾病进展和 治疗 耐药性中的作用得到了强调。
争议与缺口
该评论指出,大多数报道的 ceRNA 轴主要由细胞系实验支持,患者水平的验证有限。绝对 RNA 定量通常无法实现,这可能会影响研究结果的可靠性。此外,这些网络作为生物标志物或治疗靶点的临床适用性仍有待确定。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,需要进一步的机制和临床研究来验证 ceRNA 网络在 宫颈癌 中的作用。这包括患者水平验证、绝对 RNA 定量以及探索其作为生物标志物或治疗靶点的潜力。

Competing Endogenous Ribonucleic Acid Networks in Cervical Cancer: Mechanisms and Clinical Implications.

Authors: Lijuan Shi, Misbah Ullah Khan
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer (CC) remains a leading cause of cancer-related mortality among women, with persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) representing its primary etiological factor. Long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs), circular RNAs (circRNAs), and pseudogenes can function as competing endogenous RNAs (ceRNAs) by competing with messenger RNAs (mRNAs) for binding to microRNAs (miRNAs), thereby reducing miRNA-mediated repression of target mRNAs. This review categorizes the ceRNA axes reported in CC as oncogenic or tumor-suppressive and examines their convergence on major signaling pathways, including Wnt/β-catenin, phosphoinositide 3-kinase (PI3K)/Akt, mitogen-activated protein kinase (MAPK), Hippo, and Notch, together with the strength of the supporting evidence. The roles of these networks in prognosis, resistance to cisplatin and radiotherapy, and responses to bioactive compounds are also evaluated. Most reported axes are supported primarily by cell-line experiments, whereas absolute RNA quantification is frequently unavailable and patient-level validation remains limited. Accordingly, ceRNA networks represent potential biomarkers and therapeutic targets, although additional mechanistic and clinical evidence is required to establish their clinical applicability.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review explores the role of competing endogenous RNA (ceRNA) networks in cervical cancer (CC), focusing on their mechanisms and clinical implications. The primary etiological factor of CC is persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV). The review aims to categorize ceRNA axes as oncogenic or tumor-suppressive and examine their convergence on key signaling pathways.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of ceRNA networks in HPV-associated diseases, particularly cervical cancer. It includes studies on the roles of long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs), circular RNAs (circRNAs), and pseudogenes as ceRNAs. The review evaluates the evidence for these networks in relation to major signaling pathways and their impact on prognosis and treatment resistance.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that ceRNA networks are involved in the regulation of key signaling pathways such as Wnt/β-catenin, PI3K/Akt, MAPK, Hippo, and Notch in cervical cancer. These networks are categorized as either oncogenic or tumor-suppressive, and their roles in disease progression and treatment resistance are highlighted.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that most reported ceRNA axes are supported primarily by cell-line experiments, with limited patient-level validation. Absolute RNA quantification is frequently unavailable, which may affect the reliability of findings. Additionally, the clinical applicability of these networks as biomarkers or therapeutic targets remains to be established.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that further mechanistic and clinical studies are needed to validate the roles of ceRNA networks in cervical cancer. This includes patient-level validation, absolute RNA quantification, and exploration of their potential as biomarkers or therapeutic targets.

宫颈癌 生物学中的 TWIST 家族:从分子机制到临床意义。

TWIST family in cervical cancer biology: From molecular mechanisms to clinical implications.
作者： Usamah Sayed, Aziz Kubaev, Djamila Sh. Polatova, Mhnd Kadhim Ali, Swati Mishra, Divya Singhal, Neeraj Bainsal, Saeed Niazi Vahdati
摘要中文翻译尽管临床不断努力,宫颈癌 仍然是最常见的妇科肿瘤之一,这主要是由于其转移潜力和对化疗的耐药性。转录调节因子 TWIST 与这些特征有关,部分是通过诱导上皮间质转化 (EMT) 来实现的。TWIST 的表达受到多种外源因子的精细调节,包括缺氧驱动的 HIF-1α 信号传导和 TGF-β 通路,以及内源性致癌级联,例如 Wnt/β-catenin、NF-κB、PI3K/Akt、Hedgehog、Notch 和 IL-6/JAK2/STAT3。此外,来自高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 菌株的 E6 和 E7 蛋白促进 TWIST 上调,促进细胞可塑性和逃避衰老。转录后和表观遗传调控是由非编码 RNA 和关键转录因子(例如 Mist1、FOXC1 和 LMX-1A)组成的复杂网络精心策划的。重要的是,TWIST1 启动子的高甲基化已被提议作为宫颈上皮内瘤变的候选生物标志物。据报道,TWIST 通过下调 E-钙粘蛋白、诱导基质金属蛋白酶和维持干细胞样特性来促进肿瘤相关表型。TWIST 可能通过涉及 DNA 损伤反应和 MDR1/P-糖蛋白介导的药物流出的机制与化疗和放疗耐药性相关。肿瘤微环境影响其表达和活性,包括缺氧、癌症相关成纤维细胞、炎症细胞因子、肿瘤相关巨噬细胞和细胞外囊泡。在临床背景下,高水平的 TWIST 与 宫颈癌 的不良结果相关。越来越多的证据表明,它通过调节 PD-L1、免疫抑制性细胞因子信号传导、巨噬细胞极化、T 细胞排斥以及与癌症干细胞相关的免疫抵抗来逃避免疫,强调了它在当前免疫检查点封锁时代的翻译重要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 TWIST 家族在 宫颈癌 生物学中的作用,特别是其在转移潜力、化疗耐药和免疫逃避方面的作用。核心问题是 TWIST 如何通过 EMT、致癌途径以及与肿瘤微环境的相互作用等分子机制来实现这些特征。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对现有文献的叙述性综合,没有明确提及系统搜索方法、数据库或荟萃分析。它讨论了外源性和内源性因素对 TWIST 的调节及其在 宫颈癌 中的临床相关性。
共识与结论
该综述的结论是,TWIST 是 宫颈癌 进展的关键参与者,通过 E-钙粘蛋白下调、基质金属蛋白酶诱导和干细胞样特性促进肿瘤相关表型。它还强调了 TWIST 与 宫颈癌 不良临床结果的关联。
争议与缺口
该评论指出,虽然 TWIST 与化疗和放疗耐药有关,但确切的机制仍不清楚。此外,TWIST 在免疫逃避中的作用及其作为宫颈上皮内瘤变生物标志物的潜力需要进一步研究。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,了解 TWIST 调节及其在 宫颈癌 中的作用可能会导致靶向治疗、改进早期检测的生物标志物以及针对 HPV 相关癌症的免疫检查点阻断的更好策略。

TWIST family in cervical cancer biology: From molecular mechanisms to clinical implications.

Authors: Usamah Sayed, Aziz Kubaev, Djamila Sh. Polatova, Mhnd Kadhim Ali, Swati Mishra, Divya Singhal, Neeraj Bainsal, Saeed Niazi Vahdati
Original abstract (English)
Despite ongoing clinical efforts, cervical cancer remains one of the most common gynecologic tumors, largely due to its metastatic potential and resistance to chemotherapy. The transcriptional regulator TWIST has been implicated in these features, in part through the induction of epithelial-mesenchymal transition (EMT). The expression of TWIST is delicately regulated by several exogenous factors, including hypoxia-driven HIF-1α signaling and TGF-β pathways, as well as endogenous oncogenic cascades such as Wnt/β-catenin, NF-κB, PI3K/Akt, Hedgehog, Notch, and IL-6/JAK2/STAT3. Moreover, the E6 and E7 proteins from high-risk human papillomavirus strains promote TWIST upregulation, facilitating cellular plasticity and evasion of senescence. Post-transcriptional and epigenetic regulation is orchestrated by complex networks of non-coding RNAs and key transcription factors such as Mist1, FOXC1, and LMX-1A. Importantly, hypermethylation of the TWIST1 promoter has been proposed as a candidate biomarker for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia. TWIST has been reported to promote tumor-associated phenotypes through E-cadherin downregulation, induction of matrix metalloproteinases, and maintenance of stem cell-like properties. TWIST has been associated with chemotherapy and radiotherapy resistance, potentially through mechanisms involving DNA-damage responses and MDR1/P-glycoprotein-mediated drug efflux. The tumor microenvironment affects its expression and activity, including hypoxia, cancer-associated fibroblasts, inflammatory cytokines, tumor-associated macrophages, and extracellular vesicles. In a clinical context, high levels of TWIST are linked to poor outcomes in cervical cancer. Growing evidence links it to immune evasion through regulation of PD-L1, immunosuppressive cytokine signaling, macrophage polarization, T-cell exclusion, and immune resistance associated with cancer stem cells, underscoring its importance for translation in the current era of immune checkpoint blockade.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the role of the TWIST family in cervical cancer biology, particularly its involvement in metastatic potential, chemotherapy resistance, and immune evasion. The central question is how TWIST contributes to these features through molecular mechanisms such as EMT, oncogenic pathways, and interactions with the tumor microenvironment.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing literature, with no explicit mention of systematic search methods, databases, or meta-analysis. It discusses the regulation of TWIST by exogenous and endogenous factors, as well as its clinical relevance in cervical cancer.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that TWIST is a key player in cervical cancer progression, promoting tumor-associated phenotypes through E-cadherin downregulation, matrix metalloproteinase induction, and stem cell-like properties. It also highlights the association of TWIST with poor clinical outcomes in cervical cancer.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that while TWIST is implicated in chemotherapy and radiotherapy resistance, the exact mechanisms remain unclear. Additionally, the role of TWIST in immune evasion and its potential as a biomarker for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia require further investigation.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that understanding TWIST regulation and its role in cervical cancer could lead to targeted therapies, improved biomarkers for early detection, and better strategies for immune checkpoint blockade in HPV-associated cancers.

治疗 宫颈阴道微生物组的相关变化以及 HPV 清除率和宫颈疾病结果的微生物相关性:系统评价

Treatment-associated changes in the cervicovaginal microbiome and microbial correlates of HPV clearance and cervical disease outcomes: a systematic review
作者： Gbenankpon M. Houvessou, Manuel Mahoche, Nerys Wendy Antonieta Alfane, Louise Kuhn, Gbènankpon Mathias Houvèssou
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
治疗 宫颈阴道微生物组的相关变化以及 HPV 清除率和宫颈疾病结果的微生物相关性:系统评价。
证据基础与方法
截至 2026 年 8 月 20 日,我们检索了五个数据库,以评估 治疗 之前、之前和之后的子宫颈阴道微生物组 (CVM) 分子水平。使用 RoB-2、ROBINS-I 和纽卡斯尔-渥太华量表评估偏倚风险。研究结果在无荟萃分析的综合指导下进行叙述性综合,并按人群、治疗、采样地点和结果进行分层。没有进行荟萃分析和正式的发表偏倚评估。
共识与结论
纳入 21 项研究:16 项 队列研究、三项非随机研究和两项随机试验。大多数切除或益生菌研究报告称,治疗 后,α 多样性较低,乳酸菌优势较大;一项研究显示,在切除环后,清除 HR-HPV 的女性中,乳酸菌耗尽的群体从 32% 下降到 10%。基线惰性乳杆菌随着 HPV 持续存在而升高(62% vs 16%;60% vs 45%)。
争议与缺口
个别研究将几种厌氧菌类群与 HPV 持续存在或复发联系起来,并将干酪乳杆菌、厌氧消化链球菌和乳酸厌氧球菌与清除联系起来,但这些关联均未经过外部验证,并且多重测试校正的报告也不一致。只有五项研究包括对照组。11 项研究中有 8 项发现基线 α 多样性的结果没有差异。
科研与实践启示
基线惰性乳杆菌优势是需要前瞻性确认的不利结果的候选相关性。该综述强调需要进一步研究来验证 HPV 清除率和宫颈疾病结果之间的微生物相关性,并探索微生物组变化与 HPV 持续存在或复发之间的因果关系。

Treatment-associated changes in the cervicovaginal microbiome and microbial correlates of HPV clearance and cervical disease outcomes: a systematic review

Authors: Gbenankpon M. Houvessou, Manuel Mahoche, Nerys Wendy Antonieta Alfane, Louise Kuhn, Gbènankpon Mathias Houvèssou
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Treatment for high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV), cervical intraepithelial neoplasia, and cervical cancer is effective, but HPV may persist or recur. We synthesised evidence on post-treatment cervicovaginal microbiota (CVM) changes and pre-treatment CVM correlates of outcomes. Methods Five databases were searched to August 20, 2026 for studies assessing the CVM molecularly before, or before and after, treatment. Risk of bias was assessed using RoB-2, ROBINS-I, and the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale. Findings were synthesised narratively under Synthesis Without Meta-analysis guidance and stratified by population, treatment, sampling site, and outcome. Meta-analysis and formal publication-bias assessment were not performed. Results Twenty-one studies were included: 16 cohorts, three non-randomised studies, and two randomised trials. Twenty reported the number of women enrolled ( n = 1,345), whereas one reported 125 vaginal samples without a corresponding number of women. Most excision or probiotic studies reported lower alpha diversity and greater Lactobacillus dominance after treatment; after loop excision, Lactobacillus-depleted communities fell from 32% to 10% among women who cleared HR-HPV in one study. Only five studies included a comparison group. Eight of 11 studies found no baseline alpha-diversity difference by outcome. Baseline Lactobacillus iners was higher with HPV persistence (62% vs 16%; 60% vs 45%); the only adjusted analysis associated it with non-clearance after non-operative management (aOR 3.94, 95% CI 1.06–14.70) but yielded an imprecise estimate after excision (aOR 1.86, 95% CI 0.12–29.42). Individual studies linked several anaerobic taxa to HPV persistence or recurrence and Lacticaseibacillus casei, Peptostreptococcus anaerobius, and Anaerococcus lactolyticus to clearance, but none of these associations was externally validated and multiple-testing correction was inconsistently reported. Conclusions After treatment, the CVM often shifts towards lower-diversity, Lactobacillus-dominant communities, but causality is unproven. iners dominance is a candidate correlate of unfavourable outcomes requiring prospective confirmation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Treatment-associated changes in the cervicovaginal microbiome and microbial correlates of HPV clearance and cervical disease outcomes: a systematic review.
Evidence base and method
Five databases were searched to August 20, 2026 for studies assessing the cervicovaginal microbiome (CVM) molecularly before, or before and after, treatment. Risk of bias was assessed using RoB-2, ROBINS-I, and the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale. Findings were synthesised narratively under Synthesis Without Meta-analysis guidance and stratified by population, treatment, sampling site, and outcome. Meta-analysis and formal publication-bias assessment were not performed.
Consensus and conclusions
Twenty-one studies were included: 16 cohorts, three non-randomised studies, and two randomised trials. Most excision or probiotic studies reported lower alpha diversity and greater Lactobacillus dominance after treatment; after loop excision, Lactobacillus-depleted communities fell from 32% to 10% among women who cleared HR-HPV in one study. Baseline Lactobacillus iners was higher with HPV persistence (62% vs 16%; 60% vs 45%).
Controversies and gaps
Individual studies linked several anaerobic taxa to HPV persistence or recurrence and Lacticaseibacillus casei, Peptostreptococcus anaerobius, and Anaerococcus lactolyticus to clearance, but none of these associations was externally validated and multiple-testing correction was inconsistently reported. Only five studies included a comparison group. Eight of 11 studies found no baseline alpha-diversity difference by outcome.
Research and practice implications
Baseline Lactobacillus iners dominance is a candidate correlate of unfavourable outcomes requiring prospective confirmation. The review highlights the need for further research to validate microbial correlates of HPV clearance and cervical disease outcomes, and to explore the causal relationships between microbiome changes and HPV persistence or recurrence.

Diversity among Women with Cervical Complaints in Bangladesh 【字体: 大 中 小 】 时间:2026年11月06日 来源:Journal of Infection and Public Health 4.8 编辑推荐: 背景:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是孟加拉国宫颈癌(CC)的主要致病因素。本研究调查了孟加拉国宫颈异常女性中HPV的发生率、流行基因型及其系统发育关系,并对一株来自孟加拉国的HPV45毒株进行了全基因组、谱系及突变特征分析。 方法:在孟加拉国一家三级医院采集了102 下载详细解决方案,揭秘《无细胞表达合成系统--重组抗体表达进入Hours时代!》 为了促进科学的传播,生物通采集了这篇文章 订阅BioHot点这里解锁原文链接推送! “畅读会员”点此登录查看原文链接 Open access 背景:人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是孟加拉国宫颈癌(CC)的主要致病因素。本研究调查了孟加拉国宫颈异常女性中HPV的发生率、流行基因型及其系统发育关系,并对一株来自孟加拉国的HPV45毒株进行了全基因组、谱系及突变特征分析。 方法:在孟加拉国一家三级医院采集了102名宫颈异常女性的宫颈拭子。采用基因特异性引物通过PCR扩增检测HPV。在HPV阳性样本中,选择PCR条带强度高的样本进行L1基因部分片段的Sanger测序。代表性HPV45毒株的全基因组测序在Illumina NovaSeq X Plus平台上完成。系统发育分析使用MAFFT、IQ-TREE2和RAxML-NG软件进行。突变、结构及蛋白稳定性分析采用生物信息学和结构建模工具完成。 结果:在102名参与者中检出HPV DNA阳性率为47.1%(48/102)。在基于强PCR条带强度选出的36份测序样本中,21份成功获得序列。测序毒株的基因分型显示高危型HPV基因型占优势(95.45%),其中HPV16(68.18%)和HPV18(13.63%)最为常见。系统发育分析显示基因型特异性聚类,且与全球流行毒株具有密切的遗传亲缘关系。一株HPV45毒株(HPV_MGH-TT01)的完整基因组特征分析显示其基因组长度为7,849 bp,属于A2亚谱系,与全球参考毒株的核苷酸一致性为98.0%–99.9%。研究鉴定出多个氨基酸替换,主要集中于E2蛋白,但结构分析预测仅引起微小的构象改变。 结论:高危型HPV基因型的优势分布以及HPV45的基因组特征分析,凸显了开展大规模基因组监测和扩大分子筛查对于预防孟加拉国宫颈癌的重要性。 论文解读 下载【肥胖症和糖尿病明星治疗靶点】,获取相关药物开发的最新进展。领 取 一、研究背景与意义 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染是全球范围内最常见的性传播病毒感染之一,构成重大公共卫生负担。大多数HPV感染为一过性,可被机体免疫系统自然清除,但持续性感染与宫颈癌(CC)及其癌前病变的发生密切相关。据估计,全球约2.91亿女性感染HPV,其中约三分之一携带与宫颈癌关系最为密切的HPV16和/或HPV18。世界卫生组织(WHO)将14种HPV基因型(HPV16、18、31、33、35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66和68)列为与宫颈癌相关的高危型(hrHPV),而低危型(lrHPV)如HPV6、11和43则主要与良性增生性病变相关。在全球范围内,HPV16和HPV18约导致70%的宫颈癌病例。 南亚地区(包括孟加拉国)承担了全球近三分之一的宫颈癌负担。在孟加拉国,宫颈癌是女性第二大常见恶性肿瘤,约占女性癌症总数的12%,2018年新发病例约8,268例,死亡约5,214例。然而,孟加拉国缺乏全面的人群癌症登记系统,且仅有12%的女性了解宫颈癌筛查,早婚等根深蒂固的社会文化习俗进一步加重了该国的宫颈癌负担。尽管孟加拉国已在近600个VIA和临床乳腺检查(CBE)中心实施了全国免费的基于醋酸肉眼观察(VIA)的宫颈筛查项目,但VIA的有效性受限于其特异性不足和对操作者经验的依赖。在此背景下,HPV DNA检测完全符合WHO的建议,即使终身进行一次筛查也能显著降低宫颈癌的发病率和死亡率。然而,孟加拉国HPV筛查实施有限,导致HPV流行率和基因型分布的流行病学数据存在巨大缺口。此外,该国现有研究多局限于HPV基因分型,缺乏对流行HPV毒株的全基因组、进化和结构特征分析。因此,本研究旨在阐明孟加拉国一家三级医院就诊的不同宫颈异常女性中HPV感染频率、流行基因型及其系统发育关系,并对代表性HPV45毒株进行全基因组特征、谱系分析和突变谱分析,以更好地了解孟加拉国HPV的分子流行病学和遗传多样性。 二、主要技术方法 研究人员从孟加拉国达卡国家癌症研究与医院(NICRH)连续收集了2025年7月至12月期间就诊的102名宫颈异常女性的宫颈拭子样本,参与者来自孟加拉国22个地区。研究采用L1基因通用引物MY09/MY11进行PCR扩增检测HPV DNA;对PCR条带强度高的样本进行L1基因部分片段的Sanger测序以确定基因型;对代表性HPV45毒株采用Illumina NovaSeq X Plus平台进行全基因组测序;利用MAFFT、IQ-TREE2和RAxML-NG等工具进行多序列比对和最大似然法(ML)系统发育分析;通过生物信息学工具和结构建模工具(如trRosetta、PyMOL、DynaMut2等)进行突变分析、蛋白理化性质预测、三维结构建模和热稳定性预测。 三、主要研究结果 (一)参与者社会人口学特征与HPV阳性率 102名女性参与者的平均年龄为44.6岁(范围22–70岁)。PCR检测显示48名参与者HPV阳性,阳性率为47.1%(95% CI:37.3%–57.2%)。HPV阳性女性的平均年龄(45.9岁)略高于阴性女性(43.5岁)。多数参与者居住在农村地区(68.6%)、目前已婚(87.3%)、具有学校水平或未接受正式教育(93.1%)、报告单一终身性伴侣(94.1%)。绝大多数参与者(89.2%)不了解HPV在宫颈癌发展中的作用。所有参与者均无HPV疫苗接种史、吸烟或饮酒史。研究队列包含五种宫颈状况:正常宫颈(n=17)、CIN I(n=11)、CIN II/III(n=7)、宫颈癌(n=41)和宫颈炎(n=26)。HPV阳性率在≥60岁女性中最高(61.54%),在30–39岁女性中最低(42.31%);18岁前首次性行为的女性HPV阳性率(49.3%)高于18岁及以上者(42.4%)。 (二)HPV基因型多样性与进化关系 在48份HPV阳性样本中,21份L1基因序列成功获得,另有一份代表性样本获得全基因组序列。基于22条L1基因序列的基因分型分析显示高危型HPV占绝对优势(95.45%,21/22),其中HPV16占68.18%(15/22)、HPV18占13.63%(3/22)、HPV33占4.55%(1/22)、HPV45占4.55%(1/22)、HPV58占4.55%(1/22),仅鉴定出1例低危型HPV61(4.55%,1/22)。基于L1基因部分序列的系统发育分析证实了所鉴定HPV毒株与对应参考毒株的基因型特异性聚类。所有HPV16毒株与美国、非洲、巴西、中国和泰国报道的毒株具有密切遗传亲缘关系;HPV18毒株与美国、日本和泰国参考毒株紧密聚类;HPV33和HPV45毒株与美国、中国、多哥、卢森堡和南非毒株相关;HPV58与中国、墨西哥和巴西毒株聚类;HPV61与多哥、卢森堡和巴西毒株聚类。属级和种级系统发育分析显示,所有22个毒株均聚类在α乳头瘤病毒属(Alphapapillomavirus)内,其中HPV16、HPV33和HPV58属于Alpha-9物种组,HPV18和HPV45属于Alpha-7物种组,HPV61属于Alpha-3物种组。 (三)HPV45毒株全基因组特征分析 代表性毒株HPV_MGH-TT01的全基因组测序显示基因组长度为7,849 bp,相对于7,858 bp的参考基因组(NC_075269.1)在长控制区(LCR)存在9 bp缺失,GC含量为39.72%。基因组包含8个开放阅读框(ORF):早期基因E6(477 bp)、E7(321 bp)、E1(1,932 bp)、E2(1,107 bp)、E4(273 bp)和E5(222 bp),以及晚期基因L1(1,611 bp)和L2(1,392 bp)。与27条参考序列的两两比对显示高度基因组保守性,核苷酸一致性范围为98.0%(多哥毒株)至99.9%(美国毒株)。基于全基因组序列的最大似然系统发育分析显示,HPV_MGH-TT01毒株聚类在A谱系中的A2亚谱系内。两两核苷酸差异分析显示,HPV_MGH-TT01与A2亚谱系参考基因组之间的差异最小,而与B1和B2亚谱系的差异较大,符合预期的亚谱系内和亚谱系间变异模式。 (四)HPV45毒株ORF突变谱分析 与NCBI参考毒株(NC_075269.1)相比,HPV_MGH-TT01毒株的8个完整ORF和LCR中鉴定出多个同义和非同义替换。在各病毒蛋白中,E2蛋白变异程度最高,鉴定出7个氨基酸替换(I146M、D153E、S227C、R263L、P271S、N330K和I368M),其次是L2(R222A、L242V、N342D和H365N)、E6(L21T、Q46E和S129N)、E4(P25A、V61L和K79N)、E5(L8V和I72L)、E7(E6A和E82D)和L1(S49N和S383G)。E1蛋白未检测到氨基酸替换。与近期美国报道的参考毒株PV418764.1相比,仅观察到极少的氨基酸变异。 (五)蛋白稳定性与构象分析 理化性质分析显示,HPV_MGH-TT01毒株编码的所有蛋白与参考毒株相比仅存在微小的定量差异。二级和三级结构建模显示其蛋白与对应参考蛋白具有高度构象相似性。E2蛋白的结构分析显示氨基酸替换位于三个主要功能区域:N端反式激活结构域(残基1–207;I146M和D153E)、无序连接区(残基227–251;S227C)和C端DNA结合结构域(残基290–368;N330K和I368M)。E6蛋白在其锌指结构域内表现出序列变异,而E7蛋白在pRb结合N端无序区和C端锌指支架中均存在替换。热力学稳定性预测显示,E4和E5是结构最稳定的蛋白,E5具有最高的预测ΔΔG稳定值(1.02 kcal/mol),而E2被预测为最不稳定的蛋白,总ΔΔG值为?2.04 kcal/mol。 四、讨论与结论 本研究的HPV检出率(47.1%)高于孟加拉国既往基于人群的研究,这可能归因于纳入了具有宫颈异常和癌前病变的女性而非一般筛查人群。HPV16和HPV18的优势分布与全球流行病学报告一致。值得注意的是,41例确诊宫颈癌病例中有15例L1 PCR阴性,7例CIN II/III中仅1例阳性,这可能反映了MY09/11检测的局限性,包括引物不匹配、病毒整合过程中L1基因破坏和DNA降解等导致的假阴性结果。系统发育分析显示孟加拉国HPV16和HPV18毒株与全球不同地理区域的毒株具有密切遗传亲缘关系,表明循环变异株具有高度序列保守性和共同进化祖先。HPV45 A2亚谱系毒株的鉴定以及各蛋白中多个氨基酸替换的发现,特别是E2蛋白的变异,可能影响病毒复制、持续性、免疫逃逸和致癌潜力,但其功能意义需要进一步的实验验证。本研究存在样本量较小、单中心招募、缺乏PCR对照、仅22/48阳性样本完成基因分型等局限性。研究结论强调,高危型HPV基因型的优势分布和HPV45的基因组特征凸显了大规模基因组监测和扩大分子筛查对于预防孟加拉国宫颈癌的重要性。 打赏 展开全文 News Brief
The CoSara SARAGENE high-risk HPV test is being used in a cervical cancer screening study, as reported in a news article published on 4 November 2026 by Scientist Live. The article highlights the test's role in improving early detection and prevention efforts for cervical cancer, which is a significant health concern in many regions. However, the specific location or population group targeted by the study is not detailed in the evidence. The test's application in this context suggests a potential impact on public health, but no confirmed case counts, death numbers, or exposure data are provided. The study is part of ongoing research and development, with no further details on its current status or implementation. This development aligns with broader efforts in the biotech and life sciences sectors to enhance diagnostic tools for HPV-associated diseases.

HPV 如何重新连接 宫颈癌 细胞中的糖化学 - Bioengineer.org

How HPV rewires sugar chemistry in cervical cancer cells - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报发表在《分子生物科学前沿》上的一篇新评论研究了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 如何通过称为 O-GlcNAcylation 的过程影响 宫颈癌 细胞中的糖化学。该研究探讨了 HPV 阳性 宫颈癌 细胞的代谢需求,指出虽然 HPV E6 和 E7 癌蛋白驱动糖酵解和生物合成活性,但在这些细胞中尚未验证线粒体 O-GlcNAcNAc 化位点的因果关系。作者强调需要进一步研究,包括 HPV 阳性模型的直接实验和临床样本的验证。他们还强调了比较不同 HPV 基因型(例如 HPV16 和 HPV18)的复杂性,因为它们的生物学和遗传特征存在差异。该综述强调了了解 O-GlcNAcylation 在 宫颈癌 进展和 治疗 耐药中的作用的重要性,但强调目前的发现仍然是需要额外验证的假设。

时间
2026 年 11 月 7 日星期六。
地点与对象
全球,特别提及涉及 SiHa 和 HeLa 细胞的 宫颈癌 案例和研究。
事件
《分子生物科学前沿》上发表的一篇新综述探讨了 HPV 引起的 宫颈癌 细胞中 O-GlcNAcylation 的作用,重点关注其与代谢重编程和肿瘤生物学的潜在联系。
规模、影响与风险
该综述强调了 HPV 阳性 宫颈癌 细胞的代谢需求,包括增加糖酵解和生物合成活性,但指出在这些细胞中尚未对线粒体 O-GlcNAcNAc 化位点进行因果验证。它还强调需要对源自患者的模型和临床样本进行进一步研究。
应对、状态与不确定性
该综述指出了当前证据中的差距,并呼吁在 HPV 阳性模型中进行直接通量、底物和拯救实验。它还强调了独立复制、体内验证和患者来源样本确认的必要性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

How HPV rewires sugar chemistry in cervical cancer cells - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A new review published in Frontiers in Molecular Biosciences investigates how human papillomavirus (HPV) influences sugar chemistry in cervical cancer cells through a process called O-GlcNAcylation. The study explores the metabolic demands of HPV-positive cervical cancer cells, noting that while HPV E6 and E7 oncoproteins drive glycolysis and biosynthetic activity, no mitochondrial O-GlcNAcylation site has been causally validated in these cells. The authors emphasize the need for further research, including direct experiments in HPV-positive models and validation in clinical samples. They also highlight the complexity of comparing different HPV genotypes, such as HPV16 and HPV18, due to differences in their biological and genetic characteristics. The review underscores the importance of understanding O-GlcNAcylation's role in cervical cancer progression and treatment resistance, but stresses that current findings remain hypotheses requiring additional validation.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Saturday, November 7, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Biology How HPV rewires sugar chemistry in cervical cancer cells by November 6, 2026 in Biology Reading Time: 6 mins read 0 How HPV rewires sugar chemistry in cervical cancer cells Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Cervical cancer remains one of the most common malignancies affecting women worldwide, with an estimated 662,301 new cases and 348,874 deaths recorded globally in 2022 according to GLOBOCAN data. Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus is the principal initiating event in the disease, and sustained expression of the viral E6 and E7 oncoproteins drives malignant transformation and tumor maintenance. Yet viral positivity alone cannot explain why some lesions progress to invasive cancer while others do not, or why established tumors develop resistance to treatment. A new review published in Frontiers in Molecular Biosciences examines whether a chemical modification of proteins, known as O-GlcNAcylation, could connect the metabolic demands imposed by HPV oncogenes to the way cervical cancer cells manage energy production and stress. O-GlcNAcylation is the reversible attachment of a single N-acetylglucosamine sugar to serine or threonine residues on proteins inside the cell. The enzyme O-GlcNAc transferase, or OGT, adds this modification, while O-GlcNAcase, or OGA, removes it. The donor substrate for the reaction, UDP-GlcNAc, is manufactured by the hexosamine biosynthetic pathway from inputs drawn from glucose, glutamine, lipids, and nucleotides. Because the pathway integrates these multiple nutrient streams, O-GlcNAc cycling can report on nutrient availability, biosynthetic demand, and cellular stress without acting as the sole upstream controller of those states. Unlike classical glycosylation in the secretory pathway, this modification occurs on proteins in the nucleus, cytosol, and mitochondrial matrix, and it can respond rapidly to nutrients, oxidative stress, viral infection, and therapeutic pressure. Discover more Learn Engineering Track Biotech Tour Colleges The review, authored by Yingying Zhang, Yimei Tao, Huirong He, Ping Li, and Minbin Chen, grades the available evidence according to experimental model, temporal context, and causal depth. The authors distinguish between observations made in untreated cervical cancer models, acute responses to treatment, and durable resistance, arguing that these represent distinct biological situations that should not be interpreted interchangeably. Their central conclusion is sobering: cervical cancer studies support links between O-GlcNAcylation and pathways involved in growth and survival, but no mitochondrial O-GlcNAcylation site has been causally validated in HPV-positive cervical cancer. Mechanisms inferred from cardiac, lung, colorectal, and other experimental systems remain hypotheses requiring validation in HPV-positive cervical cancer cells, patient-derived models, and clinical samples. Discover more Engineering job board Research publication platform Scientific instrument sales Laboratory equipment supplier Sustained E6 and E7 expression increases metabolic demand in cervical cancer cells through several established routes. E6 promotes degradation of the p53 tumor suppressor mainly through an E6AP-dependent mechanism, while E7 binds pRB family proteins through its LXCXE motif and releases G1/S checkpoint control. The oncoproteins also engage c-Myc, HIF-1α, PI3K/Akt, PKM2, and E2F-related programs that regulate glycolysis, glutamine utilization, nucleotide synthesis, and adaptation to oxidative stress. Notably, HPV E6 can elevate OGT expression and O-GlcNAcylation, and E6/E7-associated proliferation is accompanied by increased glycolysis and biosynthetic demand. Greater glycolytic activity can alter the availability of hexosamine pathway substrates, but the review stresses that current evidence does not demonstrate that O-GlcNAcylation initiates metabolic reprogramming. The authors position changes in the hexosamine pathway, OGT, and OGA as a potentially responsive and feedback-regulated layer that requires direct flux, substrate, and rescue experiments in HPV-positive models. HPV genotype adds another layer of complexity. HPV16 and HPV18, the predominant oncogenic genotypes in cervical cancer, produce E6 and E7 proteins that differ in sequence, expression control, splice products, and affinity for selected host partners. A common comparison between SiHa cells, which are HPV16-positive squamous carcinoma cells, and HeLa cells, which are HPV18-positive adenocarcinoma cells, confounds viral genotype with histology, integration pattern, viral copy number, and host genomic background. Within the literature surveyed, the authors identified no comparison of matched HPV16 and HPV18 models that quantified hexosamine pathway flux, UDP-GlcNAc abundance, OGT or OGA activity, or site-specific O-GlcNAcylation. An available metabolomic comparison showed features of Warburg metabolism in both cell lines but different nicotinamide and redox profiles: SiHa cells accumulated NAD, NADH, and several glutathione pathway metabolites, whereas HeLa cells showed increased nicotinamide riboside and cysteine. Because the two lines are not isogenic, these differences cannot be assigned to HPV genotype alone. Several cervical cancer findings do reach a higher standard of evidence. Under the review’s classification, two reported relationships meet the Tier 1A criteria, which require a functionally validated O-GlcNAcylation site in a cervical cancer model. Site-directed mutation of NF-κB p65 at Thr322 or Thr352 in HeLa cells reduced CXCR4 expression, and stable OGT depletion reduced lung metastasis in a mouse model. In CaSki cells, which are HPV16-positive, OGT overexpression and depletion, STAT1 knockout, and mutation of Ser727 supported a role for tumor-cell STAT1 O-GlcNAcylation in macrophage polarization and phagocytosis in coculture. However, the study did not examine O-GlcNAcylation within macrophages themselves, and independent replication, in vivo validation, and confirmation in patient-derived samples remain necessary. Other cervical cancer work, including studies linking OGT to E6/E7 promoter activity through host cell factor 1, to epithelial-mesenchymal transition, to PTEN stability through GFPT1, and to cisplatin sensitivity, provides functional evidence without a validated causal modification site and is classified as Tier 1B. The mitochondrial question sits at the heart of the review. Cervical cancer models show variable mitochondrial oxidative activity rather than a consistent reduction in oxidative phosphorylation. In HeLa and SiHa cells and cervical tumor tissues, formation of a mitochondrial complex containing CKS2 and SSBP1 was associated with an OXPHOS-related transcriptional program and chemoradioresistance. Mitochondria therefore remain active contributors to ATP production, ROS signaling, biosynthetic precursor supply, apoptosis control, and stress responses in these tumors. Among all studies included in the review, only one directly manipulated intramitochondrial O-GlcNAcylation: an acute 30-minute intervention in isolated cardiac mitochondria reported changes in Complex I activity, respiratory capacity, and ROS release. Without independent replication or validation in cervical cancer, the authors treat this as preliminary evidence from another model rather than an established mechanism. Candidate mitochondrial substrates identified in other systems illustrate the evidence gap. Drp1, a key mediator of mitochondrial fission, has been shown to be O-GlcNAcylated in non-cervical models, where the modification is associated with mitochondrial recruitment and increased fission, but direct Drp1 O-GlcNAcylation and its functional consequences have not been established in HPV-positive cervical cancer. OPA1, essential for inner-membrane fusion and cristae stability, has been associated with mitochondrial phenotypes involving O-GlcNAcylation in other systems, but current evidence is insufficient to establish it as a direct O-GlcNAcylation substrate. In non-small cell lung cancer, O-GlcNAcylation of HK2 regulated HK2 activity, mitochondrial dynamics, proliferation, and apoptosis, and O-GlcNAcylation of PFK1 altered glucose partitioning with potential effects on antioxidant capacity through the pentose phosphate pathway. These findings identify testable indirect routes by which O-GlcNAcylation could influence mitochondrial redox balance through glucose partitioning without directly modifying mitochondrial proteins, but their relevance to HPV-positive cervical cancer remains untested. Temporal context matters for interpreting treatment-related findings. In HeLa cells, DNA damage acutely recruited OGT to damage sites, and stress-induced increases in O-GlcNAcylation can be transient in other experimental systems. Stable OGT depletion increased cisplatin sensitivity after 24 hours in HeLa cells, and metformin treatment for 24 hours was associated with reduced AMPK O-GlcNAcylation, cell cycle arrest, and apoptosis. Because OGT depletion had been established before cisplatin treatment, the experiment did not test whether cisplatin induced a protective O-GlcNAcylation response. Neither study used a validated resistant model, so the results are described as responses at 24 hours rather than evidence of acquired resistance. The review specifies that acquired resistance in cell models requires repeated drug selection from a sensitive parental line, confirmation that the selected cells are less sensitive than the parental line, and testing of whether resistance persists after drug withdrawal. The authors conclude that current evidence supports individual components of a proposed framework linking E6/E7-associated metabolic demand, hexosamine pathway activity, and substrate-specific O-GlcNAcylation to mitochondrial and immune adaptation, but not the complete causal sequence in HPV-positive cervical cancer. Direct cervical cancer evidence is strongest for selected non-mitochondrial substrates and signaling effects. The principal gap is the absence of site-specific validation of candidate mitochondrial proteins in HPV-positive cervical cancer, together with direct measurements of respiration, ROS, membrane potential, and mitochondrial dynamics. Translational applications, including biomarkers based on O-GlcNAcylation in tissue, blood, cervicovaginal fluid, or extracellular vesicles, and selective combination strategies targeting defined substrates, remain early preclinical or conceptual. Any future intervention must also be evaluated in normal cervical epithelial and immune cells to establish tumor selectivity and exclude impairment of antitumor immunity. O-GlcNAcylation, the review concludes, is best positioned as a context-dependent regulatory layer within a broader network of post-translational modifications rather than as the sole initiator of metabolic reprogramming. 南非旨在保护女孩免受 宫颈癌 感染的 HPV 疫苗接种 活动正在发挥作用 - Daily Maverick

South Africa’s HPV vaccination drive to protect girls from cervical cancer is working - Daily Maverick
新闻简报南非于 2014 年推出的 HPV 疫苗接种 计划在减少青春期女孩感染方面取得了显着成功。HOPE 研究于 2019 年和 2023 年进行,发现接种疫苗的人群中 HPV 16 型和 18 型感染率下降了 83%,艾滋病毒阳性女孩的感染率下降了 82%。这些减少表明该计划可以有效预防该国的一个主要公共卫生问题 宫颈癌。该研究还注意到群体保护,由于 疫苗接种 的广泛传播,未接种疫苗的女孩的感染率较低。尽管取得了这些积极成果,但持续监测对于确保长期利益至关重要,特别是对于艾滋病毒感染者而言。研究结果为面临类似挑战的其他非洲国家提供了榜样。
查看新闻五要素
时间
HPV 疫苗接种项目于2014年推出,该研究于2019年和2023年进行。研究结果于2026年11月报告。
地点与对象
南非,特别是在 17-18 岁的少女中,大约四分之一的参与者感染了艾滋病毒。
事件
南非于 2014 年实施了一项以学校为基础的 HPV 疫苗接种 计划,目标对象是 9 岁及以上的 4 年级女孩。通过 HOPE 研究评估了该计划的影响,结果显示 HPV 感染显着减少。
规模、影响与风险
在接种疫苗和符合条件的人群中,16 型和 18 型 HPV 感染下降了 83%。在 HIV 阳性组中,减少了 82%。这些发现表明对降低 宫颈癌 风险具有重大影响。
应对、状态与不确定性
该计划的成功正在受到监测,并且需要持续评估以确保长期保护。该研究提供了群体保护和交叉保护的证据,但需要进一步的数据来证实癌症发病率的降低。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

South Africa’s HPV vaccination drive to protect girls from cervical cancer is working - Daily Maverick

News Brief
South Africa’s HPV vaccination programme, introduced in 2014, has shown significant success in reducing infections among adolescent girls. The HOPE study, conducted in 2019 and 2023, found that HPV types 16 and 18 infections fell by 83% in vaccinated groups and 82% in HIV-positive girls. These reductions suggest the programme is effective in preventing cervical cancer, a major public health issue in the country. The study also noted herd protection, where unvaccinated girls had lower infection rates due to widespread vaccination. Despite these positive results, continued monitoring is essential to ensure long-term benefits, especially for those living with HIV. The findings offer a model for other African nations facing similar challenges.
Original source text (English)
South Africa introduced a vaccination for human papillomavirus (HPV) in public schools in 2014. The programme offered the vaccination to grade 4 girls aged nine years and older. Uptake was high from the start. About 87% of eligible girls received at least one dose during the first year. By Dorothy Nyemba-Machemedze and Sinead Delany-Moretlwe 2 Nov 2026 0 Human Papillomavirus (HPV) 3D renderings of the human papillomavirus (HPV) capsid (red) against a background image that is an inverted transmission electron micrograph of HPV virus particles harvested and purified from cell culture supernatant. HPV infection can be asymptomatic, cause warts, or disrupt cell growth. 3D rendering by NIH 3D (3d.nih.gov/); micrograph captured at the NIAID Integrated Research Facility (IRF) in Fort Detrick, Maryland. Note: not to scale. Credit: NIAID Almost all cervical cancers are caused by persistent infection with HPV, a very common virus transmitted through sexual contact. Most HPV infections clear naturally, but some high-risk types can persist and can eventually lead to cervical cancer. HPV types 16 and 18 alone are responsible for around 70% of cervical cancer cases worldwide. Cervical cancer is one of the most preventable forms of cancer, yet it remains a major cause of illness and death among women worldwide. Each year, more than 660,000 women are diagnosed globally , and over 90% of new cases and deaths caused by cervical cancer happen in low- and middle-income countries. The challenge is particularly acute in South Africa because HIV increases the risk of HPV infection persisting and progressing to cervical disease. Women living with HIV are much more likely to develop cervical cancer during their lifetime than women without HIV. South Africa had an estimated 7.9 million people living with HIV in mid-2025, accounting for 12.3% of the total population. advertisement Don’t want to see this? Remove ads We are public health researchers dedicated to advancing prevention and treatment of sexually transmitted infections. We evaluated the 2014 vaccination programme’s impact on HPV prevalence among adolescent girls in a setting of high HIV prevalence in South Africa. Our findings were striking. They showed that: infections with HPV types 16 and 18 fell by 83% between pre-vaccine and post-vaccine groups infections with the HPV types responsible for most cervical cancers have fallen by more than 80% among adolescent girls eligible for vaccination, including those living with HIV. This is encouraging news for a country that carries one of the world’s highest burdens of cervical cancer. It also provides important evidence for other African countries with high HIV prevalence that are working towards eliminating cervical cancer as a public health problem. Measuring the impact Because cervical cancer can take decades to develop after HPV infection, it is still too early to measure the programme’s success by looking at cancer rates alone. Instead, our research examined whether vaccine-targeted HPV infections are becoming less common among vaccine-programme eligible generations. A substantial reduction in these infections is a strong early sign that future cervical cancer rates will fall. To assess the programme’s impact, we conducted the HPV One and Two Dose Population Effectiveness (HOPE) study in 2019 and 2023. We compared how common HPV was among adolescent girls aged 17-18 years who were too old to receive the HPV vaccine when the national programme began, with girls of the same age who had been eligible for vaccination through the school-based programme. The study included 2,357 participants recruited from public healthcare facilities across four South African provinces. Importantly, about one-quarter of participants were living with HIV. This allowed us to examine vaccine impact in a population that faces a higher risk of cervical cancer. Our findings Across all participants, infections with HPV types 16 and 18 fell by 83% between pre-vaccine and post-vaccine groups. Before the vaccination programme, about one in five girls carried one of these high-risk HPV types. Among girls eligible for vaccination, that number fell to approximately three in every 100 girls. The programme performed equally well among girls living with HIV. In this group, HPV 16/18 infections fell from 29% to 4%, representing an 82% reduction. We also observed reductions in high-risk HPV types that are genetically related to HPV 16 and 18, a phenomenon known as cross-protection. This increased the overall impact of the vaccination programme. At the same time, other HPV types did not fall. This strengthens confidence that the observed reductions were due to vaccination, not other factors such as changes in sexual behaviour. Another important finding was evidence of indirect protection also known as “herd protection”. Girls in the post-vaccine era who had no documented record of being vaccinated, or did not report having been vaccinated, had much lower rates of HPV 16 and 18 than girls in the pre-vaccine group. This suggests that when most people are vaccinated, the virus spreads much less in the wider community, helping protect unvaccinated individuals. What this means for South Africa and the region The findings provide compelling evidence that South Africa’s HPV vaccination programme is achieving its primary goal: preventing infections that would otherwise place young women at risk of developing cervical cancer later in life. They also demonstrate that the benefits extend to adolescent girls living with HIV, a group that stands to gain substantially from effective cervical cancer prevention. This is especially important for countries in eastern and southern Africa, where both HIV and cervical cancer remain major public health challenges. In 2022, the World Health Organisation recommended a single-dose HPV vaccine for the general population, with the aim of simplifying delivery and increasing global coverage for settings that need it most. Since October 2024, South Africa has adopted a single-dose HPV vaccination approach. Continued monitoring will be important to ensure long-term protection, particularly among people living with HIV. A decade after its introduction, the country’s school-based HPV vaccination programme is delivering measurable population-level benefits and providing a model for other countries seeking to prevent one of the world’s most avoidable cancers. Time
The HPV vaccination programme was introduced in 2014, and the study was conducted in 2019 and 2023. The findings were reported in November 2026.
Location and population
South Africa, specifically among adolescent girls aged 17-18 years, with about one-quarter of participants living with HIV.
Event
A school-based HPV vaccination programme was implemented in South Africa in 2014, targeting grade 4 girls aged nine years and older. The programme's impact was evaluated through the HOPE study, showing significant reductions in HPV infections.
Scale, impact and risk
HPV infections with types 16 and 18 fell by 83% among vaccinated and eligible groups. In the HIV-positive group, the reduction was 82%. These findings indicate a substantial impact on reducing the risk of cervical cancer.

The programme's success is being monitored, and continued evaluation is necessary to ensure long-term protection. The study provides evidence of herd protection and cross-protection, but further data is needed to confirm cancer rate reductions.

仅在深入研究后才继续使用 HPV 疫苗接种 对抗 宫颈癌:贾坎德邦 CM

Proceed with HPV vaccination against cervical cancer only after in-depth study: Jharkhand CM
新闻简报Jharkhand Chief Minister Hemant Soren has called for a comprehensive study before implementing HPV vaccination against cervical cancer in the state, despite the National HPV Vaccination Campaign launched by Prime Minister Narendra Modi in February 2026. The campaign has achieved high coverage in several states, including 100% in Gujarat, Uttar Pradesh, Madhya Pradesh, and Mizoram. Soren emphasized the importance of strengthening the regular screening system for cervical cancer, noting that the estimated incidence in Jharkhand is 13 cases per 100,000 population. He directed health officials to conduct a state-wide survey and form a committee of experts to consult with medical institutions and specialists, ensuring detailed data on affected individuals, age groups, and disease stages. This decision reflects a cautious approach to public health policy, prioritizing thorough research before widespread implementation.
查看新闻五要素
时间
贾坎德邦首席部长赫曼特·索伦 (Hemant Soren) 于 2026 年 11 月 5 日在与卫生官员会面时发表了上述声明。全国 HPV 疫苗接种 运动由纳伦德拉·莫迪总理于 2026 年 2 月发起。
地点与对象
该声明是在印度贾坎德邦发表的。作为全国 HPV 疫苗接种 运动的一部分,目标人群包括青春期女孩,特别是 14 岁的女孩。
事件
贾坎德邦首席部长 Hemant Soren 强调,在该邦针对 宫颈癌 实施 HPV 疫苗接种 之前需要进行全面研究。他还强调了加强宫颈癌定期筛查制度的重要性。
规模、影响与风险
The National HPV Vaccination Campaign has achieved 100% coverage in Gujarat, Uttar Pradesh, Madhya Pradesh, and Mizoram, and over 90% coverage in Bihar, Andhra Pradesh, and Assam. The estimated incidence of cervical cancer in Jharkhand is 13 cases per 100,000 population.
应对、状态与不确定性
贾坎德邦政府已指示卫生官员在全州范围内进行调查,并成立一个专家委员会,与医疗机构和专家进行磋商。研究结果必须包括受影响个人、年龄组、疾病阶段和活跃病例的详细数据。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Proceed with HPV vaccination against cervical cancer only after in-depth study: Jharkhand CM

News Brief
Jharkhand Chief Minister Hemant Soren has called for a comprehensive study before implementing HPV vaccination against cervical cancer in the state, despite the National HPV Vaccination Campaign launched by Prime Minister Narendra Modi in February 2026. The campaign has achieved high coverage in several states, including 100% in Gujarat, Uttar Pradesh, Madhya Pradesh, and Mizoram. Soren emphasized the importance of strengthening the regular screening system for cervical cancer, noting that the estimated incidence in Jharkhand is 13 cases per 100,000 population. He directed health officials to conduct a state-wide survey and form a committee of experts to consult with medical institutions and specialists, ensuring detailed data on affected individuals, age groups, and disease stages. This decision reflects a cautious approach to public health policy, prioritizing thorough research before widespread implementation.
Original source text (English)

India Proceed with HPV vaccination against cervical cancer only after in-depth study: Jharkhand CM PTI 05 November, 2026 09:45 am IST Follow Us: Facebook X LinkedIn WhatsApp Email Copy Link Stay updated with ThePrint: Preferred Source Google Discover Ranchi, Nov 5 (PTI) Jharkhand Chief Minister Hemant Soren said a comprehensive study was needed before implementing the Human Papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination against cervical cancer in the state, while asserting that protecting adolescent girls from the disease was his government’s priority. Prime Minister Narendra Modi launched the National HPV Vaccination Campaign in February, under which 14-year-old girls receive voluntary, free-of-cost, single-dose HPV vaccination with parental consent at government health facilities. Gujarat, Uttar Pradesh, Madhya Pradesh and Mizoram have achieved 100-per cent coverage of their identified target populations under the campaign, while Bihar, Andhra Pradesh and Assam have crossed 90-per cent coverage. Show Full Article In Jharkhand, where the estimated incidence of cervical cancer was 13 cases per 100,000 population, Soren said, “Cervical cancer poses a serious health challenge, and the government will take necessary measures for its prevention and control with the utmost seriousness.” During a meeting with health officials on Wednesday, he said protecting adolescent girls from cervical cancer is a priority for his government, but a decision would be taken only after an in-depth study on HPV vaccination. “Conduct a comprehensive and in-depth study before proceeding with the implementation of HPV vaccination,” the chief minister told the officials and directed them to carry out a state-wide survey. The report prepared after the survey must have detailed data, including the actual number of individuals affected by cervical cancer, their age groups, the various stages of the disease, and the number of active cases, he said. Soren directed health department officials to constitute a committee of experts on the subject, who would hold consultations with directors of reputed medical institutions as well as with cancer specialists and other experts in the relevant field. Emphasising strengthening the regular screening system for timely detection and prevention of cervical cancer, the chief minister said that necessary steps must be taken to improve the availability and accessibility of essential diagnostic facilities at district hospitals and community health centres. 宫颈癌 预防需要的不仅仅是HPV 疫苗接种,每两年筛查一次很重要 - 헬스조선

News article (Korean original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报Health Chosun 于 2026 年 11 月 4 日发表的这篇文章强调了除了 HPV 疫苗接种 之外,每两年定期进行 宫颈癌 筛查的重要性。这解释了宫颈癌通常是默默发展的,在从HPV感染到癌症的15至20年的发展过程中很少出现症状。韩国国家癌症筛查计划为 20 岁及以上的女性提供宫颈细胞学检查,但筛查率仍然很低,根据 2024 年的一项研究,只有 18.9% 的女性接受定期筛查。2024年总体筛查率为62%,但20多岁女性的筛查率下降至40.8%。专家强调,即使没有症状,定期筛查对于早期发现和治疗也至关重要,并且对于 高危型HPV 感染者来说,后续护理也是必要的。文章还指出,妇科检查期间缺乏时间和不适等因素导致筛查率低,而通过性接触与 HPV 传播 相关的耻辱可能会进一步阻止女性寻求治疗。

News article (Korean original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Korean)
Cervical Cancer Prevention Requires More Than HPV Vaccination, Screening Every Two Years Is Essential - 헬스조선
News Brief
The article, published on November 4, 2026, by Health Chosun, highlights the importance of regular cervical cancer screening every two years in addition to HPV vaccination. It explains that cervical cancer often develops silently, with symptoms rarely appearing during the 15 to 20-year progression from HPV infection to cancer. The National Cancer Screening Program in South Korea provides cervical cytology tests for women aged 20 and older, but screening rates remain low, with only 18.9% of women undergoing regular screening according to a 2024 study. The overall screening rate in 2024 was 62%, but it dropped to 40.8% among women in their 20s. Experts emphasize that regular screening is essential for early detection and management, even in the absence of symptoms, and that follow-up care is necessary for those with high-risk HPV infections. The article also notes that factors such as lack of time and discomfort during gynecological exams contribute to low screening rates, and that stigma related to HPV transmission through sexual contact may further deter women from seeking care.
Original source text (Korean)
News Issues Wellness Beauty Pharma KOREAN SEARCH Search Health Chosun Cervical Cancer Prevention Requires More Than HPV Vaccination, Screening Every Two Years Is Essential by Jun Jong Bo Updated 2026/11/04 17:25 Photo = Clipart Korea Cancer often develops silently. Although early detection is paramount, treatment is frequently delayed because there are no outward symptoms. The same is true of cervical cancer, a major gynecologic cancer. When persistent high-risk human papillomavirus, HPV, infection causes precancerous lesions, cellular abnormalities can develop, and it may take 15 to 20 years for the disease to progress to cancer. The problem is that symptoms warranting suspicion of cancer rarely appear during this period. This is one of the reasons cervical cancer is often detected late. The best way to detect cervical cancer during the precancer stage is to undergo regular screening. Under the National Cancer Screening Program, cervical cytology tests are provided every two years for women aged 20 and older. While cervical cytology tests examine whether cellular abnormalities are present, HPV DNA tests directly determine whether a person is infected with high-risk HPV that can cause cervical cancer. However, a positive test result does not immediately mean cancer. A secondary screening process is used to identify those who actually belong to the high-risk group among people who test positive, and to determine whether a detailed examination is needed or whether retesting after a certain period is sufficient. Although the importance of screening for early detection continues to grow, the actual screening rate remains low. According to a study by a team led by Professors Kim Jong Yeop and Hong Ji Young of Konyang University, published in the international journal Journal of Gynecologic Oncology in 2024, the rate of regular cervical cancer screening among women in South Korea was only 18.9%. The study followed and analyzed 4663 women for up to 12 years. According to a report by the National Cancer Center, the overall cervical cancer screening rate among women in 2024 was 62%, but the rate among women in their 20s was 40.8%. Delaying screening is not due solely to a lack of interest in the disease. A study analyzing the 2022 Survey on Sexual and Reproductive Health by Life Stage Among Korean Women identified lack of time and the discomfort of gynecologic examinations as major reasons women did not receive gynecologic care. A domestic study published in the Journal of Korean Academy of Nursing also found that women may feel stigma and shame because the virus is transmitted through sexual contact. Experts emphasize that women should undergo regular screening even in the absence of symptoms, since cervical cancer can be detected and managed before it progresses. They explain that it is important not only to check for infection during screening, but also to identify those at high risk of developing a persistent infection and connect them with follow-up care. Professor Lee Jae Kwan of the Department of Obstetrics and Gynecology at Korea University Guro Hospital said, “Cervical cancer is preventable, but vaccination alone is not enough. The success of prevention depends on regular screening and early detection, follow-up evaluation, and continued treatment.” This article has been translated AI. by Jun Jong Bo View All Articles Copyright © Health Chosun. No unauthorized reproduction or redistribution. Scale, impact and risk

The National Cancer Center reported an overall screening rate of 62% in 2024, but this drops to 40.8% among women in their 20s. The article notes that delayed screening contributes to late detection of cervical cancer.

Karonga 利用 HPV 疫苗接种 扩大 宫颈癌 预防 - AfricaBrief

Karonga Expands Cervical Cancer Prevention With HPV Vaccination - AfricaBrief
新闻简报在马拉维卡龙加区,随着 HPV 疫苗接种 和现有筛查服务的引入,减少 宫颈癌 死亡人数的努力有所扩大。该倡议由比利时医科学生 Anais Hublet 与当地医疗服务提供者合作领导,针对 9 岁女孩进行 疫苗接种,以降低她们日后患 宫颈癌 的风险,并包括对女性进行筛查,以实现早期发现和 治疗。卡龙加地区医院 (Karonga District Hospital) 的护士兼助产士 Shida Gomeka 强调了 HPV 疫苗接种 在预防与大多数 宫颈癌 病例相关的感染方面的重要性,并指出筛查有助于识别患有 HPV 的女性或该疾病的早期迹象。该计划的目标是全年运作,即使在 Hublet 缺席的情况下,因为当地培训的护士有能力继续提供服务。虽然没有报告具体病例或死亡人数,但该倡议旨在使 宫颈癌 预防成为男性和女性的共同责任。该计划目前正在进行中,被认为是一项预防措施,而不是 暴发。

时间
该举措在卡龙加区实施,参与筛查计划的女性人数从三年前第一年的 92 名女性增加到去年的 800 名 (N9)。
地点与对象
马拉维卡龙加区针对 9 岁女孩进行 HPV 疫苗接种 筛查,针对女性进行 宫颈癌 筛查。
事件
据比利时医学生 Anais Hublet 和当地护士兼助产士 Shida Gomeka(N3、N12)报道,卡龙加区通过在现有筛查服务的同时引入 HPV 疫苗接种 来扩大 宫颈癌 预防工作。
规模、影响与风险
该计划旨在通过 疫苗接种 和早期发现来减少患 宫颈癌 的女性数量,所提供的证据中没有报告具体病例或死亡人数(N7、N15)。
应对、状态与不确定性
该计划正在进行中,计划使用当地培训的护士使该计划全年运作。该计划被描述为一项预防工作,而不是 暴发(N10、N16)。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Karonga Expands Cervical Cancer Prevention With HPV Vaccination - AfricaBrief

News Brief
In Karonga District, Malawi, efforts to reduce cervical cancer deaths have expanded with the introduction of HPV vaccination alongside existing screening services. The initiative, led by Belgian medical student Anais Hublet in collaboration with local health providers, targets 9-year-old girls for vaccination to lower their risk of developing cervical cancer later in life and includes screening for women to enable early detection and treatment. Shida Gomeka, a nurse-midwife at Karonga District Hospital, emphasized the importance of HPV vaccination in preventing infections linked to most cervical cancer cases and noted that screening helps identify women with HPV or early signs of the disease. The programme aims to operate year-round, even in Hublet's absence, as locally trained nurses are capable of continuing the services. While no specific case or death counts are reported, the initiative seeks to make cervical cancer prevention a shared responsibility involving both men and women. The programme is currently in progress and is considered a preventive measure rather than an outbreak.
Original source text (English)
Vaccination in Progress. Picture by Naomi Tadala Magawa. Mana KARONGA, Malawi — Efforts to reduce cervical cancer deaths in Karonga District have gained momentum with the introduction of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination alongside existing screening services, writes Naomi Tadala Magawa, MANA. The initiative, implemented by Belgian fifth-year medical student Anais Hublet in collaboration with Lusubilo Community Care and the Karonga Diocese, aims to prevent HPV infections and promote early detection of cervical cancer. Hublet said the introduction of vaccination marked an important step in strengthening the programme, which initially focused on screening women. The initiative targets two areas: vaccinating 9-year-old girls against HPV to reduce their risk of developing cervical cancer later in life, and screening women to support early detection and treatment. “The major purpose is to decrease the number of women dying from cervical cancer. We are approaching prevention in two ways which are vaccination which gives young girls a very low chance of developing the cancer later in life and screening, which helps detect the disease early,” she said. Hublet said the screening programme had recorded an increase in participation, from 92 women in its first year, three years ago, to 800 last year. She said the long-term goal was to make the programme operate throughout the year, even in her absence, as locally trained nurses had the skills to continue providing the services. “We are hoping that it would go on the whole year, even if I am not here, because the nurses are from Malawi and the materials are from Malawi, so it can work without me,” she said. A nurse-midwife at Karonga District Hospital, Shida Gomeka, said HPV vaccination was important in preventing infections associated with most cervical cancer cases. “The HPV vaccine will help a lot because it reduces the number of women who can have HPV on their cervix,” she said. Gomeka said screening was also essential because HPV infections often develop without noticeable symptoms, making it difficult for women to know their status without testing. She said screening helps health workers identify women who test negative for HPV, those who test positive and those who may already show signs of cervical cancer, enabling them to receive appropriate care. Gomeka urged men to support prevention efforts by encouraging their wives, mothers, sisters and children to access screening and vaccination services. She said preventing cervical cancer should be a shared responsibility involving both men and women
View five news elements
Time
The initiative was implemented in Karonga District, with participation in the screening programme increasing from 92 women in its first year, three years ago, to 800 last year (N9).
Location and population
Karonga District, Malawi, targeting 9-year-old girls for HPV vaccination and women for cervical cancer screening.
Event
Karonga District expanded its cervical cancer prevention efforts by introducing HPV vaccination alongside existing screening services, as reported by Belgian medical student Anais Hublet and local nurse-midwife Shida Gomeka (N3, N12).
Scale, impact and risk
The programme aims to reduce the number of women developing cervical cancer through vaccination and early detection, with no specific case or death counts reported in the supplied evidence (N7, N15).
Response, status and uncertainty
The initiative is ongoing, with plans to make the programme operate throughout the year using locally trained nurses. The programme is described as a prevention effort, not an outbreak (N10, N16).

HPV 疫苗 治疗疣:初级保健应该了解什么 - Medscape

HPV Vaccine for Warts: What Primary Care Should Know - Medscape
新闻简报Medscape 的这篇文章讨论了 HPV 疫苗 用于治疗或预防皮肤疣(例如寻常疣)的潜在用途。然而,它澄清了导致疣的 HPV 类型与 9 价 疫苗 所针对的类型不同。支持这种用途的证据仅限于观察性研究和小型试验,并且 FDA 尚未批准该应用。文章强调,HPV 疫苗接种 应继续因其已批准的预防适应症而得到推荐,例如预防 HPV 相关疾病。它还强调在考虑将 疫苗 作为疣标准 治疗 之前需要进行更大规模的随机对照试验。建议临床医生区分预防性 疫苗接种 和疣 治疗 的标签外使用,因为后者仍在研究中。重点是确保 疫苗 正确用于其批准的用途,而不是用于治疗现有的疣。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

HPV Vaccine for Warts: What Primary Care Should Know - Medscape

News Brief
This article from Medscape discusses the potential use of the HPV vaccine for treating or preventing cutaneous warts, such as verruca vulgaris. However, it clarifies that the HPV types responsible for warts are different from those targeted by the 9-valent vaccine. The evidence supporting this use is limited to observational studies and small trials, and there is no FDA approval for this application. The article emphasizes that HPV vaccination should continue to be recommended for its approved preventive indications, such as preventing HPV-related diseases. It also highlights the need for larger randomized controlled trials before considering the vaccine as a standard treatment for warts. Clinicians are advised to differentiate between preventive vaccination and off-label use for wart treatment, as the latter remains investigational. The focus is on ensuring that the vaccine is used appropriately for its approved purposes rather than for treating existing warts.
